- **1914.04** `iw18-infancy.ceiling-shadows::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-shadow-bows` tags: infancy, night, wonder, gaslight, fear, family, vienna, urban
  煤气灯芯拧到最小，天花板上一件挂着的大衣被风推得晃了晃。Klara躺在摇篮里忽然笑出声，朝那片影子伸出两只手。Josef Bauer抬头看了很久，只看见衣钩和一块掉漆的墙皮。
  _effects: mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +laughed_at_ceiling_shadow_

- **1915.12** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Emil Weiss的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Klara坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Emil Weiss把桶一放，夹起Klara进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Klara正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Maria Kral家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Klara递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Maria Kral端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Klara把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1918.11** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Klara的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Mitzi Lechner不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Klara把弹珠举到眼前看，Mitzi Lechner在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Klara后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Klara没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Klara用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Klara拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Anna Bauer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Klara接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Klara把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Klara踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Klara单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/turned-away` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头翻完工票，问Klara上一个东家是谁。Klara说厂子关了，工头又问有没有辞工证明；没有证明，名册就不能写。后面的人挤上来，Klara被推到台阶下面。中午的钟响了，Klara站在街对面，看新来的人把名字一个个写上去。
  _effects: cash -2 (carfare and waiting); reputation -0.03; flag +no_work_references; role; mood -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-64_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Klara最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Klara把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Klara说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 21 -> 27; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 52 -> 49; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1930.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.introduce-two-people/let-them-talk` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  星期天下午窗边三把椅子，介绍只用了两句，随后就任两人自己聊。老友讲印厂的油墨味，缝纫女工讲机针怎么断。有人只管叫侍者续咖啡，三杯一共九十赫勒，坐在旁边听了一下午。
  _effects: cash -0.9 (三杯咖啡和给侍者的小费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; skill; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1930.03** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-too-damp` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Helene Bauer却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Klara把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-1_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1931.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/daughter-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Anna 2。接生的人收六克朗，Anton Huber 2在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Klara起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:219_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +avoided_assembly; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

  Klara和{link:beloved}把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Klara在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 45 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 27 -> 24; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef Bauer的咳嗽在夜里越来越重，Klara把炉子搬到床边，还是暖不透那间屋子。医生来看了两回，第二回只留下一张写着“静养”的纸。Klara把纸折好，压在药瓶下面，开始学着给老人擦身、换床单。
  _effects: cash -7 (给床上的老人翻身); health -0.030000000000000027 (给床上的老人翻身); flag +parent_bedridden; health -0.07999999999999996 (the illness worsened despite home care); condition_

  肉商的账本上少了二十公斤马肉，Alois Weiss要Klara签字认账。Klara知道是夜班门房把肉转卖给了普拉特的香肠摊，但门房是Alois Weiss的小舅子。Klara不签，第二天被调去扫兽栏；臭味洗了三遍还在指甲缝里。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; cash -1 (lost the night-shift supplement); mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:220; flag +zoo_night_shift; flag +refused_meat_ledger; knowledge “少掉的二十公斤马肉被夜班门房卖给了香肠摊，门房是园长的亲戚。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1931.09** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.buy-coal` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Klara眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Otto Kral隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); health -0.010000000000000009 (生火时被烟呛到); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1932.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.servants-room` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Ferdinand Weiss的东家说得很清楚：结婚就得搬出佣人房，工钱也要减。那间房在厨房后面，冬天靠炉灶取暖。Ferdinand Weiss算了一夜，第二天把Klara送的围巾叠好送回来。两人在面包房门口站了一会儿，各自去上工；围巾上还留着烤炉的暖气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  Klara去了Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus，坐在后排听完了关于维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济的争论；散会时带走一张油印提纲。
  _effects: historical development:seed-vienna-social-democracy-1912 as participant; knowledge “我参加了维也纳社会民主党在工人街区、工会和报刊网络中持续活动，争取普选、劳动保护与社会救济。。”; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `ld18-raid.private-club::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/names-in-a-notebook` tags: queer, raid, police, public, scandal, nightlife, secret_intimacy, vienna
  警察在午夜后闯进地下舞厅，把灯全部打开。Anna Bauer让所有人靠墙站好，逐个记下名字和住址；Klara看见Elisabeth Bauer的鞋跟断了一只。第二天，名单上的一些名字出现在报纸的“风化事件”栏里。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:344; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 33 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 43 -> 28; reputation -0.1; cash -2 (a fine and a broken shoe); flag +name_in_police_notebook; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1935.01** `state_reaction:ld18-public-desire/a-moral-society-letter` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  道德协会寄来一封措辞客气的信，请Klara去“谈谈自己的生活方式”。她把信读完，折好，垫在桌脚下；桌子还是晃。第二天，她照常去上班，只是把头发剪短了一截，短到所有人都能看出她不在乎。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 0 -> 0; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.defiance 20 -> 28; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006019_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/someone-asks-for-the-papers` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Klara说{link:beloved}是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 53 -> 65; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 28 -> 20; reputation -0.03; mood -0.019999999999999907_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 37 -> 29; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 55_

- **1935.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Klara原定在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: reputation -0.009999999999999995_

- **1935.03** `r3-war.defeat-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::defeat-rumor-crisis/witnessed-records-evacuated` tags: war, rumor, defeat, bread, fear, vienna, urban, crisis
  第二天，面包房开门时，队伍排得比平时长。Mitzi说昨晚听见火车站在烧文件，Klara说那是暖气；话没说完，一辆卡车拉着档案柜从街口拐过去。Alois Weiss站在门口，手里的钥匙串一直响。
  _effects: food; mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +witnessed_records_being_evacuated; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; knowledge “the government is moving its files”_

- **1935.03** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family, injury
  Klara和Berta Winter 2一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Klara去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Klara先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱); health -0.020000000000000018 (在病人屋里待久了，嗓子发干); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1935.11** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Klara用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Anna Bauer翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Josef Huber在走廊里问交了多少，Klara含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.01999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Klara把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Emil Winter 3没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Klara回头看了一眼，Emil Winter 3正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1936.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-wrong-signature` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸放大的签名其实是Alois Weiss的：两个字母写得太像。更正启事登在第七版，只有三行；Klara的名字被拿掉了，Alois Weiss的名字留了下来。第二天，Alois Weiss没有来上班，桌上的茶杯还是满的。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +scandal_misdirected; knowledge “被登出来的签名是书记员的，更正启事在第七版。”_

  办公室有人问Klara，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Klara说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Klara把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 65 -> 73; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 20 -> 16; reputation -0.020000000000000004_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006535_

  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000001_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006535_

  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Klara最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Klara把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Klara说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 73 -> 79; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 16 -> 13_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Klara，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Klara没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 58; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 40 -> 44; reputation -0.03_

  介绍完就找了个由头先走，说家里炉子还烧着，账只结了自己的那一杯。晚上回到楼里，缝纫女工在楼梯上碰见，只说那人挺有意思，没再多讲。老友第二天在印厂什么也没提，这事就搁在那里。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (自己那一杯咖啡); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

- **1936.07** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Klara把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Maria Moser站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Klara手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1936.09** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara数出八赫勒交给Mitzi Bauer，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Mitzi Bauer说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Klara盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; flag +funeral_society_member_

  Maria Kral把一份任命书推到Klara面前。职位不大，但有印章、有预算、有能签字的地方。Klara签下名字时，第一次觉得那件一直想着的事有了一个可以摸到的边。

- **1937.02** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sell-the-patent-outright` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  破产拍卖会上，Klara用四克朗买下一张没人举牌的瓶盖专利，只因为图纸画得好看。三个月后，市民礼仪处的矿泉水厂找上门：那圈软木垫正好能解决他们的漏气问题。Friedrich Hofer核对完专利号，市民礼仪处当场开出八十克朗的支票。
  _effects: cash 80 (sale of the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:416; reputation 0.06; flag +sold_patent; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  到了警局，失窃清单上忽然多出一只银怀表，Ferdinand Huber说是在Klara的篮子底下找到的。Klara说那篮子从早上就没离手，Mitzi Lechner低头擦柜台，Alois Winter被叫去另一间屋子。值班的人把“盗窃”改成“加重盗窃”，让她按手印。
  _effects: cash -6 (后巷里数面包的手); condition; flag +overcharged_after_petty_theft; role; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “the silver watch was added to the list after the arrest”_

- **1939.01** `r15-fam.school-registration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/enrolls` tags: family, child, school, education, school_child, vienna, urban, crisis
  Klara带着Anna 2去学校登记。孩子的名字被写在名册最后一行，墨水还没有干；回家的路上，Anna 2一直捏着那张课程表，Klara则在算这个月还能剩下多少煤钱。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 35 -> 45; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 40 -> 20; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 65 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> true; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> false; cash -1 (the first school books and a slate); mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:known-affair/public-year/forgotten` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Klara的名字从闲谈里慢慢退了下去。街上有了新的婚礼、新的葬礼和新的丑闻；只有裁缝还记得Klara改过两次同一件外套的腰身。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 30 -> 25; state_parameter known-affair.grief 0 -> 0; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `agency:recruit_members` tags: agency, agency:recruit_members
  Klara找到Mitzi Kraus，把权力俱乐部的章程和会费说清楚；对方留下地址，答应下次再来。
  _effects: organization_membership; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000839:child; knowledge “我通过Klara加入了权力俱乐部。”_

- **1939.04** `r13-rel.proposal-accepted::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::proposal-accepted-war/wedding-before-departure` tags: relationship, courtship, engagement, family, marriage, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Huber说把婚期提前，哪怕只办一桌。Klara家凑了面粉和糖，婚礼在出征前一夜举行。第二天清早，Gustav Huber穿着军装出门，Klara把剩下的喜糖分给了整栋楼的孩子。
  _effects: cash -8 (订婚宴上的两副碗筷); role; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001159:child; household; role; role; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001159:child_

- **1939.06** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Klara跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Berta Winter 3探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1940.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, injury
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Gustav Huber在楼梯间坐了很久；Klara在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Klara的名字；此后邻居提起Klara，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: health -0.12 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial); role_

- **1940.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/reappointed` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  计票拖到凌晨，Klara以两票之差留任。Maria Kral把已经写好的交接清单折起来，笑着说下次再来。Klara回到办公室，发现桌上的文件被人重新排过；最上面那份，是新一年的预算草案。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; mood 0.06000000000000005; organization_funds; state_add office-patronage state:world-vienna-1912:533; flag +reappointed; knowledge “桌上的文件被人重新排过，最上面是新一年的预算草案。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +office_patronage_network_

- **1940.01** `state_tick:known-affair/public-year/blackmail` tags: known-affair, love, scandal, secret, public, secret_intimacy, state, state_tick
  Klara收到一封没有署名的信，里面是那家旅馆的登记页。信末写着一个数目和一句“月底之前”；Klara把钱数了两遍，还是差一点。
  _effects: state_parameter known-affair.notoriety 25 -> 40; state_parameter known-affair.grief 0 -> 8; cash -2 (the first payment to a blackmailer)_

- **1940.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Klara在Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus召集了一次小范围会议，和Josef Winter把下一次行动、会费和分工写在纸上。
  _effects: project; historical development:initiative-org-sdap-1934 as organizer; historical_influence; knowledge “我召集了一次关于Sozialdemokratische Arbeiterpartei Öster的会议。”; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1940.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Ferdinand Berg这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 21; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009043_

- **1940.01** `initiative:found_process:org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:004612`
  权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先写好的历史，而是成员自己累积出来的运动。
  _effects: historical process 权力俱乐部的持续运动; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:004612-1940 as organizer; historical development:initiative-org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:004612-1940 as organizer; knowledge “权力俱乐部的持续运动不是一次会议，而是我们这些年积累出来的持续组织。”; reputation 0.040000000000000036_

- **1940.01** `historical_phase`
  历史进程《权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招募、会议和互助，把分散的行动整理成公开纲领；这不是预先》从 emerging 进入 mobilizing。
  _effects: knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”; knowledge “我亲历或知晓「权力俱乐部在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang经过数年的招”_

- **1940.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把通知念给Anton Bauer听，Anton Bauer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

- **1940.06** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Helene Stein 2在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Maria Kral没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室); reputation 0.06000000000000005; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:008750; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1941.01** `project_progressed` tags: project
  「Klara召集的会议」继续推进了一阶段。
  _effects: project_

- **1941.01** `r15-wish.power-price::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/protected-ally` tags: politics, power, wish, office, betrayal, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Huber被指控挪用公款。Klara用自己刚拿到的签字权压下了调查，条件是Ferdinand Huber从此在委员会里站自己这一边。Klara知道这不是干净的交易，也知道自己已经离不开它。
  _effects: reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:549_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Gustav Huber。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Gustav Huber把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Klara躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee)_

- **1941.01** `fm17-saint.first-cure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-child-is-worse` tags: faith, healing, medicine, saint, investigation, child, reputation, money
  Klara给孩子擦了额头，也念了女人带来的祷词，但孩子的烧没有退。女人终于去请了医生，医生在门口说，再晚半天就麻烦了。Alois Weiss把这一切记进本子，合上时没有看Klara；走廊里只剩下孩子咳嗽的声音。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (延误了请医生的时间); state_parameter under-investigation.progress 15 -> 30; reputation -0.050000000000000044; cash -2 (替孩子家垫付的诊费); mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +fm17_healing_failed_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Klara把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Klara来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  一个当年被安排进来的老职员来找Klara，要的不是升职，而是让他的侄子进档案室。Klara翻出编制表，发现空位只有一个，名字已经有人先写了。
  _effects: state_parameter office-patronage.favorDebt 25 -> 31; mood -0.020000000000000018; knowledge “编制表上的空位只剩一个，名字已经被人先写。”_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +ration_queue_closed; knowledge “the district quota can run out before the window closes”_

- **1942.03** `r3-mig.chain-migration::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/letter-with-job-order` tags: migration, family, chain, remittance, travel, vienna, urban, crisis
  Anton Huber 2从芝加哥寄来一封信，里面有一张屠宰场的招工单和一个地址。信纸背面写着“来的时候带一双厚鞋”。家里卖了一头牛，凑出船票钱。Klara把招工单缝进衣领，Luise Weiss 3说这种信不能给海关看。
  _effects: cash 6 (先走的那个人寄回了地址); mood 0.05999999999999994; flag +chain_migration_letter; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; knowledge “the cousin has a job order at a Chicago slaughterhouse”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1942.05** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/motion-carries` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  投票结果是十九比十七。Klara把印章、钥匙和一本写满批注的账本放进纸箱，最后一圈走廊走得比平时慢。Friedrich Hofer在门口等着接手，手里已经拿着新的编制表；表上第一个被划掉的名字是Klara的秘书。
  _effects: position_end 公共礼仪官 @org:absurd15-wish-office:evt:world-vienna-1912:005096; reputation -0.050000000000000044; mood -0.07000000000000006; condition; relation rival with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +removed_by_vote; knowledge “新编制表第一个划掉的是我的秘书。”_

- **1943.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Franz Huber。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Gustav Huber把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Klara躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:653_

- **1943.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-scandal-helps` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  照片登出后，Klara收到十七封邀请函，都是请去讲“如何面对舆论”。一个工商业协会送来一笔“研究资助”，请Klara去讲一次闭门课；课酬刚好补上办公室这个月的缺口。
  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:654; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1943.01** `r13-civ.midwife::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/doctor-called` tags: health, midwife, birth, neighbor, night, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Weiss摸了半天肚子，忽然让Klara去街口叫医生，说胎位不对。Klara披着外套跑过两条街，医生来了以后只说了句“送医院”，又收了一次出诊费。天亮前，马车才把Maria Bauer拉走。
  _effects: cash -5 (夜里敲接生婆的门（其中5克朗记入债务）); health -0.030000000000000027 (夜里敲接生婆的门); flag +complicated_birth_sent_to_hospital; relation colleague with person-berta-weiss; knowledge “the midwife suspected a malpresentation before dawn”_

- **1943.02** `major-family.child-death/child-death.measles` tags: family, child_death, grief, major, lifecycle, death, injury, partnership
  麻疹先从耳后起，后来全身都是红点。Klara把窗帘拉上，怕光刺着孩子的眼睛；Gustav Huber从药房带回一包退热粉，Franz Huber喝下去就吐了。药房的人说退热粉不能多用，Gustav Huber还是买了第二包。下葬那天下着雨，Klara把一只小鞋留在坟边，鞋底还沾着屋里的煤灰。
  _effects: death (measles and pneumonia); health -0.050000000000000044 (nursing a child through measles); mood -0.09000000000000008; relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001159:child; role; cash -6 (medicine, coffin and burial（其中5克朗记入债务）)_

  孩子的名字从学校的名册上被划掉以后，家里的碗还多摆了一副。Klara没有把它收起来，直到有一天发现碗沿积了灰，才拿去洗。

- **1943.05** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/health-inspectors` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, vienna
  两人摸黑撬开一扇门，里面没有保险柜，只有背景布、三脚架和一池显影水。店主以为他们是卫生局派来查暗房通风的，殷勤地领着看排水口；Emil Winter 3只好装模作样地记了几笔，Klara顺手把柜台上一顶帽子戴走。临走时，店主还问要不要写整改通知。
  _effects: cash 1 (卖掉顺走的帽子); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; condition; flag +burglary_mistaken_for_inspection; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1944.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/wound-infection` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Klara的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Klara看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: health -0.12 (wound infection); cash -14 (drainage and medicine（其中14克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009930_

  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女婴，取名Rosa Huber。接生的人收六克朗，Gustav Huber在走廊烧水，邻居借来一只摇篮；Klara起身时眼前发黑，三天后腰还像断了一样，孩子哭声响亮，把屋里的冷气都顶了回去。
  _effects: birth female; health -0.08000000000000002 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:701_

  Klara在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Berta Weiss算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.040000000000000036; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 60 -> 65; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1944.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting

  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.02** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Rosa Moser 2追出来问怎么不领了，Klara说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Klara把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; food_

- **1944.02** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/broken-tooth` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Klara举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Klara把肉切碎，又去敲Otto Moser 2的门；第二天兽医来了，Berta Weiss在晨会上第一次记住Klara的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 普拉特动物园夜班队 (other); position 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011224; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:702; skill; reputation 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “母狮的右槽牙断了，牙龈肿得发亮，夜里不肯吃马肉。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1945.01** `vienna-life-1945-liberation/displaced` tags: war, liberation, 1945, migration
  炮火把Klara住的那条街烧掉一半。Klara跟着人群走到城外，在临时收容所登记，床位上铺的是发霉的稻草。
  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -5 (temporary shelter（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128_

  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 50; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  舅父在里屋翻账簿半天没出来，舅母趁空从围裙里掏出一卷私房钱，数出十克朗塞给他，说到了家乡先给她写封信。他把钱收下，出门时在门口站了一会儿，才把帽子戴正。
  _effects: cash 10 (舅母私下给的路费); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

  Klara把通知念给Rosa Stein听，Rosa Stein摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

  Klara和Helene Stein叫上楼下两家，一同去房东的事务所。Mitzi Weiss隔着柜台听他们说完，翻出账簿，答应先补上楼梯间的窗闩，涨租从下下个月起算。四人出门时挤在台阶上，谁也没先说话，Helene Stein把帽子往后一推，说总算没白跑。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1946.03** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Josef Winter在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Klara把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1946.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Alois Moser说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Klara站在原地看着Alois Moser拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136; flag +uncertain_courtship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-136_

- **1947.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Klara在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Klara把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010573_

- **1947.01** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.skipped` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  Klara对Friedrich Bauer说这个月实在挤不出来，房租刚涨了半个克朗。Friedrich Bauer把本子翻到另一页，说那就先记着，只是下个月得补齐。午休铃响时，Klara的工具箱上还留着本子压出的印子。
  _effects: relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002667:child_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara被叫到学校，老师说Rosa Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Klara没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 21 -> 16; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）)_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Klara的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Klara想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 27 -> 19; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 45_

- **1947.01** `p10-family-tasks.arrange-childcare/arrange-childcare.quit-job` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, childcare
  厂里说半天工没了就没了，Klara没再争。厨房里少了一只面包，晚饭只有一锅糊糊。伴侣把勺子放下，说来年春天再说。夜里Klara替邻居补袜子，三个赫勒一双，缝到灯芯烧短。
  _effects: cash -4 (辞掉半天工少了一份收入（其中4克朗记入债务）); health -0.04 (营养不足和夜里缝补的劳累); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1947.01** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 13 -> 16; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013451_

- **1947.08** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home
  有人抱怨守夜人太多，踩坏了楼梯木板。楼里的人围着看，谁也没先走开。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/flat-allocated` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  等了三年，住房局终于给Klara一张分配单：利奥波德城一栋战后修复的公寓，两间房，走廊和水龙头仍与隔壁共用。Rosa Weiss帮着把床架抬上三楼，墙上的弹孔用石灰草草抹过。
  _effects: residence; cash -10 (deposit and haulage（其中10克朗记入债务）); mood 0.050000000000000044; role_

- **1948.01** `r13-edu.correspondence-course::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/certificate-by-post` tags: education, correspondence, adult_education, literacy, mail, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara每周收到一叠油印讲义，做完题再寄回去。Lena在页边用红笔改错，有时附一句“第二步再想想”。十二个月后，Klara收到结业证书，纸很薄，但编号印得清楚。证书被Klara钉在床头，邮差再来时，问Klara要不要报下一门课。编号是手写的。
  _effects: flag +correspondence_certificate; skill_

- **1948.04** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Rosa Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Klara把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

- **1948.10** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/speak-with-rent-numbers` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Klara没有喊口号，只问在场谁家的煤还够烧三天。十几只手举起来，连Franz Lechner也举了。书记说这些数字比演说有用，散会时几个人围上来问自己那条街怎么算。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; skill_

- **1948.12** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Josef没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1949.01** `r15-history.movement-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/walked-away` tags: politics, movement, power, risk, history, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara没有接那把椅子。会后Klara照常来，但不再坐在前排。运动继续，Klara的名字还在名单上，只是越来越靠后。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +stepped_back_from_movement_

- **1949.01** `state_reaction:wish-power/power-office` tags: wish-power, wish, wish:power, power, status, ambition, state, state_reaction
  Klara坐在那张有权力的桌子后面，签下自己的名字。愿望没有因此变近，但桌上的纸张、印章和等待回话的人，都变得具体了。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014300_

- **1949.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process
  Klara想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1949.03** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/hired-as-living-exhibit` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  Klara在办公室承认自己连火柴头用什么配都不知道。Helene Stein沉默了一会儿，忽然问Klara愿不愿意每周三下午坐在展厅里，给参观者演示“发明家如何沉思”。酬劳是三克朗，外加一顶借来的礼帽。第一个周三，Klara坐了四个小时，只被一个孩子问过“你会不会变魔术”。
  _effects: cash 3 (扮演活展品的酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:842; role; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +hired_as_living_exhibit; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.06** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-go-downstairs` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  听了那些话，Klara坐不住，当晚走到Anton Winter家楼下，请看门人把原信送上去，自己站在门洞里等。看门人下来时手里拿着那封信，信封还是拆开的，说楼上让有话当面讲，不隔着人传。Klara把帽子捏来捏去，与Franz Winter 2之间也添了一层不自在。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004484:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1949.09** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accepts-the-file` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  户籍官Berta Weiss为了结掉一桩陈年失踪案，在档案上给Klara盖了“推定死亡”。三天后，Klara收到一张慰问卡和一本作废的配给簿；同一天，追了半年的债主忽然不再上门。旧货商听说后，花两克朗买走一份抄本，说要给一个躲债的朋友看看格式。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖给旧货商的死亡证明抄本); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:841; reputation -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; flag +declared_dead_while_alive; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/private-household` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership, household
  Klara和Luise Moser 3合租一间房，对外说是表姐妹。两人共用一只炉子、一本账和一张床，白天各自上工，晚上把门闩好；房租从两人的工钱里各出一半，日子紧，却比各自的旧住处暖，窗台上还并排晾着两只杯子。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133; household; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

- **1950.01** `bc18-ghost.pension-window::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/posthumous-medal` tags: bureaucracy, pension, identity, legal, paperwork, absurd, poverty, vienna
  养老金办公室寄来一枚“忠诚遗属”铜章和三克朗，说这是对死者家属的最后抚恤。Klara不能亲自去领，只好让Berta Huber代收；邻居把铜章别在自家外套上，回来时只字不提三克朗。Klara没有追问，因为那枚铜章若挂在自己胸前，实在不知道该怎么解释。
  _effects: cash -1 (代领材料的车钱（其中1克朗记入债务）); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 20 -> 28; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 1 -> 2; reputation 0.010000000000000009; condition; flag +neighbor_kept_the_medal; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Klara刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Klara站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 28 -> 40; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 1 -> 2; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:014936_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.gramophone-wall/gramophone-wall.knock-and-bargain` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara上楼敲了Rosa Weiss的门，说唱针刮在墙上，夜班的人睡不安稳。Rosa Weiss把留声机从墙边挪到窗下，垫了一块毛毡，答应十点以后不再放。下楼时进行曲还在响，但闷了些，Klara能听见自家水壶的响声了。
  _effects: health 0.03 (夜里终于能早些入睡)_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Franz Huber的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Franz Huber今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 14 -> 22; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 75 -> 72; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Klara学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Klara的材料齐全。年底，Klara从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 25 -> 29; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 35; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Klara仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Klara学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 30 -> 37; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/night-watch` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Klara整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Josef Bauer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Klara在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。
  _effects: health -0.03 (整夜未睡，受凉); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; cash -1.2 (迟到被扣的半天工钱)_

  名单上添的是Rosa 2，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Helene Stein把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Klara也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Rosa 2迟到了七次，工资照发。

  一个税务员把Klara的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Klara收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Klara先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。

- **1951.01** `p10-migration-tasks.find-lodging/lodging-courtyard-room` tags: adulthood, migration, housing, family, travel, task, lodging, arrival
  他挑了朝院子的那间单间，付了一个月押金和半个月房租，房东点清钱才给钥匙。看门人把备用钥匙挂进门房的木板上，嘱咐他晚上十点以后别在楼梯上跺脚。屋里有张床、一只铁炉和半截蜡烛。
  _effects: cash -9 (单间一个月押金与半个月房租（其中9克朗记入债务）); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.05** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Klara把通知念给Alois Lechner 2听，Alois Lechner 2摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Klara去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1951.08** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Katharina Bauer没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Klara听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1951.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  钳子夹住牙根时，Klara听见自己太阳穴里咯的一声。医生往牙槽里塞了团浸药棉，叫咬住别松。下楼时Franz Moser扶着胳膊，说嘴里的血味比啤酒苦多了。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用（其中2.6克朗记入债务）); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.09** `p9-history-medical-daily.clinic-queue/quarrel-with-clerk` tags: health, medical, public_health, family, pharmacy, sanitation, daily
  Klara和门房为号码顺序争起来，被推到街边，帽子滚进水沟。傍晚回来时窗口已经关了，长椅上空空荡荡，只剩一张踩脏的挂号单；Klara在湿冷街头耗了一天，咳嗽更深，诊所门口的人也都记住了这张吵红的脸。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `office16-office.lose-the-chair::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/term-ends-clean` tags: office, removal, loss, politics, power, transition, vienna, urban
  任期在十二月三十一日结束。Klara把印章擦干净，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把；Berta Kraus来接手续，两个人把最后一份移交清单对了两遍。走出大门时，门房照旧叫了一声“先生”，没有加头衔。
  _effects: position_end 名誉主席 @org-sdap; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +left_office_in_order; knowledge “移交清单上最后一项是印章，钥匙串上只剩自己家的那把。”_

- **1952.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Elisabeth Winter嫌Klara的床单滴水滴到Elisabeth Winter家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Klara下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Klara把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Elisabeth Winter那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001838:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Klara把Franz Huber的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Franz Huber今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Klara听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 22 -> 30; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 72 -> 69; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/kept-the-files-apart` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  这一年，Klara学会了同时带三张纸出门：一张证明活着，一张证明死了，一张证明自己从没搬过家。窗口后面的人各自只看自己那一张，偶尔还会夸Klara的材料齐全。年底，Klara从补发的配给里省下一小袋面粉，觉得自己像个熟练的走私犯。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 36 -> 40; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 45 -> 40; cash 1 (省下的配给和车钱); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.01** `p10-family-tasks.find-missing-kin/find-missing-kin.postcard` tags: adulthood, family, social, marriage, care, inheritance, task, missing_kin
  问询表要出生日期和最后通信地址，Klara写到一半停了笔，把哥哥的信从证件夹里翻出来照着抄。寄出去后每天看门房有没有信。两个月后明信片送到，画面是鲁尔河，背面铅笔字被雨水洇过。
  _effects: mood 0.12; cash -1 (寄往鲁尔的信和明信片邮费); knowledge “哥哥在鲁尔矿区做工，还活着”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 22 -> 25; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.cousin-bed/turn-away-with-fare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara直说家里腾不出地方，从抽屉里摸出半克朗车费和一块黑面包，塞给Anton Bauer。Anton Bauer的藤箱提手已经磨毛，在门槛上磕了一下。Klara关上门，听见脚步声沿楼梯往下，看门人在楼下问了一句，Anton Bauer答得含含糊糊；厨房里少了一块面包，也少了一个本该住下的人。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (给表亲车费); food; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Helene Stein 2把一张表贴在食堂门口：本营下月关闭，每人须选择遣返或重新安置。Klara在“遣返”和“安置”两栏之间站了半个钟头。Anna Winter已经签了遣返，说“死也要死在有名字的地方”。
  _effects: condition; flag +camp_closure_notice; knowledge “the camp closes next month and everyone must choose”_

  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Klara在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Mitzi Weiss把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1953.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.bridge` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river
  Klara在桥边站了一会儿，看一艘平底船靠岸卸煤，几个工人用铁锹往岸上甩。风把煤灰吹到她脸上，她抹了一把，往回走时脚步比来时轻，回到巷口还给自己买了块三赫勒的糖。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; cash -0.03 (买了三赫勒的糖（其中0.03克朗记入债务）)_

  _effects: cash -10 (拍卖槌落在第七号（其中10克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +home_sold_at_auction; role; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child; knowledge “the auction buyer is a grain merchant from outside the distr”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153_

  Klara和Gustav Huber在咖啡馆后间谈了一个钟头。没有人知道他们交换了什么，但第二天，两个人都投了对方的票。

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Mitzi的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Mitzi在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。

  _effects: mood 0.05; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Mitzi前面的大婶一直往后靠，Mitzi就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Mitzi的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1922.03** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/ignored-mother-call` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  “再提一桶！”Alois Lechner的声音从灶台边追下来，Mitzi把门在身后带上，脚步没停。傍晚回家，水缸底只剩一层浑水。Alois Lechner把扁担往地上一放，Mitzi挑着两只桶在院子和井台之间走了四趟，肩膀磨得发烫。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (傍晚被罚挑水四趟); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11_

- **1922.04** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-lost-coin` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  菜摊前的地面是湿的。Mitzi弯腰捡土豆时，一枚铜板从指缝里滑出去，顺着水沟格栅掉了下去。Mitzi把剩下的钱摊在柜台上，让摊主帮忙数，还是少了三十克鲁泽。晚上Alois Lechner把钱一枚枚排开，问了三遍怎么少的，Mitzi每答一次，声音就低一点。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (铜板掉进下水道); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.06_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-carried-afternoon` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  井台边的石沿湿得发亮，Elisabeth Lechner一挣，Mitzi只能把Elisabeth Lechner抱离地面。院子里晒的床单还没收，Mitzi背着Elisabeth Lechner在布影里来回走，用一块碎布给Elisabeth Lechner擦鼻涕。等Elisabeth Lechner睡沉，Mitzi的肩窝已经压出一道红痕，端碗时手指直抖。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1923.05** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  Mitzi把书压在算术本底下，只露出书脊。Grete Hofer走过来，抽走算术本，书也跟着滑出来。折角那页画着一艘帆船。Grete Hofer合上书，说放学后擦黑板，擦一星期；书被锁进讲台抽屉，没往校长室送。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-knowledge/knowledge-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_tick
  Mitzi暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1924.06** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/take-over-bed-and-ledger` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Mitzi Lechner走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Mitzi把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Mitzi坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; skill; mood -0.04999999999999999; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/muted-practice` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi在阁楼练琴，楼下邻居上来敲门，说孩子要睡觉。Mitzi把弱音器夹上，声音闷得像蚊子。练到半夜，Mitzi索性放下琴，把谱子默写了一遍，第二天在课上凭记忆拉完，Luise Kral问Mitzi是不是没睡好。第二天Mitzi把弱音器取下来，换到更远的阁楼。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; skill_

- **1925.04** `r3-teen.parent-remarriage::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/accepts` tags: family, remarriage, stepfamily, grief, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Kral把漏雨的屋檐修好，又在门牌上添了自己的姓。Mitzi没有叫他父亲，只在他修窗户时递钉子。晚饭桌上，Hermann Kral把最大的一块肉夹给Mitzi，Alois Lechner低头笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +accepted_parent_remarriage; relation guardian with person-hermann-kral_

- **1925.05** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Hofer把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Mitzi看期限、工钱和出师考试。Mitzi签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:64; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.07** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Luise Kral把成绩单按在讲台上，说Mitzi还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Mitzi签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.11** `r1-fam.schooling-decision::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/school.apprenticeship` tags: family, education, apprenticeship, poverty, future, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Kral把学徒合同摊在桌上，说三年管饭，出师后一天两克朗。Luise Kral说Mitzi的算术是班里最好的，再读两年可以考师范。Alois Lechner把合同推回去，说家里等不起两年；Mitzi看着窗外，操场上有人在踢一只破球。
  _effects: cash 2 (学徒合同还是课本); relation colleague with person-maria-kral; knowledge “老师认定这个学生能考上师范。”; education apprenticeship_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:wish-knowledge/knowledge-humiliation` tags: wish-knowledge, wish, wish:knowledge, knowledge, education, curiosity, state, state_reaction
  Mitzi把那句轻视记了很久。它没有变成一个决定，但也没有消失。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +wish:knowledge:wounded_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002371_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Mitzi又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/own-marks-slipped` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Mitzi每晚给Katharina Lechner讲题，自己的作业只能留到深夜。期中考试，Mitzi的名次掉了十几位，Luise Kral在家长会上点名。回家后，Mitzi看见Katharina Lechner趴在桌上睡着了，算术本还摊在胳膊下面，就没有叫醒。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +neglected_own_studies; relation rival with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.02** `yf18-body.danube-cabin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/wade-in-first` tags: body, desire, river, swimming, shame, first_love, reform, medical
  多瑙河的浴棚按男女分开，木板缝里能看见对面的人影。Mitzi穿着借来的毛线泳衣，站在第三间门口不敢下水；Elisabeth Bauer从水里冒出来，头发贴在额头上，说水不冷。Mitzi走进河里时，满脑子都是自己肋骨上那道疤。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:88; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.02999999999999997_

- **1926.06** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/stay-in-school` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  教室里只剩炉子噼啪响。Luise Kral把算术本翻到最后一页，指着那道应用题，说再念一年，商业学校可以考。Mitzi用铅笔在边上重算了一遍。门外有脚步声，Alois Lechner站在走廊里，帽檐压低半指，听完才走。
  _effects: education secondary; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1926.07** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/fined` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Gustav Berg说按条例罚三克朗，交不出就通知学校。Alois Lechner从内衣口袋里摸出叠好的钞票，一张张数，数到第三张时手停了。回家后，那三克朗被写在厨房的账单上，旁边画了一道红线。
  _effects: cash -3 (派出所里的第一次签字); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +fined_by_police; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1926.09** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/read-it-to-the-class` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi以为那是谁开的玩笑，把地图册举起来念给全班听。Luise Kral问是谁写的；教室最后一排有人把书立起来挡住脸。放学后，Elisabeth Bauer在楼梯口等Mitzi，只说了一句“你念错了一个字”，就从他身边走过去。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:87; reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/printshop-apprentice` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, employment
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Mitzi下周一到排字房报到，先搬铅字和扫地，月薪十八克朗。家里用第一笔预付工资买了双结实的鞋。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance)_

- **1927.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Mitzi看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Mitzi是不是不想出师了。Mitzi没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Emil Weiss 2接手。
  _effects: relation employer with person-ferdinand-winter; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 25; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Mitzi废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.03999999999999998_

  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Mitzi在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Mitzi准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Mitzi才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  Mitzi把六枚克朗按在登记簿旁，Luise Moser抬起眼镜看了看他的出生日期，在夜班名单上写下名字。他领到一张油印课程表，纸角还湿着。
  _effects: cash -6 (职业夜校第一期学费); education apprenticeship; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  考试周里，Mitzi把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Mitzi用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 47; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 25 -> 27_

  Luise Kral把一本拉丁文课本推到Mitzi面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Mitzi翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Mitzi把纸抄了一遍，塞进Anton的历史作业本；那天晚上，Mitzi第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Josef在第二支曲子前把Klara请走，说Mitzi答应过要替她看管外套。Mitzi抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Mitzi把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 40 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 2; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 20 -> 25; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Hermann Berg在楼梯间坐了很久；Mitzi在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Mitzi的名字；此后邻居提起Mitzi，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。

  _effects: cash 2 (the silver spoon redeemed with the old pawn ticket); flag +worthless_strongbox; knowledge “墙里的旧纸币已经作废，当票还能换回一只银勺。”_

- **1939.05** `r3-mig.refugee-aid-society::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-refugee-relief/police-raid-on-society` tags: migration, relief, organization, charity, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  两个便衣从后门进来，Helene Stein把名册摊在桌上，一页页翻。Elisabeth Huber说名册上只有领汤的人，Helene Stein说“领汤的人也要登记原籍”。地下室的锅被踢翻，豆汤流到门槛。Mitzi从煤槽爬出去时，听见Elisabeth Huber在里屋咳嗽。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +police_raid_on_aid_society; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; knowledge “the police take the society’s membership ledger”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1939.08** `hs18-occupation.piano::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/buy-it-back` tags: occupation, piano, requisition, witness, neighbor, history, vienna, urban
  Mitzi把两条舍不得抽的烟塞给Helene Stein，说这琴是替邻居保管的。Helene Stein掂了掂烟，让工人把琴抬回门厅，却把琴凳留下了。Franz Lechner晚上来道谢，Mitzi说别谢，明天要是再来，就说不认识我。
  _effects: cash -3 (two packs of cigarettes for the officer); relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:490; flag +bought_back_neighbors_piano; mood 0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-102_

- **1940.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006547_

- **1940.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/counter-motion` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  Mitzi没有等投票，而是让Alois Huber把委员会过去三年的差旅账目复印了十份。会议开始前，Alois Weiss的动议被一份“程序审查”取代；审查没有找到Mitzi的问题，却找到了两次重复报销。
  _effects: reputation -0.02999999999999997; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1940.01** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-back` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, vienna
  Ferdinand Huber把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Mitzi解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Mitzi Bauer递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

  Mitzi忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Mitzi听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。

- **1940.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Mitzi把通知念给Josef Bauer听，Josef Bauer摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Mitzi去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  Mitzi在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1940.03** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Lena追出来问怎么不领了，Mitzi说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Mitzi把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: food_

  Mitzi的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  Mitzi的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  门房说Mitzi家的女儿在车站跟一个穿军装的人上了车。话传到三楼时，女儿还没回家；Mitzi去问Mitzi Bauer，Mitzi Bauer说只是看见她替人提箱子。Mitzi Lechner在厨房门口听着，把面包掰成两半，谁也没接。

  Alois Lechner在第一个人面前就站住了，说那件灰外套不会错。Ferdinand Huber没有再看Mitzi的眼睛，只让警士把手铐拿出来。Mitzi被推上囚车时，听见Alois Lechner在身后说：“我记得那个人走路的样子。”其实Mitzi的左脚并不跛。
  _effects: state_add detained state:world-vienna-1912:608; relation rival with person-alois-lechner; flag +identified_by_witness; mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +detained_

  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1946.10** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Gustav Huber把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Mitzi把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Gustav Huber低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); flag +rent_increase_accepted_

  Mitzi说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Berta Winter 3没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Mitzi听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; flag +funeral_society_left_

- **1952.08** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.sign` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  印泥是赭红色的，像干了的面包瓤。Mitzi按完手印，工头把表举到窗边吹干，阳光里那张纸透出指印的轮廓。楼下第十区的街面传来运煤车的铁皮声，星期六下午突然显得很宽。
  _effects: cash -1 (星期六下午减少的计件工钱); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +accepted_hours_reform; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-157_

- **1952.11** `vienna-historical-social-movement.public-meeting/stay-and-tidy-chairs` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  椅子叠到一半，Anton Winter压低声音说今晚有三个人没投票就走了。Mitzi把最后一块瓜子壳扫进簸箕，说记住那三张脸，比上台多说一句有用。灯熄时大厅只剩木头发出的轻响。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004484:child_

- **1953.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/nothing-moved` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Mitzi在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Elisabeth Kral 2把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146_

- **1953.01** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.moving-job/moving-finished-paid` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, moving, neighbor
  楼梯窄得只容一个人侧身，Mitzi和Emil Hofer一前一后抬着柜子，Grete Winter在楼下喊当心磕墙。床架拆成两截，捆在手推车上推到城外新住处，回来时天已经擦黑。
  _effects: cash 3 (帮鞋匠搬家的工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-155_

- **1953.02** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-sell-up` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  床架从楼梯上拖下来时油漆蹭掉了好几块，Mitzi站在旁边没作声。收破烂的走后，屋里空得回声都变大了，Mitzi抱着铁盒出门，只把门锁好，钥匙从看门人手里交出去。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉旧床架和桌子收进的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153_

- **1953.10** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.flea-market-cup/flea-market-cup.walk-back-too-late` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, flea_market, object
  旧货摊收了一半，铁皮盒里的零钱叮当作响。Mitzi问那只描金边的杯子，摊主用手背往街角一指，说刚有个女人挑走了，还说那裂纹其实拿胶补补能用。她低头看摊布上留下的一个圆印，是杯子搁久了压出来的。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002694:child; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-132_

- **1954.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Mitzi在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Mitzi把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016970_

- **1954.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/appointed-rival` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Mitzi给Berta Weiss安排了一个“礼仪顾问”的位置：有办公室、有名牌、有固定工资，没有任何需要签字的文件。Berta Weiss接受了，并且在第一次会议上坐到了离主席最远的那把椅子上。
  _effects: position 礼仪顾问 @cinema:person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:006029; relation rival with person-berta-weiss; flag +bought_off_rival; knowledge “礼仪顾问的岗位没有需要签字的文件。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Mitzi想帮忙，但组织者不信任新来的人，只让其在外围等消息。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Mitzi Bauer 3一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Mitzi Bauer 3的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi Bauer 3嫌Mitzi的床单滴水滴到Mitzi Bauer 3家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Mitzi下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Mitzi把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Mitzi Bauer 3那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017621_

  Mitzi在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Alois Kral 2算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1955.04** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Mitzi记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Lena说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Lena陪着Mitzi去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-vote-ties` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Mitzi把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Elisabeth Huber 2在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Mitzi说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

- **1956.01** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.ask-straight` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Mitzi在楼梯间拦住Luise Weiss 2，问那句话是从哪儿听来的，又趁送活的人上门时当面看清是谁。原来是对面作坊的裁缝来取改好的裤子。Luise Weiss 2听完把话咽回去，第二天洗衣房里没人再提。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  汤锅冒着热气，Mitzi盛出两勺倒进Gustav Winter带来的罐子里。Gustav Winter说了声谢谢就匆匆上楼。隔天罐子还回来，锅底搁着一小块猪油。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006388:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1956.06** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sell-the-shares-to-a-baron` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, vienna, urban
  矿山还没出金，Mitzi就把股份转给了一个想给女儿做嫁妆的男爵，赚了二十五克朗。半年后，假金矿的消息见报，男爵在俱乐部里拦住Mitzi，问知不知道岩芯的事。Mitzi说不知道，手却一直放在酒杯后面。
  _effects: cash 25 (profit from selling the mining shares early); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1153; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +sold_mine_shares_early; knowledge “男爵买下股份时，矿山还没有出过一克金。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-173; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-by-committee` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  编制委员会只用了十分钟就否掉了Berta Lechner 2的名字：预算冻结，岗位归并，连桌子都要搬去仓库。Mitzi把那张表折起来，答应明年再试。Berta Lechner 2说没关系，第二天去了另一家事务所面试。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165; flag +protege_hiring_refused; knowledge “编制委员会今年冻住了所有新增岗位。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-165_

- **1957.07** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  Mitzi说胡话时念着车间的排班表，Emil Bauer一句句应着，拿小勺往嘴里送温水。床头那半瓶退烧药早见了底，她没舍得倒最后一滴，留着天亮再说。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-55_

- **1957.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Rosa Weiss把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Mitzi说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Emil在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Mitzi的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002924:child; reputation -0.07; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1957.11** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Mitzi把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Rosa合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912_

### 210 subject events

- **1917.08** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  窗户上的玻璃响了一下，有人探出头，让Josef立刻回家。铁环还在Mitzi Lechner脚边，Josef蹲下去抽，抽不动。Mitzi Lechner说除非明天把铁钩带来。Josef点头，Mitzi Lechner把脚一抬，铁环滚到Josef手边，上面沾了一层鞋底灰。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Josef Winter在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Josef Winter数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Berta Stein把Josef拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Josef把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Josef蹲下把两包并排，按Berta Stein说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1920.01** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-gift` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  烟草店的门铃响一次，Josef就往柜台边挪一步。Mitzi终于从柜台底下抽出一张风景卡，用手掌抹了抹，还是皱的。“拿去吧。”Mitzi说完就去招呼别的客人。Josef把卡举到鼻子前闻，一股烟草味，回家路上把多出来的一张卡分给Mitzi看。
  _effects: mood 0.06; reputation 0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Josef拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Josef Bauer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Josef接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Josef后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Josef没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Josef用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Josef站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Gustav Berg在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Josef掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Josef才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Hermann Stein把Josef拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Josef把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Josef蹲下把两包并排，按Hermann Stein说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1923.11** `vienna-childhood-street.cigarette-card/card-stolen` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷口有人跑过来，肩膀撞在Josef的胳膊上。烟画卡脱手，被风卷到路中间。Josef追了两步，鞋底在湿石板上打滑，手掌先着地。Elisabeth Bauer从后面追上去，在拐角抓住那男孩的袖子，布条撕下来一截，人还是跑了。
  _effects: mood -0.08; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; condition_

- **1924.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1925.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Helene Weiss把Josef叫到巷口，问Emil叫什么、住在哪间屋。Josef答到一半就停住了。Helene Weiss说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Josef回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Emil正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Josef。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1926.01** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Josef把手套交给Anton，让Anton替自己去还。Anton回来时说，Mitzi问是谁捡到的；Anton顺口报了一个名字，Josef听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:86; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.join-a-club/worked-for-dues` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Josef翻遍口袋只有几枚赫勒，便跟管事的说愿替大家抄歌本抵会费。他坐在窗边抄了一下午，手指被墨水染黑，换来的借书权让他捧回一本讲齿轮的旧书，书页里夹着上一个人留下的铅笔字。
  _effects: skill; skill; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; flag +workers_youth_work_trade; mood 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1926.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition, injury
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Josef站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Luise Kral追出来说座位可以保留两周，Josef点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; health -0.040000000000000036 (连续夜间加班)_

- **1926.04** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/finish-in-falsetto` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  教堂音乐会的独唱轮到Josef，第一句拉丁文还稳，唱到“荣耀”那个高音时，嗓子忽然劈了一下。Josef看见Josef Bauer在第三排低下头，Luise Kral在风琴旁边打手势让继续；Josef用假声把剩下的两小节唱完，声音细得像一根线。
  _effects: reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +yf18_finished_solo; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: reputation -0.04; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-katharina-bauer; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”_

  Elisabeth Bauer在第二支曲子前把Klara请走，说Josef答应过要替她看管外套。Josef抱着一件不属于自己的大衣站在圣像下面，看他们在蜡烛光里转圈；回家路上，Josef把舞卡撕成小片，扔进多瑙运河。

  Josef在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Klara。这一次对方先点了头，Josef准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Josef才发现，自己把书拿倒了。

  一年过去，Josef能在菜市场用德语讲价，也知道哪家铺子肯赊半块黄油。房东仍叫他“新来的”，但楼下的面包师已经记住了他买哪一种面包，还会把前一天剩的那只小圆面包留出来。

  Josef忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Josef听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。

  Josef在柱子后面站了两支曲子，才走过去问Elisabeth Berg 3下一支能不能一起跳。Elisabeth Berg 3把手里的手套交给Otto Winter，跟着Josef走进场子。Josef踩了两次对方的鞋尖，第三次终于跟上拍子；Otto Winter在墙边把钟点记在手袋上。
  _effects: cash -1 (乐队奏到第三支曲子); flag +danced_together; role; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1931.07** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Alois Lechner提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1931.08** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/borrows_coal_risk` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Emil Weiss 2，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Emil Weiss 2第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Josef当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (受凉咳嗽); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1931.10** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.with-priest` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Alois Lechner 2在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; mood 0.03999999999999998; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 67 -> 69_

- **1932.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/flowers-placed` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, reform, medical
  Josef陪Anna Stein去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Anna Stein用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Josef没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1932.06** `bc18-inherit.forty-seven-franz::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hearse-as-taxi` tags: inheritance, mistaken_identity, debt, funeral, work, absurd, family, reform
  四十七个弗朗茨·穆勒里，只有Josef拿得出干净的房租簿。公证人Helene Stein把遗嘱念完，遗产是一辆灵车、两匹老马和十四克朗的欠账。Josef没有钱下葬，先把灵车租给搬家的人；夜里跑环城大道，白天替人运钢琴。Ferdinand Bauer起初等着看笑话，后来开始给他介绍生意。
  _effects: cash 5 (用灵车替人搬家的收入); role; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:250; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; flag +hearse_became_a_moving_van; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.07** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sold-the-share` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, reform, medical
  Anna Stein把Josef寄放在杂货铺后间的木箱搬走了，里面是两个人合买的缝纫机零件和一小袋银币。Josef追到码头时，Anna Stein已经上了船，只留下一张写着“算我借的”的纸条。一个月后，Josef在旧货摊上看见那台缝纫机，摊主说是从当铺收来的。
  _effects: cash -4 (被卷走的合买本钱); flag +betrayed_over_money; knowledge “我的合伙人带着合买的零件和银币上了船。”; condition; mood -0.08000000000000002_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_
