  _effects: cash -1 (外套的干洗费); reputation -0.04000000000000001; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 25 -> 33; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 20 -> 15; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; condition; flag +egg_on_the_tenor_statue; mood -0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1933.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Josef仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Josef学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。

  Josef发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Maria Moser问是谁动的。Maria Moser说是Alois Lechner嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。

  赛马场外的泥地上，Berta Huber 2把Josef的工钱数进帽子里，说“下周翻本”。Gustav Winter拽了拽Josef的袖子，Josef没有动。回家的电车上，Josef把口袋翻了三遍，只摸到一张赛马票的存根。第二天上工，Josef把工票藏在鞋垫下面，怕家里翻出来。那天夜里，Josef把赛马票的存根烧了，灰留在鞋垫上。

- **1935.09** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.letter-answer/mother-opened-letter` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  回信没有到Josef手里。Rosa Kral的母亲先拆了它，连信封上的邮戳一起摊在洗衣房的桌上，说有人写字来缠自家孩子。话传到院子里时已经添了油，Friedrich Hofer 2隔着栏杆把原话学了一遍，说得很难听；Rosa Kral再见到Josef时，眼睛先往母亲那边看。Josef站在水桶边，知道这封信已经被拆成了两家的脸面。
  _effects: reputation -0.05999999999999997; mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-76_

  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Josef看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; health -0.040000000000000036 (没有正规病假，只能靠药水撑着); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  _effects: cash -2 (利息被重新计算); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 15 -> 21; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 15 -> 10_

- **1936.01** `r14-spec.hoax-newspaper::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-canals-of-mars` tags: hoax, newspaper, media, humiliation, fraud, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把一篇“火星运河观测”的稿子放在Luise Kral桌上，附了一张自己画的星图。Luise Kral问证据在哪，Josef说天文学家太忙，没空否认。报纸加印两次，街上有人仰头看天，Franz Lechner连夜排字，手指沾满黑铅。八克朗广告分成装进信封；三天后，Mitzi Bauer在另一家报纸上写文章驳斥，反而让更多人买了头版。
  _effects: cash 8 (整版登出的火星人); reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +spec14_hoax_success; relation colleague with person-luise-kral; knowledge “the star chart was drawn around the rim of a coffee cup”_

  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛)_

  _effects: health -0.12 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial); role_

  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.10000000000000003; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand_

  _effects: cash -1 (还掉一小笔); state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.debt 21 -> 13; state_parameter df18-borrowed-luck.luck 10 -> 13; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  助理医生拿不准，叫了救护车。Josef被抬进医院，走廊里等了六个小时才排到床位；家里人后来算了一笔账，救护车比药贵。救护车在石板上颠，他抓着担架边，数着路灯。医院门口的担架已经排到街上。
  _effects: move place-wien-allgemeines-krankenhaus; cash -15 (ambulance and hospital admission); health 0.05 (admitted for observation and oxygen); role; knowledge “实习医生拿不准就会叫救护车；走廊等六个小时才有床，车费比药贵。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1937.08** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-picked-coal` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  同来的Berta Winter 2没被点上，骂了一声把麻绳甩上肩走了。Josef跟着工头进了货栈，煤块从跳板滑进船舱，到午后点了四克朗二十赫勒，手指黑得洗不净。
  _effects: cash 4.2 (卸煤半日的工钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

  Josef说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Berta Winter 3没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Josef听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; flag +funeral_society_left; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  _effects: cash -12 (疗养院车票和住宿押金); health 0.12000000000000001 (疗养院松林空气和正规休养); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  Josef在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。

  Josef拿着锡罐在市场和厂门口转了一天。肉铺老板放了两枚克朗，面包房给了一袋隔夜面包，Katharina Bauer把准备交房租的钱数出三分之一。天黑时锡罐里有四十多克朗，Josef把每一笔都写在包装纸上，连半赫勒也没漏。
  _effects: cash -2 (罢工第七天的汤锅); food; flag +work13_strike_fund_collected; relation friend with person-katharina-bauer; organization_funds_

- **1940.06** `oj18-mortuary.first-body::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/refused-the-child` tags: death, mortuary, funeral, work, odd_job, night, everyday, vienna
  Josef跟着Alois Huber进到后间，看见一具孩子的棺材，尺寸只比鞋盒大一点。Josef把毛巾放回盆里，说自己做不了。Alois Huber没有劝，只把当天的工钱数给Josef；那枚铜板在口袋里响了一路，像有人在后面跟着。
  _effects: cash 1 (a day's mortuary pay); mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; condition; flag +refused_mortuary; knowledge “那具孩子的棺材只比鞋盒大一点，{master}没有劝我留下。”_

- **1940.10** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Katharina Bauer把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Katharina Bauer低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1941.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 38 -> 34; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 16 -> 14; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1941.01** `r14-work.negotiate-loan::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/only-nine-kronen` tags: debt, loan, money, institution, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  Gustav Bauer 2只肯借九克朗，还要Josef把工钱袋每月先送到柜台。Josef Winter说这条件比利息还重，Gustav Bauer 2把借据折起来，说那就请便。Josef追到门口，还是把九克朗收下了，纸币上有一股烟草和樟脑混在一起的味道。
  _effects: cash 9 (借据上的第二行); condition; flag +took_small_loan; knowledge “the lender wants the wage packet delivered before rent”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

- **1941.04** `r3-mig.boarding-house::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/accused-of-theft` tags: migration, housing, poverty, lodging, neighbor, vienna, urban, crisis
  Alois Lechner 2的怀表不见了，Berta Winter 3把Josef的包袱倒在走廊上。没有表，只有两件衬衣和一块干面包。Alois Lechner 2说算了，Berta Winter 3却把Josef的床位从窗边换到门口，说“这样大家都看得见”。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +accused_of_theft_in_lodging; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; knowledge “the window bed is the first to be searched”_

- **1941.04** `r3-mig.transit-camp::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-army/partner-separated-by-nationality` tags: migration, refugees, camp, family, papers, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Stein说夫妻不同国籍要分开登记，Hermann Stein 2被分到铁丝网另一侧。Josef隔着两道网把一块面包递过去，Hermann Stein 2没接住，面包落在泥里。Helene Stein吹哨让所有人回棚，Josef看见Hermann Stein 2的手还伸在网眼上。
  _effects: condition; flag +partner_separated_by_nationality; relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; knowledge “the camp separates married couples of different nationality”_

- **1941.09** `kernel-migrate.neighborhood-rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/civic-rumor-believe` tags: migration, rumor, neighbor, work, rural, vienna, urban, crisis
  楼梯间的水管又漏了，Katharina Bauer一边接水一边说，城北新开的工厂在招女工，一天给两克朗。他回屋把这句话记在账单背面，第二天去车站问车次，售票员说那条线已经改道。
  _effects: flag +believed_city_work_rumor; knowledge “城北新工厂招女工的说法来自邻居，车站说去那边的线路已改道”; relation acquaintance with person-katharina-bauer; cash -0.2 (去车站问车次的车钱)_

- **1941.11** `mb17-border.permit-window::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-back` tags: migration, border, papers, permit, station, official, belonging, vienna
  Berta Weiss把材料推回来，说出生纸缺了教区印章。Josef解释自己是从乡下赶来的，对方只指了指墙上的告示；排在后头的Anna Stein递来半块面包，两个人坐在候车室里等下一班回去的车，谁也没有先说话。
  _effects: cash -2 (the return ticket and a wasted day); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; flag +mb17_turned_back_at_border; knowledge “边境窗口只承认带教区印章的出生证明。”_

- **1942.01** `r14-work.loan-default::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/furniture-carried-into-the-corridor` tags: debt, loan, loan_default, law, household, vienna, urban, crisis
  到期日过了三天，Gustav Bauer 2带着法院的人上楼，把缝纫机和两只木箱抬到走廊里登记。Alois Weiss把一张粉色的纸贴在镜子上，说七天内可以赎。Lena把门开了一条缝，又轻轻合上。Josef蹲在地上捡散落的线轴，线缠住了手指。
  _effects: cash -20 (到期日那天); condition; flag +loan_default; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; knowledge “the household has seven days to redeem the seized furniture”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009641_

- **1942.01** `lc17-care.crisis-night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-army/stays-the-night` tags: adulthood, care, eldercare, night, family, exhaustion, illness, vienna
  Josef Bauer在凌晨三点按响床边的铃，说只是想喝水。Josef从折叠床上坐起来，把水杯递过去，又顺手换了尿湿的床单；窗外的煤气灯已经灭了，屋里只剩钟摆和呼吸声。天亮时，Josef在厨房站着睡着了一刻钟。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 34 -> 46; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 14 -> 17; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +night_watch_

  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 46 -> 42; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 17 -> 15; health -0.010000000000000002 (a year of broken nights); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1942.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Elisabeth Huber 2把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Josef在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Rosa Moser 2分给Josef半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1942.02** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/dentist-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  诊室在二楼，楼梯间飘着石炭酸味。医生把钳子举到灯下看了看，叫Josef攥紧扶手。Elisabeth Berg 3站在门边数墙上的价目表：拔牙两克朗五十赫勒，药棉五十赫勒，连消炎水另算。
  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛)_

- **1942.04** `vienna-housing-move.moving-day/moving-day-handcart` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Josef把照片铁盒交给Josef Winter抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。
  _effects: cash -1 (借手推车买烟熨谢邻居的小开销); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

- **1942.07** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/courtyard-quarrel` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  话没说完就变成了争。Hermann Stein 2把外套扔在晾衣杆下，Josef说了一句不中听的，声音大得整院都听见。Franz Lechner在三楼把窗开了一条缝，看完了全程，第二天院子里人人都知道；这一架把两个人的关系吵断了，也把Josef的名字在院子里吵薄了一层。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; reputation -0.07_

  Anton Bauer把条件说完，Josef把手插回口袋，说这门生意找别人。Anton Bauer没有生气，只把烟头踩灭，说这条街上不缺愿意干的人。Josef走出巷子，才发现后背的衬衫已经湿透。Emil Weiss 2在街角等着，问谈得怎么样。
  _effects: cash -1 (仓库后门的提议); mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +refused_criminal_offer; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the fixer keeps a list of men who owe him money”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54_

  Josef在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Josef把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:010145_

  Josef又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Josef的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。

  Lena在门口拦住Josef，说这阵子别来了，名单上多一个名字就多一份麻烦。Josef把歌本还回去，走到街角又回头，看见Freie Gewerkschaft der graphischen Arbeiter Wiens的窗户用报纸糊了一半，里面还有人低声唱。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +kept_off_choir_roll_for_safety; knowledge “the choir is being watched”_

- **1943.04** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef和Luise Moser 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Josef家，钥匙只在白天给。Luise Moser 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Anna Stein听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Josef把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1943.06** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Josef请楼下的Emil Winter 2白天来照看Katharina Bauer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Emil Winter 2上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Josef下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Katharina Bauer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; health 0.039999999999999994 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

- **1943.09** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.salt` tags: home, market, food, social
  腌鲱鱼切成两段，一段今天吃，一段留着。黑面包硬得要用刀锯。Alois Lechner 2来借盐，眼睛落在鱼上，说可以用半棵卷心菜换一块。Josef切了一指宽递过去。
  _effects: cash -2 (腌鲱鱼和黑面包); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-14_

- **1944.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Anton Bauer在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Anton Bauer在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1944.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-competent` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  Josef把Mitzi的名字写在编制表的空行上，又在旁边注了“试用三个月”。Berta Weiss提醒说预算只够半个位置，Josef还是把名字留下了。第二天，Mitzi提前半小时到，把走廊里的旧档案按年份重新排了一遍。
  _effects: position 助理文书 @morgue:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:009953; relation colleague with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +hired_protege_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef Bauer今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Josef在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 50 -> 58; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 16 -> 22; cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Josef暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Josef走到Arbeiter-Zeitung im Vorwärts-Haus门口，看见便衣和拥挤的人群，最终没有进去。

- **1944.03** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stove-and-line/stove-and-line.move-to-line-end` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef把自己泡着的衣服端到绳子最末一段，把靠近炉子的位置让给Anna Stein，条件是对方看着火，别让水烧干。Anna Stein点头，往炉膛里添了一块煤，说中午以前一定把炉子空出来。两家的盆就挨着放在窗下。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; condition_

  _effects: cash -4 (medicine); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

  Josef把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Josef折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。

  _effects: cash -2 (municipal housing application); role_

  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱)_

- **1946.02** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Luise Weiss 2把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Luise Weiss 2在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。

  书箱比看起来沉，Josef一个人抱下楼时膝盖直打颤。Hermann Stein 2把午饭摆在新住处的窗台上，黑面包夹着咸肉，汤用旧罐子装着。工钱给的是铜币，数起来哗啦响。

- **1946.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money

  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

- **1947.05** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1947.06** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-go-downstairs` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  听了那些话，Josef坐不住，当晚走到Berta Lechner家楼下，请看门人把原信送上去，自己站在门洞里等。看门人下来时手里拿着那封信，信封还是拆开的，说楼上让有话当面讲，不隔着人传。Josef把帽子捏来捏去，与Josef Winter之间也添了一层不自在。

- **1947.08** `statistical_recovery`
  Josef从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1947.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

  Josef把木架上的《新自由报》从头版读到讣告栏，中间续了两次水，第三杯 Melange 端上来时奶泡已经塌了，三杯一共六十赫勒。侍者把找零的铜板拍在桌上，没有催的意思；Josef折好报纸夹回木架，记住了议会辩论里的两个名字，出门时雨还没停。

- **1950.07** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/license-it-and-keep-the-patent` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  Josef没有卖断，而是和小巷电影院签了按瓶抽成的许可：每卖出一千瓶，付两克朗。第一年只卖了四千瓶，Josef拿到八克朗，却保住了专利。Berta Weiss在许可书上盖了一个小章，说这种条款以后会值钱；Josef把章看了一会儿，没敢笑。
  _effects: cash 8 (first royalty payment on the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:888; skill; flag +licensed_patent; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Josef只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Katharina Lechner手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Katharina Lechner把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000520:child_

- **1950.09** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Emil Winter把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Emil Winter低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001824:child_

  铅笔头短得捏不住，Josef把它夹在两指之间，在表格背面写下两个名字。被漏掉的人一个叫扬，一个叫弗兰茨，都是每天清早从火车站走来的临时工。工头看了一眼背面，没说话，把表贴回墙上。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005540:child; reputation 0.04000000000000001_

- **1953.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Anna 2从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004210:child_

- **1953.11** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.sugar-jar` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  Josef把信压在厨房的糖罐底下，想着明天再问。三天后糖罐渗了油，信角洇出一片深色，收信人的名字糊了半个。她把信拿到门房那儿，门房说这地址已经退过两回了。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; skill_

- **1954.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Anna 2嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Anna 2家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Anna 2那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004210:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016926_

- **1954.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/nothing-moved` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  账本看上去是平的：修屋顶的钱被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。Josef在每一栏都签了字，知道明年还会这样。散会后，Helene Stein把一张催修屋顶的纸条压在台灯下面。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +deferred_maintenance; knowledge “屋顶的修理费被挪去补煤款，煤款又被挪去付印刷费。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1954.07** `r3-mig.family-reunification::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sibling-refused-liability` tags: migration, family, sponsorship, papers, debt, vienna, urban, postwar
  Mitzi Weiss念到“被担保人失业或生病，由担保人承担费用”时，Klara的笔停在纸上。Klara说自己有两个孩子，房租已经欠了两个月。Josef说不怪你。回家的电车上，两个人一路没有说话，Klara提前一站下了车。
  _effects: flag +sponsor_refused_liability; relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “joint liability covers unemployment and illness”_

- **1954.08** `r3-crime.domestic-theft::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::empty-rice-crock/parent-reported-it` tags: crime, family, poverty, shame, household, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef Bauer在派出所门口站了很久，最后还是推门进去了。Josef被叫去问话时，Josef Bauer坐在长椅另一头，手杖横在膝盖上。警察问“确定要告吗”，Josef Bauer说“不告他还会偷”，声音很轻，Josef却听得清清楚楚。
  _effects: cash -3 (米缸后面藏着一只银勺); condition; flag +family_reported_theft_to_police; role; relation rival with person-josef-bauer; relation_end kin with person-josef-bauer_

- **1954.11** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Grete Winter手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Grete Winter把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Josef记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food_

- **1955.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sinecure-created` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Josef在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Anna Huber 2第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Mitzi Weiss发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @morgue:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:009953; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153; flag +private_sinecure; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-153_

- **1955.01** `r13-edu.literacy-class::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signs-own-name` tags: education, literacy, adult_education, class, belonging, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef在石板上写自己的名字，写到第三遍才把最后一笔收住。Lena把Josef的手按在粉笔上，说慢慢来。放学时，Josef把石板抱在怀里，经过肉铺时第一次看懂招牌上写的是“今日有肝”。那天晚上，Josef在账本封皮上又写了一遍，写到最后一笔时手没有抖。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +learned_to_sign_name; skill_

- **1955.11** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef把通知念给Grete Winter听，Grete Winter摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Emil Winter在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Emil Winter在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001824:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `task-vienna-family-care.inheritance-guardianship/signed-with-guardian` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  公证人Josef把遗嘱逐条念完，房产登记和监护人同意书摆在一处，Emil没有作声，Josef Bauer按老人自己的意思签了字，公证人Josef把墨水印吹干。房产暂时归照顾老人的一方，最小的孩子由谁监护也写清了。办完手续付了五克朗又四十赫勒的费用。
  _effects: cash -5.4 (公证与登记的手续费); relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; flag +inheritance_registered; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1956.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/sweat-it-out` tags: health, medical, family, money
  醋水的酸味混着汗气，屋子小得转不开身。Emil Huber 2把布拧到不滴水，敷上去时Josef打了个激灵。窗外送奶车吱呀碾过石板，她才想起自己家的炉子还灭着。
  _effects: condition; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100_

- **1956.11** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.factory-gate/gate-queue-clash` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, factory, hiring
  招工纸上的工钱写得明白，一天三克朗八十赫勒。Josef怕再等下去名额没了，从人缝里挤到前面，后面的Luise Weiss 2一把拽住袖子。门房抬头看了看乱糟糟的队伍，把玻璃窗拉上了。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1957.01** `r13-fam.vouch-for-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/refused-to-sign` tags: task, social, friendship, debt, guarantee, risk, vienna, urban
  Josef把借据推回去，说自己家里也等着米下锅。Anna Bauer的脸沉下来，说认识这么多年，连个名字都不肯签。Josef没有解释，只把桌上的茶钱付了；Anna Bauer站起来时把椅子带倒，也没有扶。
  _effects: cash -1 (借据上的第二个名字); mood -0.10999999999999999; flag +refused_to_vouch; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006310:child_

- **1957.04** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Hermann Berg用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; flag +parish_registered_

- **1957.06** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  Josef把账本摊在灯下，一页一页念出日期和克朗数，念到买纸那笔时有人 From 门口咳嗽。Anton Bauer低头拨弄围巾穗子，没有替谁说话。桌子上的旧墨水瓶被碰倒，蓝墨慢慢洇进木纹。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54; reputation 0.04000000000000001; skill_

- **1957.07** `r2-law.mortgage-foreclosure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sold-to-grain-merchant` tags: finance, mortgage, auction, housing, court, vienna, urban, postwar
  槌子落在第七号，Josef的院子归了一个外地来的粮商。Elisabeth Huber 2让Josef在十五天内搬走，Helene Stein 2把清单念到“灶台一座”时停了一下。Franz Lechner把自家的板车推来，说先装要紧的，家具以后再说。
  _effects: cash -10 (拍卖槌落在第七号); condition; flag +home_sold_at_auction; role; knowledge “the auction buyer is a grain merchant from outside the distr”_

  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (背弟妹走了一下午，胳膊酸痛); skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000520:child_

- **1918.07** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  医院走廊里全是湿鞋印。Alois Lechner把Rosa放在长椅上，自己去窗口交钱，六克朗换来一张薄薄的收据。护士把Rosa领进观察室，白灯照得人睁不开眼。Rosa抓着Alois Lechner的袖口不放，直到药水推进胳膊才松手。
  _effects: cash -6 (医院急诊押金和电车票); health 0.07999999999999999 (在医院接受处理); mood -0.04_

- **1918.11** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Rosa一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Alois Lechner听见哭声跑出来，把Rosa抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Rosa哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.04000000000000001 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Alois Lechner家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Rosa递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Alois Lechner端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Rosa把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Rosa把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Rosa踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Rosa单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Rosa Hofer在绳子那头喊。Rosa踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Rosa Hofer数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Rosa把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-11; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1921.08** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/late-for-school` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors, injury
  水桶从一楼提到四楼，Rosa在每层平台歇一次，桶底磕在台阶上，水溅出来，顺着板缝往下滴。煤球筐压在另一只手上，膝盖弯下去就发软。到Leopoldstädter Volksschule时校门已经关了半扇，老师把Rosa的名字写在迟到簿上，让站在走廊尽头。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (提水抬煤累得手臂发酸); reputation -0.03; mood -0.04_

- **1921.10** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Rosa想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Ferdinand Winter听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Rosa试着站起来，被Ferdinand Winter按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-shelter-missed-river` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  巷口的喊声第二次传来时，Rosa正用膝盖压住木盆边沿，给Elisabeth Lechner挡穿堂风。Elisabeth Lechner在旧布底下翻了个身，Rosa立刻停住，等呼吸又匀了才敢动。Alois Lechner推门进来，先看见盆里睡着的Elisabeth Lechner，再看见Rosa冻红的脚背，把灶上留的汤热了热。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000004; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.04** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-ok` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场里的菜摊一字排开，Rosa把十五克鲁泽排在掌心，先买了土豆，又去盐摊上要了一小包。摊主用旧报纸把盐裹好，Rosa把它塞进围裙内袋，土豆兜在身前。回家时Alois Lechner接过铜板一枚枚数，土豆一颗没少。
  _effects: food; cash -0.15 (市场花销); relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1922.07** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-confiscated` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书页在桌肚里翻动时，Luise Kral已经站在旁边。Rosa的手还按在算术本上，书被抽走，书脊在讲台边磕了一声。Luise Kral看了一眼折角，说“放学留下”，又拉开抽屉把书放进去。Rosa盯着抽屉缝，整节课没听进去。
  _effects: mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Rosa拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Maria Kral弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Rosa接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

- **1923.12** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1924.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1924.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.sibling-departure/take-over-bed-and-ledger` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  Mitzi Lechner走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Rosa把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Rosa坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; skill; mood -0.05000000000000002; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1924.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Rosa熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/memorized-wrong-step` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把老师讲的例题步骤全背下来，却没弄懂为什么移项。卷子发下来，第一道大题就卡住，Rosa在草稿纸上画满小人，交卷前只写出一个答案。回家后Rosa把那张卷子折成纸飞机，扔出窗外，又下楼捡了回来。纸飞机摔在煤堆上。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; skill_

- **1925.03** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reciprocated` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把回信写在电车月票背面：星期三，六点，第二站。Rosa提前半小时到，站在报亭旁边，把那张月票看了十几遍。Lena从电车上下来时，围巾被风吹到脸上，两个人都笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated; relation partner with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1925.06** `r3-teen.sexual-rumor::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-school-paint-order/denied` tags: rumor, shame, school, reputation, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  墙上出现了一行关于Rosa的脏话，Luise Kral让全班提水刷墙，刷了三遍还能看见白印。Maria Bauer在背后学那句话的腔调，Rosa回头时，他正低头抠桌角的漆。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; condition; flag +target_of_sexual_rumor; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1925.07** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Rosa在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Rosa用牙咬断，重新来。Alois Lechner坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Rosa把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1925.08** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Berta Stein把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Rosa看期限、工钱和出师考试。Rosa签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:65; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1926.01** `r3-teen.police-caution::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-parent-notice/cautioned` tags: police, caution, record, family, work, reform, medical, schooling
  Helene Stein把Rosa的名字写进一本硬皮簿子，问一句写一句，最后让他按手印。Alois Lechner赶来时，Helene Stein说带回去好好管教，这次不立案。走出门口，Alois Lechner的手一直攥着Rosa的袖子。
  _effects: reputation -0.04000000000000001; flag +police_caution_recorded; relation guardian with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the police opened a caution file in the subject’s name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Rosa又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-improved` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Berta Lechner把算术本摊在厨房桌上，Rosa用豆子摆出十以内的加减。Berta Lechner算错时，Rosa不骂，只把豆子重新排一遍。两个月后，Berta Lechner的听写和算术都及格了，Alois Lechner在饭桌上多给Rosa盛了半碗汤。
  _effects: flag +tutored_sibling_well; skill; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child_

- **1926.02** `r3-teen.parent-remarriage::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/accepts` tags: family, remarriage, stepfamily, grief, household, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Kral把漏雨的屋檐修好，又在门牌上添了自己的姓。Rosa没有叫他父亲，只在他修窗户时递钉子。晚饭桌上，Maria Kral把最大的一块肉夹给Rosa，Alois Lechner低头笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.04000000000000001; flag +accepted_parent_remarriage; relation guardian with person-maria-kral_

- **1926.04** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Luise Kral把成绩单按在讲台上，说Rosa还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Rosa签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; education apprenticeship_

- **1926.12** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Rosa把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Alois Lechner在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Rosa躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:89; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1927.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/refuse-the-machine` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Rosa看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Rosa是不是不想出师了。Rosa没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Ferdinand Huber接手。
  _effects: relation employer with person-franz-lechner; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 25; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Rosa又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 25 -> 45; skill; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/route-memorised` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa把去车站的路又走了一遍，记住了哪一段有煤气灯、哪一段住着巡夜的警察。回家后，Rosa在旧日历背面画了一条线，标出换乘的站名；那条线短得可笑，却比课本上的任何地图都清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 15 -> 27; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 13 -> 15; mood 0.010000000000000002_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/paid-night-class` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Rosa把出生证明和小学毕业证摊在桌上，Luise Moser数了数克朗，收下第一学期学费，在课程表夜班栏画了个勾。每周三晚六点到九点，教室在二楼，凳子要自己带。
  _effects: cash -6 (职业夜校第一期学费); education apprenticeship; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1927.03** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/pass-it-on` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Kral把一本拉丁文课本推到Rosa面前，说第三十四页的译文错了。Rosa翻到那一页，夹在里面的是一张油印纸，写着面粉配给和工厂检查的数字。放学后，Rosa把纸抄了一遍，塞进Josef的历史作业本；那天晚上，Rosa第一次觉得自己住的街区有一张看不见的地图。
  _effects: knowledge “配给数字与官方公告并不一致”; flag +yf18_read_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; mood 0.03_

  补考那天，Rosa在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Luise Kral跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Rosa去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-luise-kral; education apprenticeship_

  Mitzi Lechner走后，靠墙那张床空了出来，Rosa把自己的铺盖搬过去，也接过那本记着欠账的旧账本：面包店三克朗、煤铺一克朗八十赫勒。晚上Rosa坐在床沿一笔笔抄清，第一次知道家里的钱是怎么缺的。
  _effects: relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; skill; mood -0.05; cash -0.5 (替兄姐补齐欠煤铺的零头)_

- **1927.07** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Rosa把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Rosa没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。

  Alois Lechner把包袱放在桌上，问Rosa是不是打算走。Rosa说是，又说不出为什么；Alois Lechner没有打，只把包袱里的衬衫一件件叠好，说格拉茨的冬天比维也纳冷。那天夜里，Rosa听见Alois Lechner在厨房里数钱，数到很晚。

  Rosa又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 45 -> 65; skill; mood 0.02_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:yf18-runaway-plan/three-months-of-preparation/bundle-under-the-bed` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  Rosa把包袱重新塞回床底，又在上面压了一双冬鞋。母亲进来换床单时，Rosa站在门口，假装在看墙上的圣像；床单抖开的声音像一列很慢的火车，Rosa数着它经过的每一节车厢。

  _effects: cash 8 (管账的月钱); skill; mood -0.039999999999999994; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  _effects: cash -1 (请师兄喝一杯啤酒); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Rosa在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Rosa的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  Rosa在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Anna Weiss第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Alois Huber发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。

  Rosa被叫到学校，老师说Katharina Winter这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Rosa没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.confront-rumour::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::rumour-crisis/denounced-name` tags: task, social, rumor, reputation, conflict, vienna, urban, crisis
  洗衣房里的人压低声音说，Mitzi Bauer 2已经把Rosa的名字写进了门房的本子，说Rosa替罢工的人送过饭。Rosa没有吵，只问本子现在在谁手里。Elisabeth Lechner把最后一件衣服拧干，说昨晚看见本子被送进了分局。
  _effects: reputation -0.12; condition; flag +named_in_porter_book; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; knowledge “the porter’s book was taken to the district police office”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69_

- **1936.02** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/shifts-with-sibling` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money, injury
  Rosa和Mitzi Lechner把值班时辰写在门后的粉笔板上：Mitzi Lechner守前半夜，Rosa守后半夜，医生说静养二十一天。煤油灯芯拧小，药粉分成小包摆在床头，便盆换水时尽量不碰出响。二十一天熬下来，Rosa的眼皮肿了，走路发飘，但Alois Lechner每次起身总有人扶。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (连续夜里守夜睡眠不足); relation kin with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +night_care_duty; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81_

- **1936.04** `r2-law.bank-run::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/moved-to-credit-union` tags: finance, bank, panic, crisis, savings, vienna, urban, war
  Rosa没有再排队，拿着存折去了街对面的信用合作社。柜台后的老会计把利率算在纸角上，说转过来只能按八成兑。Luise Weiss 3跟着出来，站在两家门之间，最后把存折撕成两半，一半塞给Rosa。
  _effects: cash -6 (铁栅门后的第三排); flag +moved_savings_to_credit_union; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; knowledge “the credit union discounts transferred savings by twenty per”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

- **1936.11** `r2-rural.winter-isolation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/dug-out-the-road` tags: rural, winter, isolation, health, neighbor, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa和Emil Winter 3扛着木锹去挖路，雪没到腰，挖一锹塌一锹。到中午，两人从村口挖到教堂，手指冻得握不住锹柄。Maria Kral的雪橇终于进来时，车上坐着两个发烧的孩子。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (雪封住的村路); flag +dug_out_village_road; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91; relation colleague with person-maria-kral; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-91_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.050000000000000044 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004624_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Rosa的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Rosa家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006848_

- **1937.01** `r14-edu.sibling-exam::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sibling-failed` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, exam, vienna, urban, crisis, war
  Berta Lechner差三分。回家路上，Berta Lechner一句话也没说，进了门就把自己关在屋里。Rosa把饭菜放在门口，敲了两下，没有回应。半夜，Rosa听见屋里有人在翻书，翻到天亮。第二天清早，Berta Lechner出来洗脸，眼睛肿着，问Rosa能不能再教一年。补考费一克朗。
  _effects: cash -1 (弟弟妹妹的准考证); flag +sibling_failed_exam; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004375_

- **1937.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Rosa Moser 2在门口堵了三天，第四天把Rosa的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Rosa把办公室的钥匙交给Ferdinand Huber，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 助理文书 @taxi:person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-54-evt:world-vienna-1912:003406; reputation -0.07999999999999999; mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +resigned_over_scandal; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Rosa家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 3; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +windfall_went_public_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007088_

- **1937.01** `r3-crime.burglary-lookout::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::after-the-employment-office/coughed-a-warning` tags: crime, tenement, police, fear, complicity, vienna, urban, crisis
  楼梯口的灯坏了三层，Rosa抱着伞站在阴影里，听见楼上有人挪动柜子。巷口传来脚步，Rosa咳了一声，Ferdinand Huber从后窗翻下去。Katharina Lechner开门倒水，只看见Rosa在系鞋带，说这么晚了还修伞。
  _effects: flag +lookout_warned_burglar; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “the burglar left through the back window”_

  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Rosa把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 0 -> 0; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.01** `r13-edu.study-group::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/both-pass-exam` tags: education, study_group, mutual_aid, politics, belonging, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa和Anna Bauer在阁楼里支起一块旧门板当桌子，每周三晚上互相考问。Anna Bauer把容易错的公式写在烟盒背面，Rosa负责讲解历史背景。考试结果出来，两个人都过了，Anna Bauer把烟盒送给Rosa作纪念，说以后别再弄丢。烟盒被Rosa放在书架上，一直没拆。
  _effects: flag +study_group_passed; skill; relation colleague with person-anna-bauer_

- **1937.03** `bc18-crime.inept-blackmail::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/mourning-card` tags: crime, blackmail, photograph, mistake, humiliation, absurd, reputation, vienna
  Luise Weiss 3在葬礼上偷拍了一张Rosa靠着棺材睡着的照片，说只要不给四克朗，就把照片寄给Rosa的老板。Rosa还没有凑钱，照片先被家里人选去做了悼念卡；亲戚们说，只有真正伤心的人才会累成这样。Luise Weiss 3拿着底片站在门口，第一次不知道该威胁谁。
  _effects: cash 3 (相馆支付的悼念卡照片使用费); reputation 0.030000000000000027; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; condition; flag +blackmail_photo_became_mourning_card; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1937.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa Kral嫌Rosa的床单滴水滴到Rosa Kral家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Rosa下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Rosa把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Rosa Kral那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1938.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Ferdinand Huber在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Rosa排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1938.01** `r15-work.first-commission::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/paid-in-full` tags: work, craft, journeyman, commerce, reputation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Berta Winter 4来取货，把工钱一张张数在桌上。Rosa第一次没有经过师傅的手接过钱。晚上收工后，Rosa把工具箱擦了一遍，又把订单簿翻到新的一页。
  _effects: cash 4 (第一笔自己的订单); mood 0.05999999999999994; reputation 0.04999999999999999; flag +first_independent_commission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1938.01** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/wrong-coal-delivery` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Maria Stein把二十袋“劣质煤”送到Rosa门口，货到付款，想看Rosa拿不出钱。卸货时，煤块黑得发亮，是上好的无烟煤；Rosa付了钱，转手卖给面包房，净赚五克朗。Maria Stein来打听煤的去向，Rosa说已经烧了，还谢谢他记得自己怕冷。
  _effects: cash 5 (转卖无烟煤的差价); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104; condition; flag +enemy_coal_was_anthracite; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

- **1939.01** `ml18-tax.audit-of-the-windfall::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hide-the-ledger` tags: tax, legal, money, windfall, bureaucracy, comic, vienna, urban
  Berta Stein把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Gustav Berg第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Rosa看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。
  _effects: cash -10 (the accountant's fee for hiding the ledger); state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:491; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +tax_ledger_hidden; knowledge “黑皮本上被红笔圈出的三笔利息都没有报税。”; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1939.01** `hist15-occ.seed-occupation::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-flag-changes` tags: occupation, war, curfew, history, place, institution, vienna, urban
  城门口的旗换了颜色，岗哨开始检查每一张纸。Rosa回家时发现街角新钉了一块告示牌：天黑以后不许出门，地窖里的粮食要登记。晚上，巡逻队的靴声从窗下经过，比钟声还准。
  _effects: state_add occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:492; mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +saw_the_occupation_flag; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

  Rosa蹲在门口把Anton Winter的书包带系紧，又把一块面包塞进侧袋。学校里挤了七十多个孩子，Lena按名字点名，点到Anton Winter时抬头看了一眼。Rosa在走廊外等到铃响，听见里面开始念字母，才把攥皱的报名纸收进口袋。

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 26 -> 29; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.09** `statistical_infection`
  Rosa在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1939.12** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Rosa的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Rosa家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008071_

- **1939.12** `r1-fam.infant-illness::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/illness.doctor` tags: family, infancy, illness, night, debt, vienna, urban, crisis
  Otto Kral 3来的时候，孩子嘴唇已经发青。他从小包里取出一只小罐，往热水里倒了几滴，屋里立刻全是刺鼻的味道。孩子哭了出来，Otto Kral 3说这一声哭值五克朗；Ferdinand Weiss去邻居家借钱，Rosa抱着孩子，一直抱到中午。
  _effects: cash -5 (蒸汽壶和夜灯); flag +doctor_saved_infant; role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; knowledge “医生的小包里带着一小瓶樟脑。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101_

- **1939.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.stopped-clock/stopped-clock.wire-fix` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, clock, time
  Rosa把钟背板卸下来，发现一根发条断了。她从缝纫盒里找出一截细铁丝，把断头缠住，钟摆晃了几下又走了起来。走是走，每天慢一刻钟，她索性把指针往前拨了拨，凑合着看。
  _effects: skill; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1939.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Rosa的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1940.03** `state_reaction:occupied-city-regime/liberation-ends-occupation` tags: occupied-city-regime, occupation, war, repression, curfew, requisition, shortage, place
  城里的旗子又换了一次。Rosa站在街口，看见第一批士兵把岗哨撤走；没有人解释接下来会怎样，但通行证不再需要每天重签了。
  _effects: state_remove occupied-city-regime state:world-vienna-1912:492; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.03** `bc18-crime.failed-burglary::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/right-office-wrong-drawer` tags: crime, burglary, mistake, absurd, police, legal, comic, vienna
  他们终于摸到公证处的后窗，撬开的抽屉里却没有遗嘱，只有一叠慈善捐款收据，数目和报纸上公布的对不上。Rosa把收据塞进外套，找到一位律师，换回七克朗；律师说，这不是盗窃，是取证。Berta Winter 4问那遗嘱怎么办，Rosa说，先让死人再等一等。
  _effects: cash 7 (把慈善收据交给律师的酬劳); knowledge “公证处抽屉里的慈善捐款收据与报纸公布的数目对不上。”; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; condition; flag +stole_charity_receipts; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa熬过了这场肺结核，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1940.05** `r3-crime.victim-family::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::military-court-jurisdiction/case-dropped` tags: crime, family, grief, court, settlement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Huber第二次来，没有进门，只站在台阶上说证据不够。Berta Lechner问“那天的血衣呢”，Ferdinand Huber说“在证物室，但编号对不上”。灵堂里的蜡烛烧到了底，Hermann Moser把状纸收起来，折成四折，塞进供桌下面。
  _effects: mood -0.30000000000000004; condition; flag +case_dropped_for_lack_of_evidence; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; knowledge “the bloodstained coat lost its evidence number”_

  Mitzi Bauer 2的收音机只发出炒豆子一样的杂音。Rosa把后板拆开，发现一根接地线被老鼠咬断，焊点冷得像一颗坏牙。修好以后，Mitzi Bauer 2留Rosa听完了整点新闻；出门时，Rosa在巷口还听见那台机器在响。

  旅馆老板Berta Berg把一张订单摊在刨床上：四十张床架，月底交货。Rosa算了一遍工时，按现在的做法只能保本；第二天，Rosa把Elisabeth Bauer的午饭时间缩短，又把工钱改成按件计。Elisabeth Bauer没有反对，只在木料上多画了一道线。

  Rosa傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Berta Berg在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Berta Berg敲开Rosa的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-96; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1941.09** `kernel-social.misunderstanding-apology::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/misunderstanding.jam-and-apology` tags: social, misunderstanding, apology, friendship, neighbor, reputation, vienna, urban
  话是从洗衣房传出来的：有人说Rosa笑过Gustav Bauer 2死去的孩子。Rosa提着一罐李子酱上楼，在门口把事情从头说了一遍，Gustav Bauer 2听完把酱收下，又拿出两只杯子。两人坐到天黑，谁也没再提那句话，只商量明天的煤该谁去买。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107; role; cash -1 (a jar of plum jam for the apology); flag +friendship_repaired; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-107_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Winter这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Rosa的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Rosa家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 3; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Otto Kral 3先把Rosa抽屉里的往来信件搬空，再在会议上说Rosa从来没有保存过记录。没有人能证明那些信存在过，除了Rosa自己。散会后，有人让Rosa回家休息几天，工资照发。Rosa知道这不是照顾，是让Rosa别再回来。
  _effects: reputation -0.06; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-101; knowledge “我经手的信件在会议前被人搬空了。”; condition; flag +records_removed_

  一年过去，Rosa已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Rosa也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。

  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Rosa把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Rosa来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 3 -> 4; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Rosa从一九〇四年的画报上抄下一段描写雨夜的话，把地名和人名换掉，寄给一家征文比赛。抄的时候，Rosa告诉自己只借这一小段；寄出去之后，却在邮局门口站了很久，反复回想那段话里有没有一个词是别人一眼就能认出来的。
  _effects: state_add fa17-plagiarist state:world-vienna-1912:609; skill; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +borrowed_paragraph; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1942.09** `sm18-neighbor.night-light::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/watches-until-the-blind-moves` tags: neighbor, secret, night, window, suspicion, everyday, vienna, urban
  Rosa连续三夜看见Berta Winter 4家的地下室亮着灯，窗纸上有人影来回走，却从不见人从前门出来。第四夜，Rosa提着煤桶假装去地窖，听见里面有另一个人的咳嗽声。
  _effects: state_add sm18-town-secret state:world-vienna-1912:610; knowledge “邻居家的地下室藏着一个没有登记的人，夜里有人影和咳嗽声”; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +saw_the_neighbors_cellar_light; flag +carries_a_neighborhood_secret_

  Alois Huber把借据上的克朗划掉，改成美元，说“纸天天在跌”。Alois Lechner把怀表押在桌上，又添了一枚戒指。Rosa看见年息从八厘涨到一分二，Alois Huber说“嫌贵可以不签”。父亲先签了。
  _effects: cash -18 (学费借据的背面); flag +tuition_note_in_foreign_currency; role; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; knowledge “the note is payable in dollars at twelve percent”_

  Berta Lechner在城里站稳了脚，托人带来一个信封，里面是八克朗和一张夜校报名单。信上只写：当年你教我算术，现在轮到我付学费。Rosa把报名单看了三遍，填好，第二天一早就去交了。
  _effects: cash 8 (多年后的一封家信); flag +sibling_repaid_tutoring; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child_

  Mitzi Weiss带了两只空箱子来，走的时候还是空的。Rosa把三年的合同、收据和会议记录按月份排在地上，Mitzi Weiss抽查了十二份，每一份都能对上签字人；最后一句是“下次可以少准备一点”。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +passed_audit; knowledge “审计抽查了十二份合同，每一份都能对上签字人。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

- **1943.10** `r1-fam.neighbor-solidarity::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::solidarity-war/solidarity.coal-fire` tags: social, neighbor, fire, solidarity, disaster, vienna, urban, crisis
  战时的煤配给不够，有人在走廊里用木柴生火，火星引着了堆在楼梯下的旧报纸。Rosa提着水桶冲上去时，Rosa Kral 2正把一袋配给面粉往窗外递。火灭后，两家人的眉毛都燎焦了；Rosa Kral 2看着那袋面粉，说这袋面够吃到月底。

  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Rosa的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Rosa家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011086_

- **1944.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/the-papers-hear-about-it` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  报纸的社会版把Rosa家的门牌号和那笔钱的数目印在一起，标题是“一笔不该有的好运”。第二天门口多了两个陌生人，一个说是远亲，一个说代表一家孤儿院。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.public false -> true; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 2 -> 4; reputation -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:011156_

- **1944.01** `ml18-family.school-gate-car::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-child-leaves-home` tags: family, money, child, school, reputation, dark, vienna, urban
  第二个学期，Katharina Weiss的成绩单上多了两个不及格，家里的争吵从钱转到衣服、朋友和马车。一天早晨，Rosa在餐桌上找到一张字条：Katharina Weiss去了乡下姨妈家，末尾写着“我更喜欢我们没钱的时候”。Lena来家里送作业本，站在门口没有进来。
  _effects: cash -10 (travel and expenses after the child left); state_parameter raising-child.leftHome false -> true; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 65 -> 50; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005946:child; condition; flag +child_left_home; knowledge “字条上写着孩子更喜欢家里没钱的时候。”; mood -0.12_

  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Rosa把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 70 -> 85; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 52 -> 49; cash -1 (inspection fine); mood -0.040000000000000036_

  一位中学教师在读者来信里引用了同一段话，注明出处是十年前的《星期日报》。编辑把两段并排放在文化版上，问Rosa是否愿意解释；Rosa盯着那两栏字，发现连标点都几乎没有改。
  _effects: state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.exposure 22 -> 34; state_parameter fa17-plagiarist.guilt 33 -> 41; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/held-behind-the-teeth` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  半年里，Rosa在楼梯口三次遇见那件不该提的事，每次都把话咽回去。秘密没有变轻，只是被放到了一个更深的抽屉里；Rosa开始记得自己每一次沉默是在哪一层楼梯。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 32 -> 29; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 57 -> 59; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1944.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-slips-away` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  钱在一年里薄了一层：屋顶要修，姐夫来借，彩票站的老主顾说有个稳赚的买卖。Rosa把账本翻到最后一页，发现写下的支出比记得的多出四克朗；窗外有人在敲铁皮，声音像在催账。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 18 -> 13; state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.spent 0 -> 5; cash -1 (the account does not balance)_

- **1944.03** `hs18-radio.rumor::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turn-it-up` tags: radio, blackout, rumor, panic, neighbor, politics, vienna, urban
  停电后的第三个小时，Mitzi Stein把收音机搬到楼道，电池快没电了。女播音员说“请市民保持镇静”，随后是一段军乐。Rosa听见有人开始收拾行李；楼下的铁门被风撞了三次，像有人在敲。
  _effects: state_add hs18-blackout-rumor state:world-vienna-1912:705; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +heard_radio_rumor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-119_

- **1944.04** `ml18-charity.neighborhood-clinic::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/the-family-blocks-the-donation` tags: charity, money, clinic, reputation, community, warm, vienna, urban
  捐款的事刚说出口，家里的兄弟先来了。他说这笔钱该留给自家人，不该拿去给街上的孩子看病。Alois Huber在诊所门口等了半小时，最后把图纸卷起来；Rosa站在空铺面里，听见隔壁肉铺的锯骨声一下一下传过来。
  _effects: cash -2 (the notary's fee for the abandoned clinic deed); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95; flag +family_blocked_clinic; knowledge “诊所的铺面已经租下，但家里的兄弟不同意把横财捐出去。”; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 4 -> 6; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 55 -> 52; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1945.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Rosa是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Rosa在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 41; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 55 -> 57; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Rosa天没亮就把Katharina Weiss裹好，抱到楼下Berta Weiss家。Berta Weiss接过孩子，先摸了摸尿布，又指给Rosa看灶上温着的米汤。Rosa把一星期的工钱数出一份放在柜上，Berta Weiss数也没数就收进围裙；出门时，Rosa听见孩子在屋里哭了一声，又停了。
  _effects: cash -2 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +arranged_daily_childcare; relation acquaintance with person-berta-weiss; knowledge “the minder keeps a pot of rice water on the stove”_

  Rosa把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Rosa的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000018 (moving in winter)_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Rosa当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 52 -> 62; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 1 -> 2; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home); mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012424_

- **1946.01** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/resigned` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  Katharina Lechner在门口堵了三天，第四天把Rosa的辞职信全文登了出来。信只有四行，最后一行是“为避免机构继续为我的判断付出代价”。Rosa把办公室的钥匙交给Mitzi Weiss，没有带走任何文件。
  _effects: position_end 无线电修理匠 @radio:person-phase15-Rosa-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:008762; reputation -0.08000000000000002; knowledge “辞职信被全文刊登，接任者当天就搬进了办公室。”_

- **1946.01** `state_reaction:sm18-town-secret/scandal-touches-it` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  街口有人家出了丑事，围观的人转到这条巷子时，Rosa发现自己也站在门口。Rosa没有说任何名字，但回家以后，还是把窗帘拉得比平时早。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 26 -> 38; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 25 -> 30_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012502_

  Rosa把Katharina Winter的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Winter今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 47 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 66 -> 63; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  账本合上时，Rosa又多出三克朗：少算的工时、扣下的学徒午饭、一批没报税的麻布。车间里没人抬头，机器声盖住了算盘声。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 50 -> 55; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 23 -> 21; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 44 -> 46; cash 2 (workshop profit)_

  Rosa的「借来的句子」到期；相关安排结束。

- **1946.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/held-behind-the-teeth` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  半年里，Rosa在楼梯口三次遇见那件不该提的事，每次都把话咽回去。秘密没有变轻，只是被放到了一个更深的抽屉里；Rosa开始记得自己每一次沉默是在哪一层楼梯。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 38 -> 35; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 61 -> 63; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1946.01** `state_expire:ld18-public-desire` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety

  _effects: state_remove ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:704; flag -public_desire_active_

- **1946.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/soup-and-grave` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  饭桌摆在圣器室旁边，椅子不够，两人挨着坐。Mitzi说到欠账时声音低下去，Rosa没有接话，只把盐罐递过去；出门时天已经擦黑，墓地的铁门还开着。
  _effects: cash -0.5 (墓前的蜡烛); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1946.06** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.share` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼还滴着水，Rosa在走廊里分成两份。一份拿进自家，一份搁在Maria Bauer的门口。傍晚Maria Bauer来还盘子，盘子里是一小袋面粉。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鱼); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1946.10** `r3-mig.ship-passage::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/steerage-in-a-storm` tags: migration, ship, steerage, travel, health, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa的铺位在舱口下面，白天盖板一关，空气里全是煤灰和呕吐物的味道。第三天起风，船横着走了半天，Otto Weiss 3把一桶海水泼在甲板上，说“吐完就好了”。Rosa抓着床架，听见螺旋桨露出水面的声音。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (甲板下的煤灰); condition; flag +steerage_in_storm; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; knowledge “the steerage hatch is closed in heavy weather”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1946.10** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Rosa让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 34 -> 44; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 52 -> 50; cash -2 (worker compensation)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:012504_

- **1946.12** `ld18-wedding.private-rite::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-landlady-applauds` tags: love, wedding, marriage, queer, secret, ritual, commitment, secret_intimacy
  仪式进行到一半，Katharina Berg推门进来，以为她们在试婚纱。她坚持要喝一杯，还说明天可以借出院子里的葡萄架再办一次。Rosa和Katharina Lechner面面相觑，最后真的在葡萄架下又交换了一次戒指，这次有六个人围观。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:759; state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:760; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

  Rosa没有给Katharina Weiss报名。家里需要一双能看摊的手，学校也还欠着上一学期的纸笔钱；晚上Katharina Weiss把别人的课本借来看，Rosa假装没有看见。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 40 -> 55; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 38 -> 8; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 50 -> 40; state_parameter raising-child.careDebt 2 -> 6; state_parameter raising-child.schoolEnrolled true -> false; state_parameter raising-child.leftHome true -> false; cash 1 (the child's small earnings at the stall); flag +child_kept_from_school_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Katharina Weiss这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 37 -> 40; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa本来只想说一句“那家人最近不太容易”，话到嘴边却带出了地下室。听的人没有追问，只把杯子放下；第二天，楼道里多了几道从门口扫到楼梯口的目光。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 35 -> 42; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 30 -> 38; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Rosa把工钱摊在桌上，先数出房租和煤钱，再把剩下的换成一张汇款单。邮局职员在单子上敲了章，说“两周能到”；回家的路上，他买了一张最便宜的明信片，写了两行字又划掉一行。
  _effects: cash -2 (the money order and postage); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 6 -> 8; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 1; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 62 -> 59; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 25 -> 31; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 35 -> 32; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Rosa，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Rosa没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 35 -> 43; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 10 -> 14; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1947.01** `r13-fam.foster-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::foster-postwar/orphanage-full` tags: task, care, foster, child, household, orphan, vienna, urban
  孤儿院的院长说孩子太多，床不够，让Rosa再等一个月。Alois Winter把Katharina 2领回临时收容所，Rosa每周去看一次。孩子渐渐肯说话了，却总是在Rosa起身时抓住衣角，问下一次什么时候来。
  _effects: cash -2 (门口多出来的一双小鞋); condition; flag +waited_for_foster_placement; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:010600:child_

- **1947.02** `r1-work.porter-luggage::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/found-the-missing-case` tags: work, porter, station, luggage, accusation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Katharina Lechner说箱子是棕色的，Rosa在行李房里一排排看号牌，最后在最底层找到一只被压歪的箱子。锁扣开了，里面的银餐具还在。Katharina Lechner把两克朗塞进Rosa手里，Mitzi Weiss在门口记下时间，笔尖在纸上停了一停。
  _effects: cash 2 (行李房里的十七号牌); flag +found_missing_luggage; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000520:child_

- **1947.02** `r2-law.pawn-ticket::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/redeemed-before-sale` tags: finance, pawnshop, debt, household, loss, vienna, urban, postwar
  Berta Lechner把当票对着账本看了两遍，说昨天刚过期，今天还能赎，但要加一个月的保管费。Rosa把工钱和借来的钱一起推过柜台，Berta Lechner从后架取下那只银表，表盖上的划痕还在。Luise Kral 2在门口说，再晚一天就上拍卖台了。
  _effects: cash -12 (过期的当票); flag +redeemed_pawned_watch_before_auction; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child; knowledge “the pawnbroker charges one month’s storage after the ticket ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, injury
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Rosa一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Berta Lechner送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); health -0.030000000000000027 (crowded sleeping space); condition; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child_

- **1948.01** `sm18-secret.protect-or-tell::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/lets-them-pass` tags: secret, neighbor, stairwell, choice, risk, consequence, vienna, urban
  Rosa在楼梯间撞见第三个人：那人提着一只旧皮箱，见到Rosa就把帽檐压低。Emil Huber 2从门后出来，手还按在门把上。三个人站了几秒钟，谁也没有先说话；最后Rosa侧过身，让他们过去。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 42 -> 52; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.danger 38 -> 43; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 63 -> 68; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-100; mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +saw_the_third_person_on_the_stairs_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/learns` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Rosa把Katharina Winter的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Katharina Winter今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Rosa听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 58 -> 66; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 63 -> 60; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wc17-sweatshop-owner/ledger-year/inspection-week` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  检查员在楼梯口闻到了煤烟和汗味，数了数窗边的床位。Rosa把罚单折进账本，转身催工头把剩下的活赶完；这一批货的利润刚好抵掉罚款。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.inspectionRisk 100 -> 100; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 48 -> 45; cash -1 (inspection fine); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/held-behind-the-teeth` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  半年里，Rosa在楼梯口三次遇见那件不该提的事，每次都把话咽回去。秘密没有变轻，只是被放到了一个更深的抽屉里；Rosa开始记得自己每一次沉默是在哪一层楼梯。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 52 -> 49; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 68 -> 70; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 31 -> 37; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 32 -> 29; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-new-version` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  故事又换了一个版本：有人说Rosa是为了钱，有人说是为了爱，还有人说她只是喜欢看别人议论。Rosa在咖啡馆听见第三种说法，没有更正，只把杯子往窗边挪了挪；阳光照进来，她第一次觉得这些版本都离自己很远。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 43 -> 39; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.spectacle 25 -> 27; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.01** `task-vienna-family-care.inheritance-guardianship/objection-in-office` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  念到房子一段，Elisabeth Lechner抬手说不同意，理由是他这些年也往家里寄过钱。公证人Rosa把文件收回抽屉，说明需要双方再谈一次，约到下周三。走出门时Rosa先说了话，对方没有回，两人在石阶上一左一右地走开。
  _effects: relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1948.04** `state_reaction:wc17-sweatshop-owner/blood-on-the-cloth` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  一个学徒的手指被卷进皮带，血滴在麻布上。Rosa让人把她送去诊所，又吩咐把染红的布裁掉；晚上算账时，赔偿金和那批布的钱写在同一页。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 46 -> 56; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 52 -> 50; cash -2 (worker compensation)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013574_

- **1948.06** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.overnight` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  Rosa点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Emil Weiss 2量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。
  _effects: cash 0.75 (交了一整捆衬衫领子的计件工钱); health -0.040000000000000036 (通宵缝纫，眼睛疲劳，手指扎伤); mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.08** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/wages-arrive` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  厂里补发了一笔拖欠的工钱。Rosa没有先还杂货铺的账，而是去邮局多填了一张汇款单；回来的路上，他给自己买了一支最便宜的烟，站在桥上看了一会儿水，才把剩下的钱数清楚。
  _effects: cash 1 (the factory paid part of the arrears); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.supportSent 8 -> 9; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 59 -> 57; mood 0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013782_

  Katharina Weiss放学后留在车间里数扣子，一只手指被针扎穿。Rosa抱起Katharina Weiss往诊所跑，路上想起自己十二岁那年也在同一排机器前站过；第二天，Rosa把Katharina Weiss送回了学校。
  _effects: cash -2 (clinic fees for the child); state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.guilt 56 -> 71; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.loyalty 21 -> 16; state_parameter wc17-sweatshop-owner.profit 50 -> 48; mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +child_injured; knowledge “孩子的手指被针扎穿，我十二岁时也站在同一排机器前。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-125_

  _effects: cash -4 (垫付首日面粉和柴火); food; reputation 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 66 -> 69; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.06** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Rosa说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Emil Weiss 2把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Emil Weiss 2在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; flag +parish_refused_aid_

- **1949.12** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 71 -> 66; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 55 -> 60; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1950.01** `state_expire:wc17-sweatshop-owner` tags: wc17-sweatshop-owner, work, factory, ownership, profit, guilt, labor, sweatshop
  Rosa的「血汗作坊主」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove wc17-sweatshop-owner state:world-vienna-1912:578; role; flag -sweatshop_owner_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:sm18-town-secret/the-secret-keeps/held-behind-the-teeth` tags: sm18-town-secret, secret, neighborhood, rumor, knowledge, risk, obligation, mystery
  半年里，Rosa在楼梯口三次遇见那件不该提的事，每次都把话咽回去。秘密没有变轻，只是被放到了一个更深的抽屉里；Rosa开始记得自己每一次沉默是在哪一层楼梯。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-town-secret.pressure 46 -> 43; state_parameter sm18-town-secret.proof 72 -> 74_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 43 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 26 -> 23_

- **1950.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Rosa把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Josef Winter的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Rosa就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Rosa把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。
  _effects: cash -2 (第三遍换湿布); health -0.030000000000000027 (第三遍换湿布); condition; flag +nursed_child_fever; health 0.05999999999999994 (the fever broke after a night of cold compresses); relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:010593:child_

- **1950.02** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz 2这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 44 -> 47; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015018_

- **1950.05** `vienna-adult-social.sunday-table/sunday-table.repair` tags: adulthood, social, work, family, partnership
  Ferdinand Weiss从柜子里拿出攒了三个月的零钱袋，倒在桌上，说先给Rosa买双能进厂的结实靴子。Rosa数出鞋匠要的数目，把剩下的又推回袋子中央。
  _effects: cash -3 (凑钱买了一双结实的工靴); relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1950.05** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-full` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  Rosa把床上的旧褥子重新铺平，Helene Stein放下自己的铺盖卷，从怀里掏出一把钥匙看了看。厨房里多了一口人的碗，灶边的煤省着烧也够。Franz Moser在门口看了一眼，没多话。
  _effects: cash 4 (出租空床收进的月租); household; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

- **1950.05** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Emil 2这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 70 -> 73; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015045_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Franz Winter这周主动擦了黑板。Rosa把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/family-emergency` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  故乡来了一封电报，说屋顶塌了一角，老人暂时住到邻居家。Rosa当晚就去邮局，把本来留着交学费的钱先汇了出去；回家后，他把墙上的缴费单取下来，折好放进抽屉。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 54 -> 64; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 0 -> 1; cash -3 (an emergency money order sent home)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015389_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Hermann Weiss。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Ferdinand Weiss把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Rosa躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:923_

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Rosa听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 15 -> 23; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 35 -> 32; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015576_

- **1951.01** `ld18-divorce.bench::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/decree-granted` tags: divorce, marriage, scandal, queer, law, public, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Helene Stein把两封信和一本旅馆登记簿并排放在桌上，问Rosa是否承认“长期与他人保持不正当关系”。Rosa说承认。法庭里有人咳嗽了一声；Ferdinand Weiss的律师要求精神赔偿，Helene Stein判了离婚，也允许Ferdinand Weiss保留婚前的公寓。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:924; reputation -0.07999999999999996; cash -3 (the lawyer and the settlement); flag +divorced_in_public; flag +public_desire_active_

- **1951.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-family-wedding` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  亲戚的婚礼上，Rosa被安排和{link:beloved}分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Rosa低头喝汤；{link:beloved}在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 49 -> 59; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 0 -> 4_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:015579_
