  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 50 -> 58; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 68 -> 65; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Rosa把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 1 -> 2; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 64 -> 68; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Rosa最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Rosa把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Rosa说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 59 -> 65; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 23 -> 20; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Rosa的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Rosa想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 32 -> 24; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 60; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1951.01** `task-vienna-family-care.reconcile-siblings/soup-and-grave` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  忌日那天，Rosa在教堂旁的饭桌上先给Franz Lechner盛了一碗汤，把分家时那只柜子的事提了出来，说完就不再辩解。Franz Lechner用勺子搅了两圈，讲了自己当时欠的账。饭吃到一半，两人一起去墓地，路上买了半克朗的蜡烛。
  _effects: relation kin with person-franz-lechner; cash -0.5 (墓前的蜡烛); mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1951.04** `r2-law.mortgage-foreclosure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/auction-stayed` tags: finance, mortgage, auction, housing, court, vienna, urban, postwar
  Rosa在拍卖前三天把欠款凑齐，Gustav Huber点完钞票，把槌子放回抽屉，说这次算“暂缓”，不是“撤销”。Mitzi Kraus在门口把公告撕下一角，留给Rosa做凭证；Mitzi Lechner问房子保住了没有，Rosa说保到下一次。
  _effects: cash -28 (拍卖槌落在第七号); flag +mortgage_arrears_paid; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001159:child; knowledge “the auction can be stayed but not cancelled”_

- **1951.11** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Ferdinand Bauer在午休时把本子摊在Rosa的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Rosa从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Ferdinand Bauer当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; reputation 0.03999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1952.01** `ld18-admirer.gift::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-admirer-is-useful` tags: admirer, gift, public_desire, jealousy, danger, secret_intimacy, vienna, urban
  Rosa 2在户籍处做事，能替人查旧档案。Rosa请他喝了一次咖啡，问起Berta Lechner失散多年的弟弟；三天后，一张写着地址的纸条送到后台。Rosa 2没有要钱，只说下次跳舞时，给他留一个靠前的位子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003746:child; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 5 -> 20; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 30 -> 35; knowledge “户籍处的这位爱慕者能查到旧档案里的地址”; flag +admirer_with_registry_access_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/a-price-for-silence` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  有人把一张账单寄到Rosa家，背面写着“封口费”，数目正好是她一个月的工钱。Rosa把钱数了两遍，最后还是装进信封；第二天，街上又有了新版本，比上一个更贵，也更难听。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 50 -> 62; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.protection 20 -> 12; cash -2 (the first payment to a blackmailer); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1952.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.invite-to-theatre/walk-instead-of-tickets` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  票在口袋里被手指捏出折痕，话到嘴边却改成了请对方沿多瑙运河走一段。两人从桥下走到水闸，买了两只热栗子，一共十六赫勒。剧院的事谁也没有提起，票根还留在外套内袋里。
  _effects: cash -0.32 (两只热栗子和来回电车); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000290:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.03** `r1-work.servant-household::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accused-of-taking-a-brooch` tags: work, service, household, dismissal, theft, vienna, urban, postwar
  夫人的胸针不见了，Otto Weiss 4说最后进房间的是Rosa。Rosa把围裙口袋翻出来，只有一把钥匙和半截蜡烛。Katharina Lechner没有报官，但让Rosa当天就走，工钱扣下抵那枚胸针。Rosa走到街角才发现，钥匙是厨房的，带不走。
  _effects: cash -6 (楼上的铃响了三次); reputation -0.10000000000000003; condition; flag +accused_of_servant_theft; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-157_

- **1952.04** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.quarrel` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  铁桶磕在石沿上，水晃出来打湿了Rosa的裙角。Elisabeth Huber 2的声音拔高，说轮到她就是轮到她。Rosa咬着嘴没再喊，把盆往自己门前拖，湿石板上一道水痕。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; reputation -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

  走到桥洞前，Luise Weiss 2说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Rosa站在原地看着Luise Weiss 2拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1953.11** `r13-rel.introduction-friend::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/talked-until-the-stove-died` tags: relationship, introduction, friend, acquaintance, social, vienna, urban, postwar
  Helene Stein把Emil Bauer领到桌边，说这是自己厂里最能熬夜的人。Rosa给Emil Bauer倒了半杯咖啡，Emil Bauer先问Rosa上哪个班。三人聊到炉子熄了，Helene Stein借口去买烟，把位置留给两个人。
  _effects: role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005915:child; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Rosa还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 2 -> 4; mood -0.040000000000000036_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017005_

  表格有四栏，第四栏要写“工种及所属行会”。Rosa问工友平时怎么称呼自己的活，工友靠在机器旁边说“就是上油”。办事员的印章是木柄的，按下去时咔的一声。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在衙门走廊站了大半天); relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005540:child; skill_

- **1954.01** `ld18-future.two-rooms::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/two-rooms-one-wall` tags: love, future, unregistered, commitment, ordinary, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Rosa和Mitzi在同一个院子里租下两间相邻的小房，中间只隔一道薄墙。晚上收工后，一个人敲三下，另一个就敲两下；谁也没有提婚书，但每个月的房租都各付一半，煤也买双份。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 69 -> 79; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.improvisation 70 -> 85; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 68 -> 63; cash -2 (two rents and double coal); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +two_rooms_one_wall; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  Rosa把同一只抽屉拉开又推上，里面的东西已经按原来的位置摆了三遍。楼下有人搬煤，铁锹刮过石阶；Rosa忽然想不起那件东西最后一次被谁拿在手里，只觉得每个人的脸都有一点可疑。
  _effects: state_parameter sm18-missing-object.suspicion 0 -> 8; state_parameter sm18-missing-object.leads 10 -> 8; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.06** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa和Rosa Weiss谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Rosa家，钥匙只在白天给。Rosa Weiss说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Luise Bauer听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Rosa把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002924:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Berta Stein拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1955.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Rosa把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Berta Stein抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Rosa把床单对折，腾出一半，Berta Stein用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。

  Rosa第二次去时，Elisabeth Huber 2找不到上次的登记卡。柜子里堆着三个月的表格，Elisabeth Huber 2翻了半小时，说可能送到了区里。Josef Huber记得Rosa的名字，却记不得欠条金额；Rosa把数字写在手上，伸过去给Josef Huber看。
  _effects: condition; flag +legal_aid_file_lost; knowledge “the clinic lost the intake card between visits”_

  _effects: cash 2 (仓库门口的尺码); condition; flag +issued_winter_coat_to_refugee; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; knowledge “the warehouse has no women’s coats until next month”_

  电梯停在四楼半，门缝里只看见一截电线。Rosa让Franz Moser抓紧扶手，自己摇动手柄，一格一格把轿厢放下来。门开时，Franz Moser的白大褂后背全湿了；他塞给Rosa两张钞票，说手术室里还有人等着。

- **1956.08** `ld18-absurd.moral-prize::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-medal-is-withdrawn` tags: absurd, irony, scandal, public, marriage, queer, secret_intimacy, vienna
  铜牌挂了一个月，有人写信告诉协会，说她们不是姐妹。Mitzi Weiss把铜牌取回去，还在门口站了一会儿，问Rosa为什么要骗人。Rosa说没有人问过；Mitzi Weiss张了张嘴，最后只说了一句“可惜”。
  _effects: state_add ld18-public-desire state:world-vienna-1912:1155; reputation -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 66 -> 78; mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +public_desire_active_

  亲戚的婚礼上，Rosa被安排和{link:beloved}分开坐。司仪问有没有人反对，Rosa低头喝汤；{link:beloved}在另一桌替别人倒酒，手很稳，酒没有洒出来。散席时，两个人在衣帽间碰见，只说了一句“外面下雨”。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 78 -> 88; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.grief 4 -> 8; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Rosa在黑暗里被Mitzi Kraus握住手，两人的手心都是汗。银幕上正放到接吻，后排有人吹口哨；Rosa想抽回手，Mitzi Kraus没有松。散场后，两人沿着电车轨道走，谁也没有提那场电影。

  Maria Berg 2在门口拦住Rosa，说人不能没有祷告就下葬。Rosa问祷告要不要钱，Maria Berg 2愣住；Otto Huber把门关上，外面的人开始唱死者最喜欢的歌。歌声从门缝里漏出去，Maria Berg 2站了一会儿，跟着哼了半句。

### 247 subject events

- **1913.01** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/scalded-hand` tags: infancy, health, family, home, injury
  Katharina Bauer提热水壶往木盆里兑水时壶底脱了焊，滚水溅在左手背上。她咬着牙把壶搁下，Emil被响声惊得大哭。手背起了水泡，接下来三天她只能用一只手洗尿布。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (左手被开水烫伤); mood -0.05_

- **1915.02** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Emil踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Emil抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Emil举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1917.10** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-lose` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  铁环滚进沟沿，Emil扑过去抓，膝盖先着了地。Mitzi Lechner把铁环拎起来，在手里转了一圈，说不能白捡。Emil摸出一颗石弹子递过去，Mitzi Lechner这才松手。回家路上Emil一瘸一拐，裤膝上沾着石屑和血，经过Mitzi Lechner家门口时特意绕到街对面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.04; condition_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “你看看这角。”Alois Lechner把床单拎起来，煤渣簌簌往下掉。Emil伸手去拍，反而把灰按进布纹里。Alois Lechner从工钱里数回两枚，只留一赫勒在Emil手心，让重洗那角。晚饭前Emil一直蹲在井台边，换了两盆水，指甲缝里全是黑泥。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.049999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1918.10** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  楼梯间的灯罩空着，第三级踏板比别处高。Emil一脚踩空，滚到平台，右小臂磕在铁扶手的弯头上。Katharina Bauer听见哭声跑出来，把Emil抱回屋，用冷水毛巾敷那块青紫，再用旧布条缠了两圈。夜里Emil哭醒两次。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Anna Stein从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Emil。Emil把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

- **1926.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在衣帽间堵住Hedwig，问那两张票怎么到了Klara手里。Hedwig说Emil从来没敢开口，自己只是替Emil把话说了；Emil把票根揉成一团，塞进Hedwig的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。

  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Emil在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Elisabeth Bauer。这一次对方先点了头，Emil准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Emil才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 50 -> 55; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/forewoman-catches` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Maria Kral把Emil叫到巷口，问Elisabeth Bauer叫什么、住在哪间屋。Emil答到一半就停住了。Maria Kral说这半天的活不算数，说冒名顶替坏了规矩。Emil回到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang时，Elisabeth Bauer正扶着床沿站着，听完只说“别去了”，说完又低下头，像是自己连累了Emil。
  _effects: mood -0.07; relation kin with person-elisabeth-bauer; health -0.020000000000000018 (白跑一趟，路上没吃东西)_

- **1926.02** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/run-off-the-choir-loft` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil在高音上停住，嘴唇还张着，声音却没有出来。风琴继续往前走了两小节，Luise Kral开始让合唱队接；Emil从唱诗班的侧门跑下去，木楼梯在脚下响得像一串惊雷。散场时，Klara在教堂门口等，Emil从后门走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_fled_solo; mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1926.06** `yf18-runaway.bundle::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/hide-the-bundle` tags: runaway, escape, family, plan, night_train, reform, medical, schooling
  Emil把两件衬衫、一双袜子和小时候的洗礼证明卷进一块旧桌布，塞到床底下。Katharina Bauer在厨房里数硬币，数了三遍都没有抬头；Emil躺在黑暗里，听见夜车从远处经过，铁轨的声音一直传到枕头下面。
  _effects: state_add yf18-runaway-plan state:world-vienna-1912:85; flag +yf18_bundle_hidden; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_runaway_plan_active_

- **1926.11** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/signed-apprenticeship` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  毕业钟敲过三下，Luise Kral把成绩单按在讲台上，说Emil还能再读一年，只要家里肯出四克朗。家里人在走廊里把学徒契约展开，纸角还带着面粉。Emil签了名，第二天就站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: cash -2 (毕业钟响过以后); flag +left_school_for_apprenticeship; role; education apprenticeship_

- **1927.01** `yf18-runaway.companion::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/go-alone` tags: runaway, friendship, companion, plan, betrayal, reform, medical, schooling
  Klara把车票放在桌上，说家里给她找了活，走不了。Emil把两张票叠在一起，撕掉一张，另一张塞进内袋；Klara问Emil一个人怕不怕，Emil说不怕，出门时却把地址写给了她。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 55 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 15 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 5 -> 10; relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_go_alone; mood -0.019999999999999962_

  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Emil把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Emil没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 10 -> 18; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 65 -> 63_

  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 20 -> 32; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 18 -> 20; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  Emil把煤油灯芯捻到最亮，复习算术到后半夜，德文作文列了三段提纲。第二天考场上算错一道比例题，但短文写得清楚，Luise Moser收卷时多看了他两眼。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; skill; skill; health -0.050000000000000044 (熬夜复习，睡眠不足); mood 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1927.02** `yf18-firstlook.atlas-margin::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/write-back-in-pencil` tags: first_love, school, atlas, note, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  地理课上，Emil在地图册的空白处看见一行铅笔字：“多瑙河从维也纳流走，不从这儿。”Emil认出是Mitzi的笔迹，趁Luise Kral转身，在下面添了一句：“那你往哪儿流？”下课铃响时，Emil把地图册塞进Mitzi的课桌。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:112; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_atlas_note_

- **1927.02** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sell-the-coat` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  巷口的风把外套下摆吹起来。旧货贩子闻了闻领子，说给两克朗。Emil把钱卷进袜筒，回家前在楼梯口站了一会儿。Katharina Bauer发现衣钩空了，问了三遍，Emil都说没看见，脚趾在鞋里把硬币越踩越紧。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖掉哥哥旧外套); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  Emil没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Emil把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Emil说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house_

  Luise Moser在班上念了Emil的作文，念到“她的手套放在木座上”时停了一下，说这就是考试里最危险的东西：把私事当成论据。全班笑了，Emil盯着桌面上的墨水圈，听见Klara在最后一排合上了书。

  Ferdinand Huber的灯从摩天轮那边晃过来时，Klara已经跑没影了，Emil的鞋带却挂在木槽边。Ferdinand Huber把Emil带到园门口，记下住址，说星期一让学校来领人；Emil一路上只担心Klara会不会把这件事讲给全班。

  Anna Hofer说自己眼睛不行了，带不了徒弟，但从帆布袋里抽出一本旧字样本，让Emil拿回去临摹。书皮用麻线缝过三次，扉页上有一行褪色的签名。少年双手接过来，在咖啡馆门口鞠了个躬。

  最后一道弯，Emil没有捏刹车，借来的车架在石子路上跳了两下。Mitzi的黄色车轮就在右边，链条发出干涩的响声。Emil先冲过线，Friedrich Hofer举起旗子，Josef Bauer在人群里喊，别摔了我的车！Emil把车停下来，才发现车把上全是汗。

- **1930.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.servants-room` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Rosa Moser 2的东家说得很清楚：结婚就得搬出佣人房，工钱也要减。那间房在厨房后面，冬天靠炉灶取暖。Rosa Moser 2算了一夜，第二天把Emil送的围巾叠好送回来。两人在面包房门口站了一会儿，各自去上工；围巾上还留着烤炉的暖气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66; role; flag +marriage_blocked_by_lodging; cash -1 (bread from the bakery); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-66_

- **1930.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Emil在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Maria Bauer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1930.01** `p9-gap-adult-ordinary-day.organ-grinder/organ-grinder.poured-water` tags: adulthood, daily, leisure, neighborhood, ordinary, music, street

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  Emil向Karl Kraus问清下个月郊外演唱会的车费由谁出，Karl Kraus说团里只贴一半，另一半要团员自己凑。Emil把四十赫勒硬币在指间转了两圈，最终没有放进收款盒，说等发了工钱再来；这一来一去，也算清了半票、酒棚和车钱各是多少。出门时Anton Lechner正扛着啤酒桶进后厅，两人侧身让过。

  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费（其中0.4克朗记入债务）); health -0.03 (长时间站立等候，膝盖和腰背酸痛); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

- **1935.11** `ml18-gambling.card-system::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/double-to-recover` tags: gambling, luck, money, casino, risk, absurd, vienna, urban
  第四把庄，Emil把家里的房租也押了上去。庄家翻出九点，闲家只有三点；Elisabeth Berg 3没有哭，只把戒指从手上摘下来放在桌边。Emil在天亮前走出赌场，口袋里剩下一枚筹码和一张写着地址的借条；街上的扫雪人已经开始工作。
  _effects: cash -40 (rent and savings lost at the card table（其中40克朗记入债务）); state_add ml18-reversal state:world-vienna-1912:342; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; condition; flag +lost_the_rent_at_cards; knowledge “纸牌没有记忆，三把庄之后闲仍然不是必然。”; flag +fortune_reversed_

- **1936.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Emil在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Emil把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages（其中8克朗记入债务）)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004597_

- **1936.01** `ml18-reversal.old-partner-returns::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/report-him` tags: reversal, money, betrayal, business, scam, comeback, vienna, urban
  Emil没有碰那只皮包，直接去了警察局，把当年的合同、假收据和Maria Bauer的新地址一起交上去。Luise Weiss 2在门外等着，说这样至少不会再有人上当。案子拖了一年，Maria Bauer最后赔了一小笔钱；Emil拿到钱时，发现自己并没有想象中高兴。
  _effects: state_add under-investigation state:world-vienna-1912:378; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; reputation 0.03; flag +reported_old_partner; knowledge “旧合伙人把同一批糖厂债券卖给了至少六个人，合同上的公司地址是空的。”; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:ml18-reversal/reversal-half-year/another-turn-of-the-screw` tags: ml18-reversal, money, luck, reversal, risk, debt, reputation, loss
  补洞的钱又从另一个洞漏出去：煤价涨了，房东换了锁，那个说好下月还钱的人搬走了。Emil在账本上把同一笔支出记了两次，第二次是因为不相信第一次。夜里听见楼梯响，就以为是债主。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-reversal.swing -50 -> -58; state_parameter ml18-reversal.strain 55 -> 63; cash -2 (coal, a new lock and a missing debtor（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1936.01** `kernel-health.task-tuberculosis-cure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital::tb-cure-war-requisition/tb-war-home-cure` tags: health, task, tuberculosis, sanatorium, cure, vienna, urban, crisis
  疗养院被改成军医院，病床让给了伤员。Emil领到一瓶鱼肝油和一张“居家隔离”的纸条；医生说，窗户开着，别对着人咳。他把窗户开了一条缝，又把床挪到离门最远的地方。鱼肝油见底后，他把瓶子倒过来控了控。
  _effects: cash -3 (cod-liver oil and a written home-isolation note（其中3克朗记入债务）); health -0.05 (tuberculosis treated at home after the sanatorium was requisitioned); condition; role; knowledge “战争一来，疗养院先改成军医院；肺病病人领一瓶鱼肝油回家，窗户开着，别对着人咳。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-95_

- **1936.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Emil就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Elisabeth Berg 3摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

- **1936.06** `r1-hc.childbed-fever::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/neighbor-nurses` tags: health, childbirth, fever, hospital, ward, vienna, urban, crisis
  Maria Bauer教Elisabeth Berg 3用开水煮布垫，每次换完都把手泡在石炭酸里。Emil烧了四天，第五天早上要了一碗粥，粥里放了很多盐；Elisabeth Berg 3尝了一口，没说咸。
  _effects: cash -10 (产床边的洗手盆（其中10克朗记入债务）); health -0.05 (产床边的洗手盆); condition; role; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; knowledge “boiled pads and carbolic acid were the only defence in the w”_

  市政审计说慈善病房亏损，Alois Huber带来封条，把三楼尽头那两间锁了。Emil还有半瓶药在床头柜里，Luise Kral 2替Emil把药倒进纸袋，说回家按时吃。走廊里的床一张张撤走，墙上的号码还留着。
  _effects: condition; flag +charity_ward_closed_by_audit; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

  Emil没有给Luise Bauer报名。家里需要一双能看摊的手，学校也还欠着上一学期的纸笔钱；晚上Luise Bauer把别人的课本借来看，Emil假装没有看见。

  放映到第十一本，胶片在片门里卷成一团。Emil剪掉烧焦的一截，发现后面接的是一卷没送审的新闻片：军靴踏过广场，镜头扫过一张张被按下去的脸。Berta Winter 4在黑暗里说“接回正片”，Emil照做了。

- **1937.07** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Emil当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

- **1937.12** `vienna-adult-social.burial-fund/burial-fund.paid` tags: adulthood, social, work, family
  车间里管互助会的Luise Weiss 3在午休时把本子摊在Emil的工具箱上，说这个月还差最后一份月钱。Emil从马甲内袋摸出二十个克罗伊策，Luise Weiss 3当着他的面在本子上画了钩。
  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; reputation 0.04; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86_

  _effects: move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house; role; cash -20 (escape and lodging（其中8克朗记入债务）); condition_

  孩子出生后取名Gustav Bauer，Elisabeth Berg 3一直出血。Emil跑去医院，医生来晚了，只留下药和十八克朗的账单；Elisabeth Berg 3落下产后虚弱的毛病，Emil抱着孩子坐在床边，直到邻居把汤送来，才发现自己的鞋穿反了。
  _effects: birth male; cash -18 (doctor and medicine（其中18克朗记入债务）); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:446_

  Emil在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Alois Huber算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.039999999999999994; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +signed_balanced_budget; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

- **1938.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 22; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 50 -> 45; flag +odd_job_scare_

  Katharina Bauer不肯躺着，第三天就去帮人洗衣挣回一克朗。Emil晚上发现她的腿又肿了，那点工钱抵不上药钱；炉边的药布还没干，又得重新煮。
  _effects: cash 1 (母亲洗衣挣的零钱); mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family
  Emil没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-wwii-conscription/drafted-again` tags: war, conscription, adulthood, migration

  _effects: role; move place-wien-landwehr-barracks; cash 6 (family allowance)_

- **1939.01** `office16-appointment.patronage-ledger::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/list-rejected` tags: office, appointment, patronage, organization, politics, power, vienna, urban
  名单在委员会卡住了。Lena的名字被划掉，理由是“没有相关资历”；Emil没有争，只把名单收回公文包。晚上，Lena来问结果，Emil说再等一年；Lena没有坐，站了一会儿就走了。
  _effects: reputation -0.02; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; flag +appointment_list_rejected; knowledge “委员会以“没有相关资历”为由划掉了联络员的名字。”_

  一年过去，Emil已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Emil也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 19 -> 24; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 26 -> 31; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 0 -> 12; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

- **1939.02** `p10-family-fate.separation/separation-move-out` tags: adulthood, family, marriage, birth, death, inheritance, household, housing
  箱子是结婚时抬上来的那只，磕掉漆的角朝外。Elisabeth Berg 3合盖时压了两次才扣住，Emil站在窗边没转身。分手的话早说完了，剩下的只有箱子在楼梯上磕碰的闷响。

  夜班结束前，Emil听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 18 -> 26; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 22 -> 19; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

- **1939.06** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Landwehr-Kaserne病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1939.07** `bc18-enemy.accidental-help::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-hospital/letter-printed-as-praise` tags: enemy, mistake, work, reputation, absurd, luck, social, vienna
  Katharina Bauer 2写了一封告状信，列数Emil的懒惰、贪婪和“可疑的出身”，却把信封上的地址写成了报社。Friedrich Hofer以为这是一篇自荐小品，删掉几处脏话登了出来；读者喜欢这种不留情面的自嘲，两个商行给Emil寄来聘书。Katharina Bauer 2在咖啡馆看见报纸，差点把杯子捏碎。
  _effects: cash 4 (商行聘书的预付薪金); reputation 0.05; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:487; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; condition; flag +enemys_letter_became_an_advert; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.02; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.08** `statistical_recovery`

- **1940.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 26 -> 33; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 2 -> 3; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

  医院要腾床位给伤员，Josef Bauer被送回家，带回来的只有半瓶药和一张复诊条。Emil把厨房的长凳拼成床，Anton教他用温水擦身；夜里空袭灯罩只留一条缝，药瓶放在伸手够得到的地方。

  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); health -0.020000000000000004 (moving in winter)_

- **1947.01** `task-vienna-family-care.care-sick-parent/night-watch` tags: family, care, courtship, siblings, inheritance, housing, task, task:family
  Emil整夜没合眼，按时把最后两包药粉喂给Katharina Bauer，水盆换到第五次。天快亮时咳嗽声稀了，老人的呼吸慢慢平下来。Emil在椅子上打了个盹，醒来时炉子灭了，赶到工坊迟了一刻钟，被扣了半天工钱。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (整夜未睡，受凉); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; cash -1.2 (迟到被扣的半天工钱)_

- **1947.08** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/collect-the-benefit` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  邻居出主意说，死者家属可以领一笔丧葬补助。Emil让Ferdinand Huber冒充远房表姐，自己在旁边按了手印；钱到手后，两人在厨房里分成三份，第三份留着买煤。第二个星期，Helene Stein寄来一封信，说抚恤金已经核准，请家属来拍一张遗像；Emil把信压在盐罐下面，盐受潮结了块。
  _effects: cash 4 (冒领的丧葬补助分成); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:780; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; condition; flag +collected_own_funeral_benefit; mood 0.010000000000000009; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1947.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.hours-ledger/hours-ledger.refuse` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  工头把表折成四折塞进围裙口袋，说“你会后悔的”。Emil当天下午去账房窗口排队，窗上的铁栅栏有股锈味。账房先生翻了半天蓝皮本子，最后从抽屉里数出八克朗硬币，一枚一枚推过台面。
  _effects: cash 8 (追回的拖欠计件工资); reputation -0.03; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-129_

- **1948.01** `ml18-charity.neighborhood-clinic::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/the-family-blocks-the-donation` tags: charity, money, clinic, reputation, community, warm, vienna, urban
  捐款的事刚说出口，家里的兄弟先来了。他说这笔钱该留给自家人，不该拿去给街上的孩子看病。Berta Weiss在诊所门口等了半小时，最后把图纸卷起来；Emil站在空铺面里，听见隔壁肉铺的锯骨声一下一下传过来。
  _effects: cash -2 (the notary's fee for the abandoned clinic deed); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; flag +family_blocked_clinic; knowledge “诊所的铺面已经租下，但家里的兄弟不同意把横财捐出去。”; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Emil的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Emil收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Emil先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 39 -> 46; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 40 -> 50; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket/p9-social-daily-culture.last-standing-ticket.buy-for-elder` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  前面的老人掏零钱时手抖得厉害，数了三遍也没凑够。Emil替老人把四十赫勒递进窗口，老人连声道谢，硬塞回一枚十赫勒铜板。等Emil自己买票时，窗口已经挂出“售罄”的牌子，只好站在剧院门廊下听里面隐约的乐声，脚底冰凉；这一趟净少三十赫勒，却让队伍里的人都记住了这份体面。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (替排队的老人垫付站票钱); cash 0.1 (老人硬塞回的铜板); reputation 0.04; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1948.05** `bc18-ghost.census-night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/counted-twice` tags: bureaucracy, census, identity, paperwork, absurd, neighborhood, legal, vienna
  普查员在门口翻了半天名册，说Emil在死亡栏里，不能算进住户。Emil把他请进屋，倒了一杯水；普查员想了很久，最后在“死者”旁边的空行里又添了一个“同名房客”。月底，住房补贴按两个人头发下来，Emil买了一双新鞋，另一份钱塞进了门房的口袋。
  _effects: cash 1 (多算一个人头得到的住房补贴); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 35 -> 39; condition; flag +counted_as_two_people; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1948.10** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013697_

- **1949.01** `ml18-family.school-gate-car::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/move-the-child-to-a-better-school` tags: family, money, child, school, reputation, dark, vienna, urban
  新雇的马车每天把孩子送到校门口，车夫穿得比老师还整齐。第三天，Lena把Emil叫到走廊，说班上有家长联名，要求孩子转学，理由是“这家人突然有钱，来路不明”。Emil没有争辩，第二周把孩子送进了城另一头的学校，学费贵了一倍。
  _effects: cash -30 (tuition and carriage for the new school); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 0 -> 0; state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 59 -> 71; state_parameter raising-child.childConfidence 70 -> 75; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005846:child; flag +child_moved_school; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  一个税务员把Emil的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Emil收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Emil先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。

- **1949.01** `kernel-family.housing-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/housing-relative.lodged-elsewhere` tags: task, task:family, family, housing, relatives, godparent, guardian, household
  Emil在楼里替教父Franz Lechner问了一圈，最后在洗衣妇家找到一张空床，每月三克朗。Franz Lechner把箱子搬过去，Emil替他垫了第一个月的房租；两人在楼梯口分开，Franz Lechner说等找到工就还。床板短了一截，箱子只能竖着放。
  _effects: cash -3 (first month’s bed rent for the relative); relation kin with person-franz-lechner; role; flag +relative_lodged_elsewhere; knowledge “洗衣妇家有一张空床，每月三克朗。”_

- **1949.03** `bc18-ghost.census-night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/ghost-story` tags: bureaucracy, census, identity, paperwork, absurd, neighborhood, legal, vienna
  普查员把当晚的事讲给了酒馆里的人听，第二天，街上的孩子开始敲Emil的门，喊“活鬼出来”。Emil开门，他们就跑；Emil关门，他们又回来。月底，一个走江湖的戏班找上门，愿意出两克朗请Emil在幕间站一会儿，不用说话，只要穿白衣服。
  _effects: cash 2 (戏班请人站幕间的酬劳); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 5; reputation -0.020000000000000004; state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:840; flag +local_ghost_story; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.019999999999999997; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.06** `lc17-care.offers-to-care::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/hires-the-neighbour` tags: care, family, aging, health, household, duty, vienna, urban
  Emil算了一夜账，第二天去敲楼下退休护士的门。看护每天下午来两小时，工钱按周付；Josef Bauer嫌贵，Emil说“总比我辞工强”。第一周结束时，药盒上的时间第一次没有写错。
  _effects: state_add lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:839; cash -3 (the first week of paid care); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +paid_for_parent_care_

- **1950.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/stay-with-relatives` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, injury
  分房名单又往后推了一年，Emil一家搬进亲戚家的厨房隔间。Franz Huber送来一块旧帘子当隔断；夜里有人起身，全屋都听得见。
  _effects: cash -3 (shared household costs); health -0.030000000000000027 (crowded sleeping space); condition; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002694:child_

- **1950.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Emil和Otto Kral 5在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Otto Kral 5在门后放了一根木棍，Emil把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152_

- **1950.01** `bc18-office.new-director-speech::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/speech-misread` tags: office, promotion, speech, bureaucracy, public, absurd, comic, vienna
  第一次训话，Emil把预算备忘录拿倒了，从头念到尾。底下的人先是发愣，随后有人小声说，主任是在用倒着的账提醒大家：钱不够。散会后，各科主动减了文具和加班费，Emil没有解释，Helene Stein 2在门口递上一杯咖啡，说这招比拍桌子管用。
  _effects: cash 1 (各科省下来的办公费分成); state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 0 -> 5; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 20 -> 25; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; skill; flag +budget_read_backwards; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/reprinted-wrong` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  报纸的周末副刊又把Emil的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Emil写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 25 -> 31; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.absurdity 85 -> 90; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 5 -> 1; mood -0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_remove bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:780; flag -bureaucratic_ghost_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/worn-thin` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Josef Bauer今年摔了两次，第二次把胯骨摔裂了。Emil在医院走廊的硬椅上睡了四夜，回家以后发现厂里已经把位置给了别人；药费单、住院单和一张停工的证明一起压在桌玻璃下面，边角每天都被翻卷一点。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 20 -> 28; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 5 -> 11; health -0.040000000000000036 (four nights on a hospital chair); cash -2 (medicine and hospital fees); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1950.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 31 -> 38; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 2; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000002; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `kernel-family.care-sick-parent::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/care-sick-parent.hired-nurse` tags: task, task:family, family, care, health, parent, work, vienna
  Emil请Katharina Lechner白天来照看，自己照常上工，每天多付一克朗。Katharina Lechner把Katharina Bauer扶到窗边晒太阳，回来时顺便买药；Emil晚上接班，给Katharina Bauer读报纸上的战况和物价，读到一半就有人睡着。读报的声音越来越低，最后只剩炉子里的煤响。
  _effects: cash -10 (daily fee for the neighbour nurse and medicine); relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000520:child; role; flag +hired_nurse_

- **1950.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Otto Huber嫌Emil的床单滴水滴到Otto Huber家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Emil下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Emil把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Otto Huber那侧。
  _effects: relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004601:child; cash -0.15 (重洗床单的肥皂钱)_

- **1951.01** `af17-wish.family-inheritance::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/kept-the-shop-and-the-sisters` tags: wish, love, family, inheritance, shop, debt, irony, vienna
  遗嘱把烟铺、楼上的两位老小姐和十四克朗的赊账一起留给Emil。Ferdinand Huber第一次见面就叫Emil“侄子”，把钥匙、账本和一只总在柜台下打盹的猫交过来。Emil原本只想要一个家，现在有了一个需要每天清晨六点开门的家；Berta Winter 3在门口抽完一支雪茄，提醒Emil月底先还利息。
  _effects: role; cash -2 (先还一部分烟铺赊账); relation kin with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93; reputation 0.019999999999999997; condition; flag +kept_the_tobacco_shop; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

  Ferdinand Bauer翻了一遍Emil的证件，又看了看那双开胶的鞋，把借据收回抽屉。Ferdinand Weiss想替Emil说句话，Ferdinand Bauer抬手止住，说不是不肯，是这一行已经倒了两家。出门时，Emil把口袋里最后一枚铜板捏得发热。
  _effects: cash -1 (借据上的第二行); flag +loan_refused; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; knowledge “two small lenders in the district have already failed”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

  胶片在片门里起火，火苗顺着观察窗舔进大厅。Emil没有先去拿灭火沙，而是把后排的孩子往侧门推；Franz Weiss用湿毯子捂住片盒，手背烧掉一层皮。第二天，环城大道写字楼管理处把烧黑的片盒留在柜台上，说“留着，提醒我们”。

- **1951.06** `lc17-care.crisis-night::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/calls-the-neighbor` tags: adulthood, care, eldercare, night, family, exhaustion, illness, vienna
  Emil敲开隔壁的门，请那位退休的医院杂工来看一眼。老人翻了翻Katharina Bauer的眼皮，说该叫救护车；Emil在走廊里数着口袋里的钞票，最后只叫了一辆出租马车。医院门口的队伍一直排到街上，Emil把外套脱下来垫在Katharina Bauer背后。
  _effects: cash -3 (a cab to the hospital and the outpatient fee); health 0.019999999999999997 (the parent was seen at the hospital); state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 28 -> 32; mood -0.010000000000000009; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer; flag +called_ambulance_

  Emil在Helene Stein 2面前签下放弃继承的声明，Josef Bauer从内袋里摸出二十克朗推过来，纸币上还带着体温。Anna Bauer把房契往自己那边挪了挪。Emil数也没数就把钱收好，出门时听见公证人办公室的门在背后合上，铜把手凉了一下手心，这二十克朗把兄妹之间的话买断了一截。
  _effects: cash 20 (放弃继承换来一笔现钱); relation kin with person-josef-bauer; flag +renounced_inheritance_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 77 -> 82; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 49 -> 53; cash -1 (another year of food and lamp oil); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Emil又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Emil的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 40 -> 36; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 17 -> 15; health -0.010000000000000009 (a year of broken nights); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 40 -> 32; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 65 -> 60; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1952.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1952.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/recognized-neighbor` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil舀到第七碗时，认出碗后面那张脸是楼下的Anton Winter。Anton Winter把帽檐压低，Emil什么也没问，只把勺子沉下去，多捞了一块土豆。后面的人开始敲碗。
  _effects: mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +saw_neighbor_in_soup_line; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004484:child; knowledge “the neighbor has been queueing at the soup kitchen”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-106_

- **1952.02** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/keys-and-photo-returned` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, separation
  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Emil把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Otto Kral 5把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-152; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly_

- **1952.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_lost_landlady` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Mitzi借给Emil一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Emil睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.11** `ml18-charity.neighborhood-clinic::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-clinic-opens` tags: charity, money, clinic, reputation, community, warm, vienna, urban
  诊所设在街角一间改过的铺面里，门口挂着一块红砖色的木牌。Alois Kral 2每周来两个下午，给孩子量体重、给老人听肺；Helene Stein 2送来一盆天竺葵，Otto Moser 2把候诊室的长凳刨平。Emil没有剪彩，只把第一张药方折好，放进抽屉最里面。
  _effects: cash -30 (endowment for the neighborhood clinic); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.principal 78 -> 48; organization_found 街角诊所 (other); reputation 0.1; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; flag +clinic_opened; knowledge “街角诊所的第一张药方锁在抽屉里，药是给孩子退烧的。”; mood 0.09999999999999998; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-118_

- **1953.01** `lc17-care.last-letter::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-false-alarm` tags: adulthood, care, death, inheritance, grief, family, funeral, vienna
  Katharina Bauer半夜喘不上气，Emil已经叫了神父。天亮前，Katharina Bauer却坐起来要喝咖啡，说只是梦见有人追。Emil把神父送出门，回来时看见Katharina Bauer正把葬礼上要用的黑领带系在猫脖子上。Gustav Huber下午来电话问遗嘱要不要改，Emil说“先放着”。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 36 -> 40; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 15 -> 17; health 0.03 (the parent recovered by morning); mood 0.020000000000000018; cash -1 (the priest's car fare and a bottle of coffee); flag +false_alarm; relation kin with person-katharina-bauer_

- **1953.01** `state_reaction:lc17-caregiver/patient-death` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Katharina Bauer去世的消息传来时，Emil正在把当天的药片分进小格。护士问要不要留一件衣服，Emil说“留着那件灰的”，说完才发现自己的声音很平，像在替别人办手续。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; health -0.020000000000000018 (another death in the family); flag +buried_parent_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:016919_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:lc17-caregiver/care-year/carried` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Emil又撑过一年。药盒按星期分好，账本上多了一栏“看护”，工钱少了一些，但还没有断；Josef Bauer今年记得住Emil的名字，偶尔也记得住昨天吃了什么。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-caregiver.burden 40 -> 36; state_parameter lc17-caregiver.resentment 17 -> 15; health -0.010000000000000009 (a year of broken nights); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 29 -> 21; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 60 -> 55; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1953.01** `r13-fam.look-for-lost-relative::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/no-record` tags: task, family, missing, official, war, search, vienna, urban
  Elisabeth Kral 2把三本名册都翻完，说没有这个人，也许登记时用了别的写法。Anna Bauer不肯走，又把名字念了一遍，念到第三遍时声音低下去。Emil扶她到走廊的长椅上坐下，替她把围巾重新系好；窗口后面已经叫了下一个号。
  _effects: cash -1 (念错的名字); mood -0.020000000000000018; condition; flag +missing_relative_no_record; knowledge “there is no record under the name the aunt remembers”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146_

- **1953.02** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Emil听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 10 -> 18; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 32 -> 29; mood -0.030000000000000027; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017063_

- **1953.05** `statistical_infection`
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1953.06** `statistical_recovery`
  Emil从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1953.07** `ld18-bathhouse.vow::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-vow-in-the-steam` tags: love, queer, men, intimacy, secret, vow, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Emil和Josef Huber在澡堂最里面的隔间交换了戒指，蒸汽把两个人的脸都熏红了。没有证婚人，只有一只漏水的铜龙头在滴答响；Josef Huber说这声音像教堂的钟，Emil说那就当它是。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:1003; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; cash -1 (two rings and the bathhouse fee); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +vow_in_the_steam; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1953.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Franz Huber抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Franz Huber用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002694:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.11** `r3-crime.vagrancy-arrest::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sent-to-the-workhouse` tags: crime, police, poverty, workhouse, homelessness, vienna, urban, postwar
  Gustav Huber说拘留室满了，把Emil送上了一辆去乡下的卡车。车上还有三个人，谁也不说话。Emil被分到甜菜地，晚上睡在谷仓，工钱直接记在拘留所的账上。Grete Kraus后来告诉Emil的妹妹，说“他去外地找活了”。
  _effects: condition; flag +sent_to_the_workhouse; role; knowledge “workhouse wages are credited to the police station”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-126_

- **1954.01** `ld18-divorce.bench::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/the-case-is-dismissed` tags: divorce, marriage, scandal, queer, law, public, secret_intimacy, vienna
  Elisabeth Huber 2说证据不足，建议双方回去冷静。Josef Huber在走廊里问Emil要不要一起回家；Emil说再想想。那天晚上，Emil回到Alois Kral 2的住处，发现门缝里塞着一封信，只有一句：“我不催你。”
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 25 -> 40; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 40 -> 30; flag +divorce_dismissed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116_

- **1954.01** `state_reaction:ld18-impossible-attachment/a-question-at-the-office` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  办公室有人问Emil，为什么总有一个女人在楼下等她。Emil说那是表妹；对方笑了一下，说现在的表妹真体贴。下午，Emil把桌上的相框扣倒，又在没人时翻回来，用袖子擦了擦玻璃上的灰。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 40 -> 48; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 30 -> 26; reputation -0.01999999999999999_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017602_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Emil最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Emil把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Emil说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 48 -> 54; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 26 -> 23; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.01** `state_expire:lc17-caregiver` tags: lc17-caregiver, care, family, illness, elder, duty, household, state
  Emil的「照料一个衰弱的亲人」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove lc17-caregiver state:world-vienna-1912:839_

- **1954.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/blamed-for-the-noise` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  楼上住户投诉夜里有人拖椅子，管事的把巡查簿翻到Emil的那一页，说签字太潦草，看不出巡过几趟。Emil想辩解，却想起那晚确实在楼梯间多站了一刻钟；扣掉的工钱刚好够买一双不漏水的鞋。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 21 -> 13; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.reliability 55 -> 50; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1954.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.cut-the-guarantee/pay-small-part` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  替他还了四克朗，剩下的数目不碰，条件是把赌债字据交出来，当天就去戒酒会挂个名。对方把字据给了，人却没去挂名。四克朗从工资袋里出去，母亲那瓶药只好再等十天，晚上咳得更紧。
  _effects: cash -4 (替友人偿还部分赌债); health -0.030000000000000027 (母亲的药钱被挪去还债，家里几夜没睡好); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:006388:child; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.02** `bc18-inherit.parrot-condition::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/parrot-repeats-secret` tags: inheritance, family, condition, absurd, death, legal, quiet, vienna
  鹦鹉在公证处忽然开口，反复说“盒子在钟后面”。Berta Weiss脸色变了，Berta Kraus说鸟只会学舌；可回家一找，钟后面真有一只铁盒，里面是姑妈早年存下的银器。亲戚们打起了官司，Emil作为养鸟人出庭，拿了三克朗证人费，也记住了Berta Kraus在庭上擦汗的样子。
  _effects: cash 3 (出庭作证的证人费); reputation 0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000680:child; knowledge “公证人知道钟后面的铁盒，却从没在遗嘱里提起。”; condition; flag +parrot_revealed_the_silver; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.05** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/borrows_coal_risk` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Emil把自家煤箱里仅剩的四块煤分了两块给Rosa Berg，自己家那一夜没生火，裹着大衣睡的。孩子烧退了，Rosa Berg第二天还煤时多塞了一把粗盐。Emil当天下午就开始流清鼻涕，第三天咳得直不起腰。
  _effects: health -0.07999999999999996 (受凉咳嗽); food; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004580:child_

- **1954.06** `bc18-fame.wrong-bust::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/plaque-stays` tags: fame, mistaken_identity, museum, public, invention, absurd, status, humiliation
  市立蜡像馆新添了一间“发明家厅”。Alois Kral 2把Emil请到绿绒椅子上，说照片是从旧报纸上剪的，名字也核对过三遍。揭幕那天，Emil看见自己的蜡像握着一根安全火柴，铭牌上写着一个从未属于Emil的专利号；掌声很整齐，Alois Kral 2把下一场讲解的酬劳塞进口袋，动作像在递一块糖。
  _effects: cash 4 (蜡像馆的讲解酬劳); state_add bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:1049; reputation 0.02999999999999997; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-147; flag +ridiculously_famous; reputation 0.01999999999999999; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `ld18-breakup.returned-keys::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/one-last-winter` tags: breakup, grief, love, keys, queer, ending, secret_intimacy, vienna
  两个人约定再过一个冬天，等Josef Huber的弟弟找到工作就分开。那个冬天特别长，煤贵，汤稀；她们还是每周洗一次头发，还是为谁忘了买盐吵架。春天来的时候，Josef Huber的箱子已经收好了，谁也没有先提。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 54 -> 64; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 60 -> 55; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; flag +one_last_winter_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Emil最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Emil把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Emil说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 64 -> 70; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 23 -> 20; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-audience-gets-bored` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  观众开始厌倦同一个故事。园艺会请了更年轻的发明家，女子学校把Emil的名字从海报上撤下；Emil在咖啡馆听见有人说“那个人还在讲火柴”。同一个月，一张报纸把旧照片配上新标题，问Emil是否已经“过气”。纠正的冲动又回来，但没有人再提问。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 12 -> 4; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.boredom 8 -> 20; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 1 -> 0; reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1955.01** `state_expire:oj18-odd-apprenticeship` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  Emil的「奇职学徒」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:922; role; flag -odd_job_apprentice_

- **1955.01** `r13-fam.help-neighbour::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/carried-the-coal` tags: task, social, neighbor, help, care, vienna, urban, postwar
  Emil看见Mitzi Bauer在楼梯口歇了三回，便把煤筐接过来，一筐一筐抬上三楼。最后一趟，Emil的腰在门框上撞了一下，Mitzi Bauer从柜里摸出一小瓶李子酒，硬倒了一盅。
  _effects: food; health -0.030000000000000027 (把煤筐抬上三楼); flag +carried_neighbour_coal; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1955.07** `ml18-inheritance.conditional-will::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/keep-the-candle-workshop-open` tags: inheritance, legal, family, money, conditions, cinema, quiet, vienna
  姑婆的遗嘱只有一页：六十克朗留给Emil，条件是那间放无声电影的小影院再开五年，并且把她的侄孙女送进学校。Gustav Huber把三个条件念了两遍；Grete Winter在门外等着，手里捏着另一份没有拆封的遗嘱。Emil先领了十克朗，把影院的钥匙挂在腰上。
  _effects: cash 10 (first payment under the conditional will); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1098; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; flag +conditional_heir; knowledge “遗嘱要求小影院再开五年，并把姑婆的侄孙女送进学校。”; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Emil把钥匙丢在了回家路上的有轨电车轨道旁，天黑也没找着。房东太太说换锁芯要一克朗五十赫勒，从下月房租里扣。Grete Winter借给Emil一把旧挂锁，但锁鼻和门扣对不上，只能用铁丝拧着凑合。那夜Emil睡不踏实，总觉得有人在拧门。
  _effects: cash -1.5 (换锁芯费用); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-99_

- **1956.01** `bc18-fame.merchandise::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/carnival-stall` tags: fame, merchandise, business, absurd, legal, public, reputation, vienna
  厂里剩下一箱卖不掉的假胡子，Emil用三克朗买下，在狂欢节门口摆了个摊。孩子们把胡子别在帽檐上，年轻人买去参加化装舞会；一个老妇人买了两副，说给远行的儿子留着。收摊时，Emil数出五克朗，比本金多了两克朗，还剩下半箱胡子。
  _effects: cash 3 (狂欢节卖假胡子的利润); skill; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 38 -> 36; role; flag +sold_moustaches_at_carnival; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-133_

- **1956.01** `state_reaction:bc18-ridiculous-fame/reprinted-wrong` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  报纸的周末副刊又把Emil的旧照片翻出来，配上一个更整齐的故事：发明、失败、谦逊的晚年。Emil写信去更正，编辑回了一张便条，说读者喜欢现在这个版本。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 36 -> 42; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.absurdity 72 -> 77; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.legitimacy 4 -> 0; mood -0.010000000000000009_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019086_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/the-date-that-is-not-on-paper` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  Emil和{link:beloved}把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Emil在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 55 -> 59; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 70 -> 67; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:bc18-ridiculous-fame/ridiculous-fame-year/the-story-holds-another-year` tags: bc18-ridiculous-fame, fame, reputation, mistaken_identity, promotion, media, absurd, public
  又一年过去，Emil仍在被介绍成那个人物。邀请函从市郊的园艺会、女子学校和退伍军人酒馆寄来；Emil学会在签名后加一句模糊的谦辞，听起来像有许多往事不便细说。每一次应付都带来一点小钱，也让真正的解释更不合时宜。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.notoriety 42 -> 49; state_parameter bc18-ridiculous-fame.invitations 0 -> 1; cash 1 (一场小型演讲的车马费); reputation 0.010000000000000009; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `state_tick:wish-love/love-pressure/put-away` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_tick
  Emil暂时把那件事收进抽屉；日子还有别的东西要应付。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1956.01** `r13-fam.care-sick-child::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/fever-breaks` tags: task, care, health, child, fever, night, vienna, urban
  Emil把湿布拧到不再滴水，敷在Berta 2的额头上。孩子烧得说胡话，Emil就一遍遍念窗外的街名，直到天快亮时，孩子的手心终于出了一层汗。Emil把被子掖好，自己靠在床沿睡了过去，醒来时锅里还剩半碗冷粥。
  _effects: cash -2 (第三遍换湿布); health -0.030000000000000027 (第三遍换湿布); condition; flag +nursed_child_fever; health 0.05999999999999997 (the fever broke after a night of cold compresses); relation guardian with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:012524:child_

- **1956.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Emil没有立刻答话，先去敲Grete Bauer 3的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Grete Bauer 3提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Emil把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-161_

- **1956.05** `ml18-business.bottle-cap-patent::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sell-the-patent-outright` tags: business, patent, money, windfall, invention, comic, vienna, urban
  破产拍卖会上，Emil用四克朗买下一张没人举牌的瓶盖专利，只因为图纸画得好看。三个月后，小巷电影院的矿泉水厂找上门：那圈软木垫正好能解决他们的漏气问题。Gustav Huber核对完专利号，小巷电影院当场开出八十克朗的支票。
  _effects: cash 80 (sale of the bottle-cap patent); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:1152; reputation 0.06; flag +sold_patent; mood 0.09999999999999998; flag -sudden_fortune; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1956.10** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Emil在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Mitzi Winter从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007452:child_

- **1957.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/compromise_split_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Emil把洗好的床单搭上院子里的公用晾衣绳，占了整条。Mitzi Winter抱着一盆湿衣服下来，说自家孩子明天要穿干净衬衫上学。两人在绳子两头扯了一阵，最后Emil把床单对折，腾出一半，Mitzi Winter用木夹子把衬衫一件件排上去。谁也没道歉，但第二天见面都点了头。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007452:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019089_

- **1957.01** `state_reaction:ml18-sudden-fortune/a-relative-remembers` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一个多年没走动的亲戚提着一盒点心上门，先问Emil的身体，再问那笔钱是怎么来的。临走时他说起小时候替Emil家垫过一冬天的煤钱，数字记不清了，但意思很清楚。
  _effects: state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 0 -> 1; flag +windfall_claim_by_relative; mood -0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019636_

- **1957.01** `r15-fam.child-leaves::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/stays-for-now` tags: family, child, leaving, home, child_left_home, vienna, urban, postwar
  Luise Bauer说再等一年，等家里的债还掉一点。Emil没有劝，只把晚饭多添了一勺；夜里Emil听见Luise Bauer在隔壁翻书，煤油灯一直亮到后半夜。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 52 -> 57; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 35 -> 39; cash -1 (another year of food and lamp oil); mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/someone-asks-for-the-papers` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Emil说{link:beloved}是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 67 -> 79; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 20 -> 12; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Emil的「荒唐的名声」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove bc18-ridiculous-fame state:world-vienna-1912:1049; flag -ridiculously_famous_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Emil被叫到学校，老师说Anna 2这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Emil没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 13; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 55; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils)_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:ml18-sudden-fortune/fortune-year/money-keeps-working` tags: ml18-sudden-fortune, money, windfall, sudden_fortune, household, luck, inheritance, lottery
  一年过去，钱没有变多，却也没有一下花完。Emil把一部分存进邮局储蓄所，一部分借给楼下的鞋匠；鞋匠每月送来一双补好的鞋，利息就夹在鞋垫下面。街口的杂货商开始把好面粉留到Emil来的时候。
  _effects: cash 2 (interest and small repayments on the windfall); state_parameter ml18-sudden-fortune.claims 1 -> 2; mood 0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.01** `state_tick:raising-child/school-year/struggles` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_tick
  Emil被叫到学校，老师说Emil Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Emil没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 46 -> 41; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 63 -> 68; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.01** `r13-work.find-supplier::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/thirty-days-credit` tags: commerce, supply, credit, shop, negotiation, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Bauer把样布铺在装土豆的木箱上，先问Emil一个月能卖几匹。Emil报了实数，没有夸大，Maria Bauer反而点头，说头一批货可以三十天后再结。走的时候，Emil把样布角剪下一小块，回家对着灯比了半宿。
  _effects: flag +work13_supplier_gave_credit; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; knowledge “the supplier extends thirty days of credit to small shops”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-144_

- **1957.02** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-hospital/correct-it-twice` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  Emil在窗口要求更正，Elisabeth Huber 2把表格翻了三遍，最后开出一张新的出生证明，名字却少了一个字母。于是城里同时住着一个死人、一个活人，和一个只在档案里出生的陌生人。Emil拿着两张纸站在区公所门口，忽然不知道该用哪一张去领下个月的煤票。
  _effects: cash -1 (更正材料的手续费和车钱); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:1210; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-116; condition; flag +two_living_files_one_death; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-170; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1957.05** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Emil看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Klara追出来问怎么不领了，Emil说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Emil把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; food_

- **1957.08** `state_reaction:raising-child/school-note` tags: raising-child, family, child, care, school, household, state, state_reaction
  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anna Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Emil把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 57 -> 60; mood 0.020000000000000018_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:020050_

- **1957.09** `r2-faith.begging-license::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/arrested-in-the-sweep` tags: charity, begging, police, permit, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  皇帝生日前一周，巡警沿街收铜牌，说市容整顿，有证的也先避一避。Emil被带上囚车时，帽子还留在马路牙子上。Anton Huber 2在局里登记，问职业，Emil说原来有证，Anton Huber 2把“无业”写进了栏里。
  _effects: cash -3 (街角的铜牌); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +arrested_in_begging_sweep; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; knowledge “the police clear licensed beggars before public holidays”_

- **1957.09** `state_reaction:wish-love/love-betrayal` tags: wish-love, wish, wish:love, love, family, intimacy, state, state_reaction
  Emil反复回想最后一次见面。对方说过的话被拆成很多小块，每一块都能扎人。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:019869_
