  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (school books（其中2克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-bread-with-friend` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  Anna Moser家的孩子站在绳子另一头，Lena递夹子时故意慢半拍，两个人把床单拉得没有一道褶。收工后Anna Moser端出半包隔夜面包，掰开时掉了一桌屑。Lena把分到的那块用围裙兜住，回家路上闻着麦味，硬是没咬。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/punished` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  放学路上，Lena把手揣在口袋里，碰一下就疼。回到家门口，Lena先把手背在身后，等Josefine Hofer转身才敢看；那道红印到晚上还没有消。
  _effects: condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-half-credit` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  “月底一起结。”掌柜说完，把半筐煤推到柜台外。Lena用胸口顶住筐沿往家挪，煤灰落进领口，手心被麻绳磨得发亮。Friedrich Hofer在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口等到人，先看了看筐底，再看了看Lena的手，把漏掉的碎渣扫进簸箕。
  _effects: food; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  湿煤灰写出第一笔时，Lena还挺高兴，写到第三行，手一蹭就全花了。Josefine Hofer看见了，把自己的旧手帕撕下一角，蘸水给木板擦了底。第二天在Leopoldstädter Volksschule门口，同学指着那块发黑的木板问是什么，Lena说是石板，对方笑得弯下腰。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Lena的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Lena在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.02999999999999997; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1921.08** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/eraser-cut-in-half` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  教师儿子把Lena的橡皮切成两半，说穷人的橡皮本来就只有半块。Lena没有还手，把半块橡皮收进铅笔盒。第二天Lena发现自己的作业本被撕了两页，Grete Hofer问是谁干的，全班都看着窗外。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +bullied_by_teachers_son; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1921.10** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  “Notwendigkeit”少了一个字母，Luise Kral没说话，只敲了敲桌角。Lena回家时手指上全是墨水，母亲问起，只说写了点作业。
  _effects: mood -0.060000000000000005; skill; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1921.11** `p9-childhood-school.truant-prater/truant-river-dark` tags: childhood, school, teacher, peers, lessons, truancy
  河面上最后一条船过去，灯一盏盏亮起来。Lena把两赫勒烤土豆的空纸袋揉成一团，塞进Josef的口袋。到家时家里人正站在门口，手扶着门框。家里人闻了闻Lena的头发，又看了看鞋上的河泥，什么也没问，只把门开到最大。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (花两赫勒买了烤土豆（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  “老师，我能留下来吗？”Lena问完就盯着自己的鞋尖。Grete Hofer想了一会儿，说早上来，人少。天没亮，Lena已经站在校门口，手指冻得伸不直。教室里只有炉火和粉笔灰的味道，Grete Hofer把一道竖式拆成三行，让Lena跟着念一遍。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1923.01** `r14-edu.boarding-arrival::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/cold-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_isolation, boarding_school, childhood, university, culture, schooling, school
  Hermann Kral把Lena领到三楼的宿舍，指着一张靠窗的铁床，说被子每周三换。床垫上有一道凹痕，是上一个学生留下的。Maria Bauer在对面铺被子，没有抬头。窗外是操场，雾很大，旗杆只露出半截。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +boarding_school_arrival; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Anna Moser把Lena拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Lena把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Lena蹲下把两包并排，按Anna Moser说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1924.01** `r14-edu.boarding-letter::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/classmate-posted-it` tags: education, boarding_isolation, letter, childhood, university, culture, schooling, school
  Maria Bauer把信带出学校，在街角的邮筒前站了很久，又拿回来，说邮票不够。第二天，Maria Bauer从家里带来一张旧邮票，把信重新封好。信寄出十天后，Friedrich Hofer来学校看Lena，带了一罐咸菜，站在门口没有进来。
  _effects: flag +letter_posted; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001620_

- **1924.01** `r14-edu.boarding-letter::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/wrote-and-hid-it` tags: education, boarding_isolation, letter, childhood, university, culture, schooling, student
  Lena在自习课上写信，写家里那条狗，写宿舍的冷，写Gustav Berg查房时手电筒的光。写到一半，Maria Bauer探头来看，Lena把纸捂住。信最后没有寄出去，折成小方块，塞在枕头套里，夜里翻身时能听见纸响。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +wrote_unsent_letter; skill_

- **1924.04** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, university, culture, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Luise Kral说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Friedrich Hofer来签字。Friedrich Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Lena把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.15000000000000002; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1924.05** `r3-teen.disability-at-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/workshop-track` tags: disability, school, injury, access, institution, university, culture, schooling
  校长把Friedrich Hofer叫来，说学校没有坡道，不如早点去刷坊学门手艺。Friedrich Hofer看着Lena的腿，点了头。第二天，Lena的课桌被搬到走廊尽头，桌上放着一张刷坊的地址。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +pushed_to_workshop_track; role; education apprenticeship_

- **1924.08** `p9-childhood-adolescence.courtyard-gang/gang-corner-turn-back` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Lena只把Mitzi送到巷口的煤油灯下就折回去。第二天在水泵边，Mitzi学Lena走路的样子，几个人跟着笑，Lena拎起水桶没有接话。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/teacher-explains` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena在办公室门口站到下课，才敢把作业本递进去。Luise Kral翻到错得最多的那一页，没有骂，只让Lena把题目再读一遍。读到第三遍，Lena忽然明白自己一直看漏了条件。Luise Kral把红笔放下，说以后不懂就来。那天以后，Lena的作业本上多了几行红字，都是Luise Kral夜里改的。
  _effects: skill; relation colleague with person-luise-kral_

- **1925.04** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Lena在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Luise Kral跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Lena去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-luise-kral; education apprenticeship_

- **1925.05** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/unpaid-trial` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Maria Kral先说试三个月，不给工钱，只管一顿午饭。家里同意了，因为多一张嘴比多一双手更难。Lena每天最早到，最晚走，三个月后师傅才把契约拿出来。
  _effects: food; mood -0.040000000000000036; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:63; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1926.01** `r13-turn.school-leaving::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-leaving-age/straight-to-factory` tags: education, school, work, family, transition, reform, medical, schooling
  Luise Kral把Lena的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Lena。Lena进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Lena又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 8 -> 28; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/no-vacancy` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Lena问了三家印刷厂，得到的回答都是“今年不收学徒”。最后一家让他把名字写在纸角上，说有空缺再通知，但谁都知道那张纸会被压在账本下面。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; knowledge “今年印刷厂大多不收新学徒。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

- **1926.02** `p9-childhood-adolescence.borrowed-coin/coin-persuade-return` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Lena让Otto Kral趁没人看见把钱放回围裙口袋，Otto Kral犹豫了一刻钟还是照做了。结果口袋里的数目早被记下，钱多出来，Alois Lechner当晚把孩子骂了一遍，Lena也在自己家挨了几句。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; reputation -0.02; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1927.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/call-the-inspector` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  Lena趁Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien去喝咖啡，跑到街角的工厂检查所，把卸下来的护板指给Alois Weiss看。Alois Weiss第二天带着卷尺来了，在压印机旁边量了很久；Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien陪着笑，等门一关，就把Lena的工具从案子上扫进了木箱。
  _effects: relation employer with person-franz-lechner; reputation 0.03; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 28 -> 31; flag +yf18_reported_press; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Lena又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 31 -> 51; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1927.01** `r13-civ.first-aid-course::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/certificate-earned` tags: health, first_aid, course, skill, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Huber把一条绷带绕在Lena的小臂上，教怎么打结才不会勒住血管。Lena在同桌的手臂上练了三遍，最后一遍得到了点头。课程结束时，Elisabeth Huber发了一张油印证书，上面盖着红十字的章。
  _effects: flag +earned_first_aid_certificate; role; skill; knowledge “a bandage must be tight enough to hold but loose enough for ”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1927.10** `r2-leisure.cinema-fire::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::cinema-fire-reform/reported-blocked-fire-exit` tags: leisure, cinema, fire, panic, rescue, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena在检查那天看见消防水带被挪去压幕布，检查员一走，又挪了回去。Lena把这事告诉Marie Huber，Marie Huber说别多事，老板刚交了罚款。当晚电影放到一半，Lena一直盯着侧门，手心里全是汗。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; reputation 0.02; condition; flag +reported_blocked_fire_exit; relation friend with person-marie-huber; knowledge “the manager moved the fire hose back behind the screen”_

- **1927.11** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/borrow-notes-at-home` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  学费凑不出来，Lena就向同屋的学徒借笔记。晚上把灶台边的桌子擦干净，用铅笔头把讲义抄在旧账单背面，Luise Kral允他每两周交一次抄本抵作练习。抄到第三页时灯芯烧短了，字迹开始发颤。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1927.12** `r1-child.cuff-answers::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/classmate-reported-it` tags: exam, cheating, school, shame, numeracy, university, culture, schooling
  Elisabeth Bauer举报了Lena，说袖口上有字。Luise Kral把Lena叫到讲台前，让她把袖子翻过来。教室里安静得能听见煤炉噼啪响。Luise Kral没有记过，只把卷子撕了，让她重考一次。
  _effects: reputation -0.1; flag +caught_cheating; relation rival with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation colleague with person-luise-kral_

- **1928.01** `state_expire:apprenticeship-contract` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick, state:apprenticeship-contract
  Lena的「学徒契约」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:63; role_

- **1928.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/admitted-to-dormitory` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena把铁皮箱绑在自行车后座，骑了三十里到寄宿学校。舍监检查行李，把家里的腌菜罐没收，说会招老鼠。Lena在熄灯后偷偷把罐子从垃圾堆捡回来，藏在床底，第一顿饭就着腌菜吃完了两碗粥。第一个星期Lena把腌菜分给同屋，换回三张邮票和一支不漏水的钢笔。
  _effects: flag +boarding_school_admitted; education secondary_

- **1928.03** `ld18-letter.schoolbook::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-letter-changes-hands` tags: love, letter, school, secret, queer, courtship, secret_intimacy, reform
  Lena把写给Mitzi的信折成四折，夹在拉丁文课本第三百页。第二天，书还回来时，页边多了一行铅笔字：“星期四，图书馆关门前。”两个人在书架之间站得很近，谁都没有先提那封信。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:143; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +letter_in_the_schoolbook; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

- **1928.10** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/quoted-without-permission` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Lena的信寄出半年，没有回音。后来Mitzi Bauer在一篇文章里引用了这封信，没有署名，只写一个读者说。Lena在报上读到自己的句子，先是高兴，随后发现文章里的意思和自己写的相反。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.03; flag +letter_quoted_without_permission; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; knowledge “the quoted sentence means the opposite of what was written”_

- **1929.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Lena最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Lena把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Lena说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 15 -> 21; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 55 -> 52; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.boarding-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/homesick-return` tags: education, boarding_school, residence, family, scholarship, university, culture, schooling
  Lena在寄宿学校住了不到一个月，夜里总听见火车声，想起家里的炉子和妹妹。Lena给Friedrich Hofer写信，写了撕、撕了写，最后只寄出一张明信片。Friedrich Hofer来接Lena那天，Lena的箱子还没完全打开。回到家，Lena把铁皮箱放在床底，再没打开，腌菜罐却一直留在桌上。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1929.03** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/offer-declined-black-humour` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, reform, medical, schooling, school, teacher
  Lena把入学通知折成纸船放进河里；家里少了一张嘴吃饭，也少了一个读书人。
  _effects: cash 10 (sold the textbooks instead); education none_

- **1929.03** `r2-uni.scholarship-renewal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-income-test/reform-neighbor-refuses` tags: education, scholarship, inspection, family, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  邻居不肯在收入表上签字，说“签了就得罪人”。Alois Weiss按规定把申请退回，Lena的奖学金停了一学期。Friedrich Hofer在楼道里骂了一句，邻居家的门始终没有开。Lena去教务处问能不能补交，窗口说“等下一年”。
  _effects: cash -6 (奖学金复核表); flag +scholarship_suspended_for_missing_testimony; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1929.09** `r3-teen.night-school-return::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/re-enrolled` tags: education, night_school, work, literacy, fatigue, reform, medical, schooling
  夜校点名时，Luise Moser念到Lena的旧名字，停了一下。Lena举起沾着机油的手，说想接着上。Luise Moser把半截粉笔递过来，让她在黑板上写自己的住址，写错了两个字母。
  _effects: flag +returned_to_night_school; skill; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; education secondary_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/a-form-with-an-empty-line` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  户籍窗口换了一张新表格，配偶一栏不能空着。Lena最后写了“同住人”，办事员抬头看了一眼，说这不作数。回家后，Lena把表格折成很小的一块，塞进针线盒；{link:beloved}问今天顺不顺利，Lena说和平时一样。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 21 -> 27; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 52 -> 49; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Anton Lechner把稿子退回来，夹着一张写得潦草的便条：细节好，人物像纸。Lena在电车上把便条读了两遍，回家后把稿子摊在桌上，从第一行开始改。改到天亮，只删掉一句。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +manuscript_rejected; skill; knowledge “the editor thought the characters were made of paper”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44_

- **1930.08** `vienna-market-debt.rent-arrears/rent-borrow-from-neighbor` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  看门人正把垃圾桶往院角推，见你回来，眼神在你和门牌之间转了一圈。房东收下四克朗，在账本上划掉一行，没提换锁的事。你上楼时腿有点发软，楼梯扶手凉得扎手。
  _effects: cash 4 (向邻居借来补交欠租); relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; mood 0.040000000000000036_

- **1930.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.closed` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Lena把窗关上，争吵声立刻变成闷响，像隔着一层棉被。可那男人的一句话还是从缝里钻进来，她躺到床上翻来覆去，数着墙上的水渍，到很晚才睡着。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:ld18-impossible-attachment/invented-anniversary/someone-asks-for-the-papers` tags: ld18-impossible-attachment, love, intimacy, queer, unregistered, commitment, secret, desire
  房东要续租，必须出示结婚证或亲属关系证明。Lena说{link:beloved}是表妹，房东却问为什么表妹的姓和籍贯都对不上。那天晚上，两个人把箱子重新打包，又把已经叠好的衣服一件件拿出来；谁也没有说下个月住哪里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 27 -> 39; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 49 -> 41; reputation -0.029999999999999995; mood -0.040000000000000036_

  排练到一半，有人提出只唱德语，另一边的成员把歌本合上。Luise Moser敲了敲谱架，说教堂不是议会。Hedwig站起来走了，Lena坐在原位，听见门在身后关上。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +choir_split_over_language; knowledge “half the choir wants German only and the other half refuses”_

- **1931.03** `hs18-queue.figure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/give-your-place` tags: historical_figure, queue, recognition, bread, witness, history, reform, medical
  队伍往前挪时，Lena认出前面那个把帽檐压低的人——报纸上见过那张脸，Arthur Schnitzler。对方手里也攥着一张配给证，指节冻得发白。Lena把自己的位置让出来，说自己去另一条队；Arthur Schnitzler点了一下头，没有报名字。
  _effects: state_add hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:221; relation acquaintance with person-arthur-schnitzler; flag +yielded_place_to_historical_figure_

- **1931.04** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Maria Kral的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Maria Kral提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

- **1931.07** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Lena自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: role; reputation 0.02_

- **1932.01** `ld18-intimacy.hairwash::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/a-knock-at-the-door` tags: love, intimacy, gentle, everyday, queer, body, secret_intimacy, reform
  头发刚擦到一半，Josef Winter来敲门收煤气钱。Lena把Hedwig推进壁橱，自己披上外套去开门；床上的湿毛巾还没来得及收。Josef Winter看了一眼，说下次记得把水倒了，楼梯会滑。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 39 -> 47; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.discretion 41 -> 46; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4_

  Lena和{link:beloved}把认识的那一天当作纪念日。没有登记，没有证人，只有一块从旧货摊买来的蛋糕和两杯兑了水的酒；Lena在日历上画了一个别人看不懂的记号，第二年又画在同一个格子里。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 45 -> 49; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 47 -> 44; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/blurs` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Lena忽然想不起那个人先迈的是左脚还是右脚。邻居说自己记得清清楚楚，还补了一个细节；Lena听着，竟分不清那是自己看见的，还是后来听来的。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 80 -> 72; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 20 -> 23; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1932.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert/p9-social-daily-culture.pictures-or-concert.box-seat` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:culture
  Lena咬牙买了音乐厅楼座的票，两克朗五十赫勒，又向人借来一件硬领衬衫，领子磨得后颈发红。演奏到慢板时，Lena手心里的汗把节目单边角浸软，却连咳嗽都憋着，生怕在周围体面人中间出声。散场后Emil等在门口，问值不值，Lena只把节目单折好收进口袋。
  _effects: cash -2.5 (音乐厅楼座票价); mood 0.07999999999999996; reputation 0.02; skill_

- **1932.02** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.pay` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena数出八赫勒交给Helene Winter，看她在蓝皮本上找到自家门牌，把这一周的格子划掉。Helene Winter说上个月隔壁巷子的人走了，公账出了棺材和一场小弥撒，剩下的钱记在册尾。楼梯口的灯很暗，Lena盯着自己的名字看了两遍才转身。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一周丧葬互助会会费); flag +funeral_society_member_

  _effects: cash -5 (分摊母亲药费中自己的一份（其中5克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912_

- **1934.01** `r13-edu.engineering-study::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university/bridge-holds-thirty-five-kilos` tags: education, engineering, university, model, calculation, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena用木条和铁丝搭了一座一米长的桥，加载到二十公斤时，中间开始下弯。Luise Kral让Lena再加一根斜撑，第二次加载到三十五公斤才断。Lena把断口收进盒子，毕业多年还留着，木条上写着当天的日期。盒子后来被Lena的孩子翻出来当玩具。
  _effects: flag +engineering_diploma; skill; education higher_

- **1934.07** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hire-neighbor-daycare` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Lena请楼下的Helene Stein白天来照看Friedrich Hofer，先付一周六克朗工钱，讲好每天另管两顿饭，煤钱也从这六克里出。Helene Stein上午换床单，下午喂药，临走把便盆提到楼道口。Lena下工回来先摸煤箱，再在练习本上记下当天的饭钱和煤钱，看见Friedrich Hofer的嘴唇不再干裂。
  _effects: cash -6 (支付邻居照看工钱和煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; health 0.04 (白天有人按时喂药和换洗)_

  Lena觉得一个人去不合适，绕到教堂请了修女一同上楼。修女看过病人，留下药包和一小瓶圣水，说会从教区账上出钱请医生来看一趟。Helene Winter在楼下等着，说这样妥当，病人家里没男人，多个见证人心里安稳。
  _effects: mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +parish_sick_visitor_

- **1935.09** `vienna-history-kraus-lecture/question-the-reader` tags: history, culture, karl_kraus, cafe_central, public_lecture, press
  提问时，Lena 问Karl Kraus 是否也会嘲笑普通读者的迟钝。Karl Kraus 盯着Lena 看了几秒，说“我先嘲笑自己，再嘲笑你们”；台下有人笑，也有人起身。Lena 没有等到散场，只把没喝完的咖啡钱压在杯底。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus; knowledge “Kraus 的公开朗读既是文学批评，也是当场点名新闻界的表演；被点名的人可能在场。”; cash -2 (coffee and admission to the reading); reputation -0.009999999999999995_

- **1936.01** `r13-edu.medical-study::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-university::reform-social-medicine/tenement-fieldwork` tags: education, medicine, university, anatomy, health, vienna, urban, crisis
  Lena在新设的市立医院实习，Luise Moser让Lena跟着去工人住宅区出诊。Lena第一次看见一家五口挤在一间屋里，孩子的咳嗽和墙上的霉斑连在一起。回校后，Lena把见闻写成报告，附上了通风改造的草图。那张图后来被贴在医院的走廊里。
  _effects: flag +social_medicine_fieldwork; knowledge “the tenement damp is linked to the children coughs”; education higher_

- **1936.03** `vienna-social-movement.recruitment/report-to-official` tags: development, social_movement, organization, politics
  Lena把传单交给楼里的门房。门房没有接，只让Lena自己去找警察局。那天傍晚，两个穿便衣的人在街口站了很久。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004_

- **1936.07** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.dock-morning/dock-watched-tools` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, dock
  河面的雾散尽时，码头上的人走了一半。Lena蹲在石阶上替Mitzi Lechner守着麻绳和铁钩，看装卸工在跳板上喊号子。汤送来时还烫嘴，豆子沉在碗底。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1936.10** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Katharina Lechner追出来问怎么不领了，Lena说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Lena把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000520:child; food_

  Lena用三个月把账本上的零碎支出并成四类，又停掉两份没有人看的简报。年底结账时，Gustav Berg发现不仅没有赤字，还剩下够换一批档案柜的钱；没有剪彩，只有仓库里新柜子的木头味。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.04; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +balanced_the_books; knowledge “停掉两份简报、合并零碎支出后，年底还剩一笔够换档案柜的钱。”_

  Lena在改革后的师范学校学新教学法，Luise Kral让Lena带学生去公园上自然课。Lena蹲在草地上教孩子认叶脉，一个男孩把毛毛虫放在Lena手背上。Lena没有甩开，就着那条虫子讲完了整节课，下课才发现自己跪得膝盖发麻。裤脚全是草屑。
  _effects: flag +reform_teacher_training; role; education higher_

- **1937.02** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Lena把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Anton Huber 2站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Lena手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。
  _effects: cash -2 (paper, ink and tram fare for the tenants meeting); role; flag +rent_strike; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-43; reputation 0.03_

- **1937.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money

  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  Lena发现自己搭在栏杆上的床单被人挪到了绳尾，角上沾了煤灰，一时火起，在楼梯间拦住Elisabeth Berg 3问是谁动的。Elisabeth Berg 3说是Elisabeth Bauer嫌滴水，两人越说越响，最后各自回屋摔上门，炉子空着，谁也没再去烧水。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-42_

  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79_

  _effects: cash -3 (牙医拔牙费用); health 0.05999999999999999 (拔除病牙解除疼痛); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  _effects: flag +factory_callback; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-88_

  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Franz Moser点了牛奶咖啡，Lena要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。
  _effects: cash -0.12 (自己那杯黑咖啡); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-103_

  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; skill; relation rival with person-anna-bauer; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”_

  账目连续第二年没有超支，Alois Huber在报告里加了一句“未发现异常”。Lena用结余给夜班守卫换了炉子，又把旧账本按年份装进箱子；没有人写报道，但来办事的人发现窗口开得比从前早。
  _effects: organization_funds; reputation 0.06; skill; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-79; flag +quiet_surplus; knowledge “审计报告里只有一句“未发现异常”，但窗口提前开门被办事的人记住了。”_

  _effects: organization_funds; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; flag +auditor_was_beneficiary; knowledge “审计员的哥哥就是去年那个顾问，报告最后一页没有签名。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -2 (coal tickets set aside for the neighbour nurse); food; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; role; flag +nurse_paid_in_bread_

  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品); health 0.05999999999999999 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +parent_hospitalized; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  两人拐进街角的咖啡馆，站到锌皮柜台前。Helene Berg 2点了牛奶咖啡，Lena要了黑咖啡，十二赫勒一杯。侍者把两杯推过来时，两人几乎同时把钱币按在柜台上，各付各的，谁也没说什么；那一声铜板响，把客气摆得比咖啡还清楚。

- **1947.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.sanatorium/home-rest` tags: health, medical, family, money
  安娜的汤锅外面包着旧毛巾，端进来时还烫手。Lena靠着枕头喝，喝一半咳一半。房东太太在走廊里故意大声说，肺病可不是闹着玩的，安娜探出头去，说医生讲了不传染。
  _effects: health 0.03 (在家休养但条件有限); food; cash -0.5 (房东要求的清洁费); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child_

- **1947.06** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.friend-intercession/intercession-too-far` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  Lena听到Franz Moser把工钱和家底夸过了头，脸沉下来，当晚就照地址找上门去解释。话到嘴边绕成一团，Berta Lechner的母亲把网兜往桌上一搁，说年轻人有话该早说，不该让旁人来传。Lena站在门里，名声在面包店门口折了一道，Berta Lechner始终没有出来。
  _effects: reputation -0.04_

- **1947.12** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Otto Kral 4一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Otto Kral 4的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113_

- **1948.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/subject_yields_line` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena看Otto Kral 4盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Otto Kral 4愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Lena门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Lena打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-113; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013089_

- **1948.01** `vienna-postwar-housing-1950s/private-sublet` tags: adulthood, postwar, housing, family, household
  Lena从报纸小广告上找到一间转租屋，付了三十先令“钥匙钱”。墙皮一碰就掉，冬天窗框漏风；Gustav Huber说这一带都这样，先住下再说。
  _effects: residence; cash -30 (key money and rent in advance); condition_

- **1948.01** `state_tick:hs18-street-witness/memory-drift/holds` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street
  Lena又把那天的顺序在脑子里走了一遍：先是门响，再是靴子，最后才是那声喊。细节还在，只是每讲一次，旁边就多出一点别人替你补上的颜色。
  _effects: state_parameter hs18-street-witness.certainty 61 -> 63_

- **1948.02** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Lena没有立刻答话，先去敲Ferdinand Weiss的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Ferdinand Weiss提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Lena把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1948.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.belt-patch` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Lena拆下一条旧皮带的头，蒙在鞋底破口上，用钉子一点点敲进去。第三下时锥子扎透鞋面，扎进左手拇指根，血滴在鞋帮上。她用围裙擦了擦，还是把皮子钉完了，第二天走路时鞋底有块硬响。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1948.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.food-only` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Lena只收下篮里的面包和豆子，把煤袋推回Josef手里，说房租自己再想办法，请她们给介绍点零工。Josef把煤放在走廊窗台，说礼拜四后院有人要拆旧木箱，一赫勒一捆，工钱不多但现结。Lena记下门牌，答应一早过去。
  _effects: food; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

- **1949.01** `state_expire:hs18-street-witness` tags: hs18-street-witness, witness, memory, history, testimony, rumor, risk, street

  _effects: state_remove hs18-street-witness state:world-vienna-1912:703_

- **1949.06** `p9-social-courtship-friendship.opened-letter/opened-letter-go-downstairs` tags: courtship, friendship, social, adulthood, reputation, leopoldstadt
  听了那些话，Lena坐不住，当晚走到Elisabeth Kral 2家楼下，请看门人把原信送上去，自己站在门洞里等。看门人下来时手里拿着那封信，信封还是拆开的，说楼上让有话当面讲，不隔着人传。Lena把帽子捏来捏去，与Hermann Stein 2之间也添了一层不自在。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1949.09** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Franz Bauer把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Lena把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Franz Bauer低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); flag +rent_increase_accepted_

- **1949.10** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/move-to-shared-tenement` tags: housing, home, health, family, household
  Lena把床架拆开，用绳子捆在借来的手推车上，搬进利奥波德城的合租房。押金和车钱花掉八克朗；新住处人多、墙薄，但至少每个人有一块能躺下的地方，Lena的腰在冷风里疼了一路，夜里却第一次不用侧着身睡。
  _effects: residence; cash -8 (deposit and cart); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-117_

- **1949.12** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.sunday-walk/part-at-arch` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family
  走到桥洞前，Elisabeth Kral 2说该往回走了，家里炉子还没生。Lena站在原地看着Elisabeth Kral 2拐上台阶，围巾的一角被风掀起来；下周的事谁也没有提，两人就此分开，口袋里只剩一颗凉透的栗子。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-146; flag +uncertain_courtship_

- **1950.01** `office16-corruption.inspection::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/cover-up` tags: office, corruption, audit, legal, power, dark, vienna, urban
  Lena请Helene Stein去咖啡馆，把一只没有标记的信封留在桌上。Helene Stein没有碰信封，只把报告里“重复报销”改成“记账口径不一”；作为交换，Lena签了Helene Stein侄子的实习证明。两个人都没有提那只信封后来去了哪里。
  _effects: reputation -0.02; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; flag +bribed_auditor; knowledge “审计报告里的“重复报销”被改成了“记账口径不一”，实习证明由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.04** `hs18-queue.refugee::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-university/give-a-false-address` tags: refugee, queue, station, papers, official, history, vienna, urban
  Lena把隔壁空屋的门牌号写在表格上。Alois Kral 2看了一眼，居然没有追问。Anna Stein千恩万谢，提着箱子往那条街走；Lena没有提醒那屋已经塌了半边，只把围巾拉高，转身去赶电车。
  _effects: knowledge “空屋的门牌号也能通过登记。”; flag +gave_false_refugee_address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

- **1950.07** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-argument` tags: faith, social, family, home

  Helene Stein把居留证翻到住址页，说章盖的是旧地址，和申报的不一样。Lena解释上个月搬了家，Luise Moser 2在门外探头，说合同是从月初算的。Helene Stein让Luise Moser 2写一张确认书，又让Lena按手印；章盖下去时，正好压住了旧地址的半行字。
  _effects: cash -5 (居留章盖在旧地址上); flag +residency_permit_renewed; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; knowledge “a landlord’s confirmation can correct the address on a resid”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

- **1951.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-female/public-suspicion` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, housing, lifecycle, partnership
  楼里的邻居开始议论Lena和Berta Lechner。有人把门牌记下来，房东来敲门，说这栋楼不接受“这种住法”；Berta Lechner把箱子拖出来又推回床下，搬家花掉五克朗，两人那一夜都没睡稳。Lena在院子里被人盯着看，门上的名字也被人用手指划过。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child; reputation -0.11999999999999998; cash -5 (moving and lost rent); role_

- **1951.01** `agency:support_process` tags: agency, agency:support_process

  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.02** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_burns_curtain` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Lena在公用灶台上热猪油，油星子溅到旁边晾着的床单上，火苗顺着床单爬上木隔板。Helene Stein 2拎起水桶泼过去，火灭了，但床单烧掉半幅，隔板熏黑一片。房东扣了那户人家两克朗押金，那家的女人在楼道里哭了一下午。
  _effects: cash -2 (赔给房东的隔板熏黑损失)_

- **1951.09** `vienna-housing-move.flat-viewing/flat-viewing-bed-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money, household
  看门人收下三克朗，把一把磨得发亮的钥匙交到Lena手里。厨房角落的炉子上还坐着别人的锅，水汽混着霉味。Lena把钥匙挂在脖子上，打算明日一早再回旧处取剩下的东西。
  _effects: cash -3 (先付一张床位的月租); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-137_

  Lena端着一只旧锅去敲Josef Berg的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Josef Berg让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Lena连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143; food; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-143_

  _effects: health 0.05 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_

  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; flag +permit_for_favor; knowledge “许可证少了一页排水图，盖章时没有补。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash 3 (洗衣房试工半天按件计酬); skill_

  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

  _effects: relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:003746:child; mood 0.050000000000000044_

  _effects: cash -3 (为平账先垫出去的钱); reputation -0.1; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001159:child; condition; flag +seal_used_by_trusted_hand; mood -0.06000000000000005_

  胜利一周年，广场上立起临时舞台，Grete Bauer 3被请去讲两句。他站了半天，只说“我回来的时候，村里少了十一个人”。台下有人鼓掌，也有人低头；Helene Stein赶紧接过话筒，说接下来是军乐队。

  Lena从肺结核中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

  Emil Huber 2在门口替Lena捏着帽子，听见里面一声闷哼。弗朗茨把牙扔进搪瓷盘，说瞧这牙根都黑了。收钱时他多要了二十赫勒，算明矾水的钱，说下次别再拖成这样。

  一年过去，Lena已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Lena也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。

  Lena的伤口在几天后肿起来，手腕发热。医生说要切开引流，费用比上次更高；Lena看着账单，先问能不能分两次付。
  _effects: cash -14 (drainage and medicine); condition_

  _effects: reputation -0.02999999999999997; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; flag +rival_audited; knowledge “委员会的差旅账目里有两次重复报销。”; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: cash -1 (职业介绍所登记费); mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1914.03** `iw18-infancy.first-word::ctx-vienna-1912::milieu-tenement/the-dead-grandmothers-name` tags: infancy, first_word, memory, family, wonder, eerie, vienna, urban
  Anton扶着桌沿站起来，指着门口的空处，清清楚楚叫出一个名字。屋里没人教过这个词；Friedrich Hofer手里的铝勺停在半空。那是去年冬天埋掉的老人的名字，窗外正好有送煤的车经过，铁轮压过石缝。
  _effects: mood -0.03; knowledge “孩子说出的第一个词，是去年死去的祖母的名字。”; flag +first_word_dead_name_

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/half-day-place` tags: childhood, school, fees
  学校只收半日班，Grete Hofer说下午还有空位，但课本要家里自己买。Josefine Hofer先交了一克朗，把Anton的名字写在候补名单上。
  _effects: education primary; cash -1 (partial school fee（其中1克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

  湿煤灰写出第一笔时，Anton还挺高兴，写到第三行，手一蹭就全花了。Friedrich Hofer看见了，把自己的旧手帕撕下一角，蘸水给木板擦了底。第二天在Leopoldstädter Volksschule门口，同学指着那块发黑的木板问是什么，Anton说是石板，对方笑得弯下腰。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  Grete Hofer让Anton站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Grete Hofer在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Anton把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  Anna Bauer把Anton拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Anton把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Anton蹲下把两包并排，按Anna Bauer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-anna-bauer; skill_

  Anton被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Anton一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Klara提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.049999999999999996; condition_

  教师儿子把Anton的橡皮切成两半，说穷人的橡皮本来就只有半块。Anton没有还手，把半块橡皮收进铅笔盒。第二天Anton发现自己的作业本被撕了两页，Grete Hofer问是谁干的，全班都看着窗外。
  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; flag +bullied_by_teachers_son; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912_

  “借你可以，拿劳动换。”Grete Hofer把旧石板推到桌沿，又指了指墙角的灰桶。Anton先去后院倒灰，风把炉灰吹回来，落在睫毛上。回来时Grete Hofer已经用粉笔在石板背面写了一个“借”字，说擦掉那天就是还石板的日子。

  晒衣绳上的水一滴一滴落在石板上。Anton跑到第二圈，鞋底一滑，膝盖先着地。Friedrich Hofer从屋里端出半盆清水，蹲下冲掉伤口里的沙，又用手帕扎紧，说今天别跑了。

  Anton把离校证明推回Luise Kral的桌面，说想再读一年，考商业学校。Luise Kral翻出旧课本和一张油印的考试科目表，书费要两克朗四十赫勒。家里那口锅还得照常烧，这笔钱最终是从母亲的首饰盒底抠出来的。
  _effects: cash -2.4 (商业学校考试用的旧课本与报名费（其中1.4克朗记入债务）); education primary; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1924.12** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/split` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, university, culture, schooling
  团里有人偷了市场的香肠，被抓住后供出了所有人。Maria Bauer把Anton拉到楼梯拐角，让他把责任推给最小的那个，说这样大家都能脱身。Anton没答应，只把口袋里的香肠纸揉成一团。第二天，屋顶上的折叠椅少了一把，Maria Bauer见了他也不再吹那两声口哨。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000003; flag +friend_group_split; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation_end friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1925.01** `task-vienna-youth-apprenticeship-search/no-vacancy` tags: task, task:work, work, adolescence, apprenticeship
  Anton问了三家印刷厂，得到的回答都是“今年不收学徒”。最后一家让他把名字写在纸角上，说有空缺再通知，但谁都知道那张纸会被压在账本下面。
  _effects: mood -0.04999999999999999; knowledge “今年印刷厂大多不收新学徒。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-5_

- **1925.03** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Anton把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Anton每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:59; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1925.03** `r14-edu.leaving-school::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/kept-home-for-kin` tags: education, school_dropout, child_labour, poverty, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Friedrich Hofer病倒在床，Anton留在家里看弟妹、煮稀粥、去井边打水。Luise Kral托人带来一包旧课本，Anton在灶火边翻了两页，弟弟哭了，只好合上。一个月后，学校把Anton的名字从名册上划掉，墨迹很淡。
  _effects: mood -0.06; flag +left_school_for_family; role_

- **1925.12** `vienna-adolescence.brothers-torn-jacket/sew-it-yourself` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  Anton在厨房找到一截粗线，颜色和外套差了一截。缝到一半，线打结，Anton用牙咬断，重新来。Friedrich Hofer坐在对面，没问外套的事，只把汤碗推过来，说“先吃”。Anton把最后一针打完，汤面已经结了一层薄皮。
  _effects: skill; food; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1926.01** `vienna-life-apprenticeship/kept-at-home` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship
  家里算了一笔账：学徒工钱扣掉车费和饭钱，剩不下多少。Anton留在家里接缝纫店的零活，顺便照看弟弟妹妹。
  _effects: role; cash 2 (home piecework)_

- **1926.01** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Anton翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Hedwig在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Anton翻回来时，Emil把一支烟塞进Anton嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 70; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 20; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 5; reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/answered-glance` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Anton在教堂门口或学校走廊里又遇见Mitzi。这一次对方先点了头，Anton准备好的整段话全忘了，只把手里的书换到另一只手上；回家后Anton才发现，自己把书拿倒了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 70 -> 75; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 28; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.reciprocated false -> true; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.02999999999999997_

  Maria Kral把材料推回来，说洗礼证明上的名字和户口册对不上，要先去找教堂更正。Anton跑了三趟，教堂的副本要两克朗。等证明补齐，名额已经发完。Anton学会在每张证明背后写下日期。
  _effects: cash -4 (copies and stamps for the corrected certificate（其中1.4克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.03; flag +scholarship_application_incomplete; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral; skill_

  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Anton和Elisabeth Bauer从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Berta Winter 2提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.04999999999999999; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.010000000000000002; relation friend with person-elisabeth-bauer; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1926.04** `kernel-edu.friendship::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-friend-moves-away` tags: adolescence, school, friendship, books, family, university, culture, schooling
  Mitzi的父亲在车站找到活，全家要搬去另一区。临走前，Mitzi把一本旧书塞给Anton，扉页上写着地址。Anton把书读完，按地址写了信，信被退回来，上面盖着“查无此人”。那本旧书Anton一直留在书架上。
  _effects: knowledge “朋友搬走前留下的地址已经找不到人了。”; skill; flag +friend_moved_away; mood -0.04999999999999999_

  _effects: education secondary; cash 20 (book grant after the appeal); role; flag +appeal_granted; reputation 0.06; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

  _effects: mood 0.02999999999999997; flag +learned_instrument_by_ear; skill_

  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +oral_exam_introduced; skill_

  _effects: food; mood -0.03999999999999998; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:111; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51; education apprenticeship; role_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Hedwig-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.03999999999999998; flag +burned_the_schoolbook_letter_

  Friedrich Hofer在饭桌上说，既然考不上，就去印刷所当学徒，至少能挣口饭。Anton第二天跟着Friedrich Hofer去见工头，围裙比身子大一圈。机器响起来时，Anton把师傅说的铅字字号记在手心，晚上洗手时全花了。第一个月工钱两克朗。

  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.02999999999999997_

  Anton的课程里多了公共卫生和统计，Luise Kral带全班去查自来水站。Anton在一条巷子里数出七个共用水龙头，把这个数字写进作业。报告交上去后，市政厅派人来装了两个新龙头，Anton特地跑去看了一遍。那条巷子的人从此叫她“数龙头的”。
  _effects: reputation 0.05000000000000001; flag +public_health_survey; knowledge “seven households shared one water tap in the lane”_

- **1932.02** `p9-gap-adult-unemployed.soup-kitchen/soup-got-scoop` tags: adulthood, unemployment, casual_labour, poverty, daily, relief, food, church
  风从教堂的拱廊里灌过来，队伍里没人说话，只听见罐子碰罐子的声音。Hedwig排在前面，领完没走，站在墙根等Anton，把自己的面包掰了一半递过去。
  _effects: food; health 0.03 (热汤下肚驱了寒气)_

- **1932.03** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Anton缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1932.08** `af17-luck.wallet-in-the-snow::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/the-wallet-belonged-to-the-police` tags: luck, money, street, chance, irony, daily, reform, medical
  Anton刚把钱包交给街角岗亭，Friedrich Stein 2就翻了翻里面的纸，问“你住哪条街”。钱包确实是失物，但失主是警局的人；Anton拿回一张收据，回家路上总觉得有人在后面跟着。
  _effects: reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; flag +returned_wallet_to_police; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80_

- **1932.11** `absurd15-stage.cast-the-king::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-government/cast-as-king` tags: performance, theatre, power, irony, work, pretend, reform, medical
  Friedrich Hofer把一顶掉漆的铅皮王冠扣在Anton头上，说不用演得像国王，只要像一个人突然被允许坐下。首演那天，Anton在台上忘了两句台词，观众却以为那是停顿。
  _effects: state_add stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:248; cash 2 (首演酬劳); role; reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-31; flag +stage_sovereign; reputation 0.04_

  Friedrich Stein 2把改好的歌词放在化妆镜前，钥匙换成了花环，部长换成了“某位先生”。Anton看完，把纸对折，放回镜子前，说今晚不唱了。Friedrich Stein 2骂Anton不识好歹，取消了下个月的合同；Anton从后门走出去，街上正在下雨，那顶铅皮王冠还在后台的帽盒里。
  _effects: cash -1 (取消合同损失的下月订金（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.04000000000000001; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-80; mood -0.020000000000000018; state_remove stage-sovereign state:world-vienna-1912:248; flag +refused_to_perform; flag -stage_sovereign_

  Anton被叫到学校，老师说Otto Huber这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Anton没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 23 -> 18; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 45 -> 50; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.030000000000000027_

  Mitzi Bauer 3把稿子退回来，夹着一张写得潦草的便条：细节好，人物像纸。Anton在电车上把便条读了两遍，回家后把稿子摊在桌上，从第一行开始改。改到天亮，只删掉一句。
  _effects: mood -0.019999999999999796; flag +manuscript_rejected; skill; knowledge “the editor thought the characters were made of paper”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1933.04** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  施特劳斯医生用酒精棉擦手，把药粉倒进小纸袋，折口时指甲掐出印子。他说夜里别再喝醋水，那东西烧胃。Alois Weiss把炉火拨旺，在灶上坐了一壶水，答应第二天替Anton去铺子里告假。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费（其中4克朗记入债务）); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1933.05** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.remake-coat/remake-coat.left-on-chair` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, mending, clothing, household
  Anton只把外套的里衬拆了一半，就搁在椅背上。冬天天短，她每天回来都看见那件敞着口子的外套，想着明天接着弄，明天又总是有别的事。到开春外套还搭在那儿，呢面上落了一层灰。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; flag +unfinished_coat_

- **1933.11** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/organize-relief-table` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics
  后屋的桌上摊着旧报纸，Anton用尺子画格子，一格一户，旁边写清煤价和面包房欠条。Josef Huber指着第三格说那家孩子咳了整夜，先划过去。蜡烛烧到桌沿时，名单终于能看懂了。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  Anton的毕业设计是一套工人住宅的采光方案，Luise Kral让Anton去实地量窗。Anton在巷子里蹲了三天，画出每户的日照时间，最后把图纸交上去，评审给了最高分，说这是唯一一份带着住户姓名的方案。评审把方案转给了市议会的一个委员会。
  _effects: reputation 0.06; flag +graduation_project_passed; education higher_

- **1934.02** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.window-argument/window-argument.nod` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, neighbor, overheard
  Anton在窗边听了一刻钟，只听出是工钱和面包账的事，最后把窗虚掩上睡了。第二天清早在楼梯口碰见那个女人，眼睛有点肿，两人错身时她点了点头，谁也没说话。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_downstairs_quarrel_

- **1935.01** `r14-edu.first-recital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/broken-string` tags: education, arts_training, music, performance, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Anton把琴谱翻到最后一页，琴弦突然断了一根，声音像鞭子。Maria Bauer上台把琴盖合上，说今天就到这里。Anton蹲下去捡断弦，手指被钢丝划破。Maria Bauer在幕布后面看着，没有出来。修琴八克朗，Anton分了三个月还。
  _effects: cash -8 (礼堂第三排的掌声（其中8克朗记入债务）); mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +instrument_broken_

  Anton把Otto Huber的旧课本包上书皮，又在本子上签了名。Otto Huber今年学会了自己系鞋带，也学会了在饭桌上说“不”；Anton听着，觉得屋子比去年更满。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 18 -> 26; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 50 -> 47; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  字牌贴在石灰墙上，最后两行他实在分不清。Gustav Berg用笔杆敲敲桌面，Alois Huber把名字写进另一本册子，说有缺再叫。Anton把证明一张张叠好，折边已经发软。
  _effects: mood -0.06000000000000005; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  Anton和Josef Huber在澡堂最里面的隔间交换了戒指，蒸汽把两个人的脸都熏红了。没有证婚人，只有一只漏水的铜龙头在滴答响；Josef Huber说这声音像教堂的钟，Anton说那就当它是。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:341; cash -1 (two rings and the bathhouse fee); mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +vow_in_the_steam; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

  _effects: cash 1 (卖掉顺走的帽子); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; condition; flag +burglary_mistaken_for_inspection; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  老师托人带来一张纸条，说Anna Huber这周主动擦了黑板。Anton把纸条压在桌玻璃下面；那张玻璃下面已经有三张纸条了。

  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.050000000000000044_

  第四天，Elisabeth Bauer的回信从门缝底下塞进来，纸边被门槛磨毛了。Elisabeth Bauer约Anton星期六早上在街角面包店门口见，说想买一个两赫勒的小圆面包，两个人分。Alois Weiss在楼道里撞见Anton读信，笑着问是不是好消息；Anton把信折回四折，塞进内袋贴着胸口。
  _effects: relation partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; mood 0.07000000000000006_

- **1936.01** `vienna-family-divorce/separate-under-one-roof` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle, separation
  Anton和Elisabeth Bauer把床铺分开，米和煤也各算各的。两人仍住在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang，因为谁都没有钱再租一间房；孩子在两床之间来回，三克朗买了隔开的锅碗和煤油，家里从此有两本账，夜里两盏灯先后熄灭。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person-elisabeth-bauer; mood -0.07000000000000006; mood -0.07999999999999996; cash -3 (separate household goods)_

  Josef Huber坐在床边，Anton用搪瓷盆替她洗头。水凉了又添，肥皂泡顺着耳后流下来；Josef Huber闭着眼说，小时候她母亲也是这样替她洗的。窗外有人收煤，铁锹刮过石板，两个人谁也没有动。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.devotion 60 -> 65; state_parameter ld18-impossible-attachment.pressure 25 -> 22; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +hairwash_sunday; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-65_

- **1914.03** `statistical_infection`

  _effects: reputation -0.08; flag +fought_teachers_son; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6_

- **1921.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/kept-home-for-coal` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig被Friedrich Hofer留在家里看炉子，因为煤只够烧到中午。Hedwig把弟弟绑在背上，隔着窗户听街对面学校传来的唱歌声，傍晚去厂门口捡煤核，换回两枚十字币。晚上Hedwig把捡来的煤核按大小排好，最大的留给明天早晨。手心全是黑灰。
  _effects: cash 1 (点名册上的第三个空位); mood -0.06; flag +kept_home_for_work; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1921.09** `vienna-childhood-friends.secret-kiosk/traded-for-picture` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  报亭后面的墙根下，Hedwig把咬了一半的糖递过去，换回一张火车头画片。糖纸被风吹到摊主的脚边。Josef Bauer弯腰捡起来，把画片从Hedwig手里抽走，说先把地扫了。Hedwig扫到墙根，Josef Bauer才把画片还回来。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Hedwig拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Ferdinand Bauer弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Hedwig接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

- **1922.03** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.win` tags: school, social, childhood
  拼写比赛结束时，Grete Hofer敲了敲讲台说“记住这个词”。Hedwig袖口上多了一枚小十字章，回家路上Mitzi追着要看，最后用三颗李子换了眼福。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; mood 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-6; food_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  Friedrich Hofer把空口袋翻给Hedwig看，里面只有线头和一枚扣子。Hedwig没再要，去木料堆挑了块没毛刺的薄板，用指头抹上湿煤灰，写一个字，抹一次。到Leopoldstädter Volksschule时木板边角已经把书包染黑，后排有人笑了一声，Hedwig把木板往桌肚里塞了塞。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.03999999999999998; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1924.01** `kernel-edu.appeal-exam-result::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-appeal-refused` tags: task, adolescence, school, appeal, official, university, culture, schooling
  申诉信退回来，Maria Kral在末尾盖了一个方章：维持原议。Luise Kral把信折好，说至少试过了。Friedrich Hofer把十五克朗那一行从本子上划掉，改成“明年再说”。放学后，Hedwig在教室里多坐了一会儿。
  _effects: cash -3 (stamped copies and fees（其中3克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.03; flag +appeal_refused; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; relation kin with person-friedrich-hofer; mood -0.06_

- **1925.01** `major-education-scholarship-examination/full-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, adolescence, major
  考试设在女子中学的二层教室。Luise Kral当着Hedwig的面拆开牛皮纸袋：一篇作文、两道算术、一段听写。Hedwig写到最后一个数字时，手心里全是墨。三周后学校寄来通知，学费全免，首年发三十克朗书籍费，余下按学期发；条件是期末平均分不得低于良。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 30 (annual scholarship grant); role; flag +lyceum_scholarship; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1925.01** `r13-edu.library-study::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/inventory-closed` tags: education, library, study, reading, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig赶到时，阅览室门口挂着“盘点停开”的牌子。Hedwig在台阶上坐到天黑，把带来的笔记背了两遍，回家时鞋底湿透，第二天发起低烧。低烧退了以后，Hedwig再去，门口换了新牌子：阅览室每天只开三个钟头，Hedwig把借书证收进抽屉，等秋天再来。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1925.04** `r15-work.master-takes-on::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signed-contract` tags: work, craft, apprentice, trade, poverty, reform, medical, schooling
  Alois Weiss把学徒契约摊在桌上，指给Hedwig看期限、工钱和出师考试。Hedwig签了名，第二天站到刨床前，手指上多了第一道木刺。
  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:62; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38; education apprenticeship; role_

- **1925.05** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rejected` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把叠成方块的纸条塞给Helene Moser，第二天在课桌里找回同一张纸，边角压平了，字没有被打开过。放学时Helene Moser从另一边走了，Hedwig坐在电车上，一直坐到终点站。
  _effects: mood -0.16000000000000003; flag +first_love_rejected; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1925.09** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/keep-it-in-the-satchel` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Hedwig把手套塞进书包最里层，回家后放在枕头下面。第二天上学，Hedwig每隔一会儿就摸一下书包带，像在确认一件不能给家里看见的东西；手套上还留着电车木座的气味。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:61; flag +yf18_kept_glove; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1926.01** `yf18-friend.two-tickets::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/confront-at-the-cloakroom` tags: first_love, friendship, betrayal, dance, tickets, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig在衣帽间堵住Mitzi，问那两张票怎么到了Helene Moser手里。Mitzi说Hedwig从来没敢开口，自己只是替Hedwig把话说了；Hedwig把票根揉成一团，塞进Mitzi的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 60 -> 65; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 0 -> 10; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 5 -> 0; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; reputation -0.020000000000000004; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/talked-about` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Hedwig只把Mitzi的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Hedwig是不是要请人喝咖啡；Hedwig说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 15 -> 27; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 15 -> 9; reputation -0.030000000000000006; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/learned` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又学会了一道工序。师傅没有夸，只把下一块料推到面前。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1926.01** `r13-edu.choose-subjects::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university::reform-curriculum-choice/science-lab-opens` tags: education, curriculum, tracking, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig赶上课程改革，学校第一次开出自然科学实验课。Luise Kral带着全班用借来的天平称矿石，Hedwig把每次读数都记在纸上，回家用母亲的秤复核，发现天平不准，第二天在课上指了出来。下课后Luise Kral把天平留下，让Hedwig再称一遍，说仪器不会替人撒谎。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +reform_science_track; skill_

- **1926.02** `yf18-dare.prater-slide::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/ride-in-the-dark` tags: dare, prater, fairground, comic, friendship, reform, medical, schooling
  普拉特的木滑道在闭园后还亮着一盏值班灯。Hedwig和Emil从围栏底下钻进去，用一块麻袋垫着滑下来，木槽里的蜡味呛得人直笑。滑到底时，Gustav Winter提着灯站在出口，问他们要不要再买一张票。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; cash -1 (a late ticket paid to the watchman); reputation -0.009999999999999998; flag +yf18_prater_stunt; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

- **1926.04** `vienna-youth-threshold.night-class/give-up-for-overtime` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, transition
  工头说这个月赶印一批账簿，晚上加班每小时多给二十赫勒。Hedwig站在教室门口听完课程表，转身回厂里搬纸。Luise Kral追出来说座位可以保留两周，Hedwig点点头，手已经摸上了作坊的门把，夜里机器的声音把课本的事盖了过去。
  _effects: cash 1.6 (夜间加班多挣的工钱); skill; mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1926.12** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Hedwig翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Rosa在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Hedwig翻回来时，Helene Moser把一支烟塞进Hedwig嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 65 -> 75; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 0 -> 15; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; reputation 0.02; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.050000000000000044_

- **1927.01** `yf18-apprentice.press-guard::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/tie-the-sleeve-and-work` tags: apprenticeship, work, danger, machine, injury, refusal, reform, medical
  Hedwig把袖口卷到肘上，用麻绳扎紧，替Magistrat der k. k. Reichshaupt- und Residenzstadt Wien把一叠纸送进压印机。前两百张很顺，第二百零一张时，纸角卡住，Hedwig伸手去拨，机器没有停；等Mitzi Lechner拉开Hedwig，食指的指甲已经翻了起来。
  _effects: condition; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 30 -> 22; cash -2 (a doctor and a bandage); flag +yf18_press_injury; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1927.01** `state_expire:yf18-first-awakening` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Hedwig的「第一次心动」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:61; flag -yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1927.01** `state_tick:apprenticeship-contract/workshop-year/mistake` tags: apprenticeship-contract, education, craft, apprentice, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig废掉了一块料，师傅扣了工钱。晚饭时谁也没提这件事。
  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 0 -> 1; cash -1 (wasted material); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1927.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.trade-school-application/missing-certificate` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  Luise Kral用指甲刮了刮毕业证上的印章，说这印子模糊得像被水泡过。Hedwig把课程表折好放进口袋，走出教室时听见身后有人问下一个。
  _effects: cash -5 (报名手续费与押金); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +needs_school_certificate; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1927.02** `r1-fam.sibling-rivalry::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/rivalry.lost` tags: family, siblings, rivalry, apprenticeship, craft, reform, medical, schooling
  Mitzi Hofer 2收了Lena。Hedwig在门口站到天黑，回家把父亲的工具箱打开，照着记忆里的样子练。Friedrich Hofer进来，说别跟你兄弟争，争来的东西不长久；Hedwig没有抬头，刨花落在脚背上，像一层薄雪。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +lost_apprenticeship_to_sibling; role; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1927.02** `r3-teen.first-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/distributes` tags: politics, pamphlet, print, police, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把油印纸折成四折，塞进外套内袋，在工厂换班时一张张递出去。有人接了就走，有人揉成团扔在地上。一个老工人把纸展开，读了两行，塞进帽檐里，说这东西比面包顶饱。
  _effects: mood 0.05999999999999994; flag +distributed_political_pamphlet; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; organization_membership; knowledge “workers accept the pamphlets at the factory gate”_

- **1927.07** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/name-missing` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  补考那天，Hedwig在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Luise Moser跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Hedwig去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; education apprenticeship; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  教堂年历的订单催得紧，Leopoldstädter Volksschule把压印机的护板卸了，说这样进纸快一半。Hedwig看着那块铁板，说手会进去；Leopoldstädter Volksschule把工钱袋往桌上一扔，问Hedwig是不是不想出师了。Hedwig没有碰机器，那天下午的活由Maria Bauer接手。
  _effects: relation employer with person-franz-lechner; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 22 -> 17; state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.failures 1 -> 2; flag +yf18_refused_unguarded_press; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Hedwig交不起住宿费，校长让Hedwig每天清早去厨房帮工抵账。Hedwig四点起来削土豆，手上冻出裂口，上课时打瞌睡被Luise Moser点名。期末，Hedwig的操行栏写着“勤勉”，成绩却掉到中游。第二学期Hedwig的操行还是“勤勉”，成绩单寄回家，家里只看了学费那栏。
  _effects: cash -3 (寄宿学校的铁皮箱（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; education secondary_

  Hedwig把写给Lena的信折成四折，夹在拉丁文课本第三百页。第二天，书还回来时，页边多了一行铅笔字：“星期四，图书馆关门前。”两个人在书架之间站得很近，谁都没有先提那封信。
  _effects: state_add ld18-impossible-attachment state:world-vienna-1912:140; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +letter_in_the_schoolbook; flag +impossible_attachment_active_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.04; flag +transferred_to_infantry_training; role_

- **1929.01** `yf18-friend.inkwell::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/take-the-blame` tags: exam, cheating, friendship, betrayal, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig说公式是自己写的，Anton只是看见了。Luise Kral没收了Hedwig的钢笔，在成绩册上记了一笔；Anton放学后在校门口等Hedwig，递过来一块用纸包着的巧克力，Hedwig没有接，只把书包带往肩上提了提。
  _effects: reputation -0.05; relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; flag +yf18_took_blame; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1925.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍从面包房门口拐进巷子，Katharina Lechner把布袋夹在腋下，学着前面人的样子，用胳膊肘顶住后面人的篮子。窗口的伙计舀面粉时，Katharina Lechner踮脚看着秤，直到半袋面倒进袋口。挤出人群时鞋跟被踩掉，Katharina Lechner单脚跳了两步，在泥里摸回鞋，索性赤脚走回家。
  _effects: food; health -0.020000000000000018 (赤脚走石板路磨伤脚底); skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1925.02** `iw18-friend.chair-by-the-stove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/a-guest-named-by-the-child` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, family, wonder, reform, medical
  Katharina Lechner从碗橱里多拿了一只缺口的盘子，摆在灶边那把没人坐的椅子上，还认真地告诉屋里的人：今天有客人。盘子里放着一小块土豆和一片面包边。Alois Lechner问客人叫什么，Katharina Lechner说了一个从没听过的名字。
  _effects: state_add iw18-imaginary-friend state:world-vienna-1912:67; food; mood 0.04; flag +invisible_guest_named; flag +imaginary_friend_active_

- **1925.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.hidden-book/book-borrowed-and-returned` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  书在Katharina Lechner的书包里藏了三天，课间才敢翻两页。还书那天，Katharina Lechner把书角淋皱的事先说了，又把书页一张张抚平。Luise Kral翻到最后一页，看了看书脊，说下次别带进院子，书却还是借给了Katharina Lechner。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1925.05** `iw18-secret.cellar-door::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/saw-the-bundle` tags: childhood, secret, family, cellar, knowledge, fear, reform, medical
  地窖门虚掩着，Katharina Lechner下去找腌菜坛，看见Alois Lechner蹲在煤堆后面，把一包用油纸裹好的东西塞进墙缝。Alois Lechner听见脚步，回头时脸上还带着灰；Katharina Lechner说自己只是来拿坛子，声音比平时高。
  _effects: state_add iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:68; knowledge “大人在酒窖的墙缝里藏了一包用油纸裹着的东西。”; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.020000000000000004; flag +saw_cellar_bundle; flag +child_secret_active_

- **1925.12** `iw18-play.kite-on-roof::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-kite-is-lost` tags: childhood, play, kite, roof, neighbor, comic, reform, medical
  风把风筝往屋脊另一边推，竹骨卡在两片瓦之间。Katharina Lechner和Elisabeth Lechner仰头看到脖子发酸，最后只看见一截纸尾巴在烟里抖。Elisabeth Lechner说再糊一只，Katharina Lechner说纸不够了；两个人蹲在墙根，把剩下的线绕回木轴上。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000006; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child; flag +kite_lost_on_roof_

- **1926.01** `iw18-friend.takes-the-blame::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-knows` tags: childhood, imaginary_friend, blame, family, secret, small_cruelty, reform, medical
  Berta Lechner从门后探出头，说看见是Katharina Lechner自己碰倒的。Katharina Lechner赶紧把Berta Lechner拉到楼梯间，答应把攒了半年的彩色玻璃珠分一半。Berta Lechner想了一会儿，说还要替自己写一页大字；两个人在楼梯上坐了很久，牛奶在厨房里慢慢渗进砖缝。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 25 -> 30; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child; cash -1 (milk lost on the kitchen floor); flag +sibling_keeps_friend_secret; mood -0.020000000000000004_

- **1926.01** `iw18-friend.sugar-cube::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-cube-disappears` tags: childhood, play, imaginary_friend, food, family, comic, reform, medical
  Katharina Lechner把家里最后一块方糖放在空椅子旁边的碟子里，说是给朋友留的。下午糖不见了，Katharina Lechner一口咬定是朋友吃了；晚上Elisabeth Lechner从口袋里摸出黏着糖渣的纸，笑得直不起腰。Katharina Lechner追着Elisabeth Lechner绕了三圈桌子。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 60 -> 64; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.secrecy 30 -> 33; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child; mood 0.020000000000000004_

- **1926.01** `state_reaction:iw18-child-secret/a-school-note-reminds` tags: iw18-child-secret, childhood, secret, knowledge, guilt, family, fear, state
  Katharina Lechner把老师要家长签字的纸条折成四折，忽然想起自己还有一件事没有告诉家里。纸条在口袋里放了一路，边角被手指捏得发亮。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 35 -> 39; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 50 -> 52_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:002414_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:iw18-imaginary-friend/friend-year/fading` tags: iw18-imaginary-friend, childhood, play, imaginary_friend, solitude, imagination, secret, state
  Katharina Lechner这一年很少再和那把椅子说话。学校里有了新的同桌，放学后要在楼梯间跳格子；有一天Katharina Lechner把书包扔在椅面上，才想起自己已经三天没有给朋友留位置。椅面凉得很快，像一块普通木头。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.closeness 64 -> 56; state_parameter iw18-imaginary-friend.boldness 40 -> 38; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.01** `state_tick:iw18-child-secret/secret-year/held` tags: iw18-child-secret, childhood, secret, knowledge, guilt, family, fear, state
  这一年Katharina Lechner把秘密藏得更深。走过地窖门口时，Katharina Lechner会故意哼歌；饭桌上有人提起那件事，Katharina Lechner就把汤勺在碗底转三圈。秘密没有变大，却像一颗卡在鞋里的石子，走每一步都知道它在。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 52 -> 56; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 39 -> 42; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1926.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-twice-cleaning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  错题纸被Katharina Lechner攥出了汗印。Luise Kral看了一眼，说留两次可以，先把炉灰倒了。灰桶提到后院时，风从巷口灌进来，灰扑了Katharina Lechner一脸。Luise Kral回来没催，用粉笔在黑板上把一道竖式拆成三步，让Katharina Lechner跟着写在自己的石板边上。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; health -0.020000000000000018 (倒炉灰吸入灰尘)_

- **1926.08** `iw18-secret.sibling-bargain::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-sibling-tells` tags: childhood, secret, sibling, bargain, family, small_cruelty, reform, medical
  Elisabeth Lechner没有要珠子，转身就去找大人，说Katharina Lechner一定在地窖里看见了什么。Katharina Lechner被叫到厨房时，大人正在擦一只已经擦干净的碗；那只碗在手里转了很久，谁也没有先提地窖。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 55 -> 50; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.discovered false -> true; relation kin with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child; mood -0.05; flag +sibling_told_the_secret_

- **1926.12** `r1-fam.childcare-burden::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/childcare.inspector` tags: family, childcare, childhood, school, exhaustion, reform, medical, schooling
  区里的视察员来了，看见Katharina Lechner背着孩子在井边打水，问为什么不去上学。Luise Kral说家里没有别人；视察员在簿子上写了一行字，说下个月再来。Helene Winter后来告诉Luise Kral，那行字的意思是“家务缠身”，不算旷课。
  _effects: flag +school_board_notice; role; relation acquaintance with person-helene-winter; knowledge “视察员写的是“家务缠身”，不算旷课。”; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35_

- **1927.01** `vienna-life-school-day/hungry-in-class` tags: childhood, school, hunger, injury
  黑板上的字忽然变成一片白，Katharina Lechner扶住桌沿才没栽下去。Luise Kral让去走廊坐着，过了一会儿递来半块面包；面包很干，Katharina Lechner嚼得很慢。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (went to school hungry); condition; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1927.01** `iw18-secret.confession::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-child-cannot-say-it` tags: childhood, secret, confession, school, relief, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Lechner在空教室里坐到天快黑，Luise Moser批完一摞本子，问是不是有话要说。Katharina Lechner摇头，说只是等雨停。Luise Moser把伞递过来，Katharina Lechner接伞时忽然说“地窖”，又立刻改口说“地窖里有老鼠”。Luise Moser看了孩子一眼，没有再问。
  _effects: state_parameter iw18-child-secret.tellUrge 42 -> 48; state_parameter iw18-child-secret.weight 56 -> 59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; mood -0.03; flag +confession_stuck; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  两个孩子在院子两头跑，一个递，一个夹，床单在风里鼓起来。最后一条挂好，Franz Lechner从厨房拿出面包，先给了自家孩子，再掰一块给Katharina Lechner。Katharina Lechner把面包塞进围裙口袋，回家后放在家里人的碗边，说自己在外面吃过了。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-franz-lechner; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-48_

- **1927.11** `vienna-childhood-friends.courtyard-race/fell-on-wet-stones` tags: childhood, social, school, street, injury
  Katharina Lechner想从床单底下钻过去，脚下一滑，膝盖磕在石板棱上。Ferdinand Weiss听见哭声出来，先看了看伤口，又回去端水。手帕扎得很紧，Katharina Lechner试着站起来，被Ferdinand Weiss按回台阶上。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1927.12** `lc17-prodigy.noticed::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/signs-the-card` tags: childhood, prodigy, talent, school, music, family, promise, reform
  老师在Katharina Lechner的练习本背面画了五条线，说手指还要再长一年。第二天，Luise Moser把一张写着“每周三次”的纸条交给Katharina Lechner家里的大人，学费按季度付，先交一半。Katharina Lechner把纸条折成小船，放在窗台上，直到墨迹被煤气灯熏黄。
  _effects: state_add lc17-child-prodigy state:world-vienna-1912:113; cash -1 (the first quarter of music lessons); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; skill; flag +prodigy_lessons; mood 0.03; flag +child_prodigy_

- **1928.01** `lc17-prodigy.recital::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-dog-howls` tags: childhood, prodigy, performance, practice, family, shame, music, reform
  曲子拉到第二段，窗外的狗跟着调子嚎起来，先是低一声，后来索性趴在门口长嚎。客人笑，主人也笑，Katharina Lechner干脆把曲子改成进行曲，狗却跟不上了。Luise Kral事后说，至少这一次，没有人睡着。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 35 -> 38; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 70 -> 75; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 55 -> 53; mood 0.03; reputation -0.010000000000000002; cash 1 (the amused host still paid for the evening); flag +dog_howl_recital; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:lc17-child-prodigy/practice-year/keeps-practicing` tags: lc17-child-prodigy, childhood, promise, talent, pressure, family, education, prodigy
  Katharina Lechner今年自己把练习时间从半小时加到三刻钟。琴谱的边角被翻得起毛，厨房里有人听见同一段反复了七遍，最后一遍终于没有错音；Katharina Lechner停下来，才发现窗外天已经黑了，煤气灯在玻璃上照出一小块黄色的自己。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 53 -> 56; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 75 -> 79; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 38 -> 36; skill; mood 0.01999999999999999_

  _effects: state_remove iw18-child-secret state:world-vienna-1912:68; flag -child_secret_active_

- **1928.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Katharina Lechner的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Katharina Lechner在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1928.02** `r1-child.pencil-betrayal::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement::reform-police-school/denounced-to-police` tags: friendship, betrayal, theft, school, shame, reform, medical, schooling
  新校规说偷窃要记入品行册，还要通知警察。Elisabeth Lechner的父亲是巡警，当天就来了学校。Elisabeth Lechner在办公室里改口，说Katharina Lechner逼Elisabeth Lechner偷的。Katharina Lechner看着窗外的警帽，一句话也没说。
  _effects: reputation -0.14; flag +denounced_to_police_by_friend; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child; relation_end friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child_

- **1928.08** `vienna-school-childhood.fountain-pen/fountain-pen.broken` tags: school, social, childhood
  Luise Kral把账单翻了个面，上面全是数字。Katharina Lechner没再问，回家用线把旧笔尖绑在木杆上。第二天墨水从裂缝渗到指缝，Luise Moser把本子推回来让重写。
  _effects: mood -0.05000000000000002; relation kin with person-luise-kral; skill_

- **1928.08** `p9-childhood-household.water-and-coal/helped-neighbor-for-bread` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  煤球滚到楼梯口，Katharina Lechner一颗颗追回来，膝盖在台阶上磕了一下。Friedrich Hofer扶着门框说够了够了，Katharina Lechner还是把最后一颗放进筐里。老人塞来一块面包，Katharina Lechner边跑边咬了一口，到Leopoldstädter Volksschule时嘴角还沾着煤灰。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; reputation -0.01999999999999999_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.music-lesson::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/muted-practice` tags: education, music, lesson, craft, culture, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Lechner在阁楼练琴，楼下邻居上来敲门，说孩子要睡觉。Katharina Lechner把弱音器夹上，声音闷得像蚊子。练到半夜，Katharina Lechner索性放下琴，把谱子默写了一遍，第二天在课上凭记忆拉完，Luise Moser问Katharina Lechner是不是没睡好。第二天Katharina Lechner把弱音器取下来，换到更远的阁楼。
  _effects: mood -0.03999999999999998; skill_

- **1929.08** `r3-teen.first-friend-group::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/split` tags: friendship, roof, belonging, betrayal, class, reform, medical, schooling
  团里有人偷了市场的香肠，被抓住后供出了所有人。Mitzi把Katharina Lechner拉到楼梯拐角，让她把责任推给最小的那个，说这样大家都能脱身。Katharina Lechner没答应，只把口袋里的香肠纸揉成一团。第二天，屋顶上的折叠椅少了一把，Mitzi见了她也不再吹那两声口哨。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; flag +friend_group_split; relation rival with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  Luise Kral把Katharina Lechner的名字从升学名单上划掉，说厂里正缺人手。家里没有人争，只把午饭里的半块面包塞给Katharina Lechner。Katharina Lechner进了纺织厂，工牌上写着“临时”，每天站十二个钟头，月底工钱比学徒多两克朗。
  _effects: cash 2 (毕业钟响过以后); health -0.06000000000000005 (毕业钟响过以后); condition; flag +left_school_for_factory; role_

  _effects: relation employer with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; state_add apprenticeship-contract state:world-vienna-1912:191; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; education apprenticeship; role_

  冬天屋里冷，Katharina Lechner把手指压在暖气片上，等关节活动开才肯碰琴键。老师上周留下的曲子还停在第三页；Katharina Lechner听见楼下孩子在雪地里喊，便把琴盖合上，坐到窗边去看，直到手指重新变凉。
  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 36 -> 42; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 79 -> 73; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 56 -> 54; mood -0.03999999999999998_

- **1930.01** `p9-childhood-apprenticeship.cover-for-sibling/damp-linen-returned` tags: adolescence, work, apprenticeship, labour, training, family, task, task:family
  Katharina Lechner解释说路上没下雨，是床单叠得太厚。客户不听，Mitzi Bauer 3也没帮腔，只把湿床单抖开，让Katharina Lechner自己晾。Katharina Lechner把布角一寸寸翻过来，跟Mitzi Bauer 3说以后会分开叠、不让潮气闷在里头。Mitzi Bauer 3哼了一声，算是记下了。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (蹲久了膝盖发酸，又吹了晚风); mood -0.03999999999999998; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1930.05** `yf18-firstlook.tram-glove::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/let-friend-return-it` tags: first_love, love, tram, glove, youth, courtship, reform, medical
  Katharina Lechner把手套交给Berta Lechner，让Berta Lechner替自己去还。Berta Lechner回来时说，Friedrich Winter问是谁捡到的；Berta Lechner顺口报了一个名字，Katharina Lechner听见那名字不是自己的，笑得比平时响。
  _effects: state_add yf18-first-awakening state:world-vienna-1912:190; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000405:child; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +yf18_first_awakening_active_

- **1930.10** `vienna-adolescence.school-or-brickworks/go-to-brickworks` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social, injury
  “下周一，跟街尾的工头走。”Alois Lechner说完就把算术本推到桌角。Katharina Lechner知道再争也没用。Luise Moser在门口拦住Katharina Lechner，把一张夜校地址塞过来，说晚上可以去。Katharina Lechner把纸条折进鞋垫，第二天就去了砖厂。头一周四克朗，搬砖搬得腰直不起来，纸条在鞋里磨得发毛。
  _effects: role; cash 4 ({subject}砖厂首周工钱); health -0.030000000000000027 (砖厂头几天搬砖); flag +has-evening-school-address; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:yf18-first-awakening/half-year-of-glances/rehearsed-conversation` tags: yf18-first-awakening, love, youth, first_love, desire, school, awakening, state
  Katharina Lechner在厨房里对着镜子练了七遍开场白，第二天却在离Friedrich Winter三步远的地方拐进面包店。Katharina Lechner买了一袋并不需要的盐，回家路上把开场白又想了一遍；这一次，连标点都更熟练了。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 45 -> 51; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 15 -> 18; mood 0.010000000000000009_
