  _effects: state_parameter apprenticeship-contract.progress 10 -> 30; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1931.01** `r14-edu.tutoring-sibling::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/sibling-resented-drilling` tags: education, tutored_sibling, family, tutoring, reform, medical, schooling, home
  Elisabeth Lechner把铅笔一摔，说再也不想看见豆子。Katharina Lechner把豆子一颗颗收进碗里，说随你。那天晚上，Elisabeth Lechner自己把错题改了一遍，改完把本子推到Katharina Lechner面前，不说话。Katharina Lechner看了一遍，在页角画了一个很小的勾。
  _effects: flag +sibling_resented_tutoring; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000618:child_

- **1931.04** `yf18-dare.bridge-rail::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/walk-the-rail` tags: dare, bridge, river, friendship, risk, reform, medical, schooling
  多瑙河上的铁桥在夜里只剩两排灯。Katharina Lechner翻过栏杆，鞋底踩上结霜的钢梁，走了七步；Berta Lechner在桥头喊了一声，风把声音吹散。Katharina Lechner翻回来时，Elisabeth Lechner把一支烟塞进Katharina Lechner嘴里，手抖得点不着火。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.intensity 35 -> 45; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 10 -> 25; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 10 -> 15; health -0.030000000000000027 (cold and fear on the bridge); reputation 0.01999999999999999; flag +yf18_walked_bridge_rail; mood 0.04999999999999999_

- **1931.04** `r2-faith.parish-school-fee::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/free-place-granted` tags: faith, school, charity, education, fees, reform, medical, schooling
  Hermann Stein 2在学费簿上找到Katharina Lechner的名字，用红笔在旁边写了“免”。Luise Moser说课本还得自己买，Hermann Stein 2就从柜子里翻出一本旧算术，封面写着上一个学生的名字。Katharina Lechner把书抱回家，Alois Lechner用报纸包了书皮，说念完这本再说。
  _effects: flag +received_free_parish_school_place; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; knowledge “the parish keeps a red-ink list of fee exemptions”; education primary; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

  补考那天，Katharina Lechner在考场门口等到钟响，监考说名单上没有名字。Luise Kral跑去教务处，回来时手里只有一张转学建议。Katharina Lechner去了印刷厂当学徒，师傅说铅字比字母好认，至少不会骗人。
  _effects: condition; flag +left_school_after_exam_failure; relation colleague with person-luise-kral; education apprenticeship_

  考试周里，Katharina Lechner把课本摊在桌上，眼睛却一直看着窗外那条电车线。笔记的页边多了几个没有写完的名字，Katharina Lechner用橡皮擦掉，纸面起了毛。

  Katharina Lechner在衣帽间堵住Berta Lechner，问那两张票怎么到了Elisabeth Lechner手里。Berta Lechner说Katharina Lechner从来没敢开口，自己只是替Katharina Lechner把话说了；Katharina Lechner把票根揉成一团，塞进Berta Lechner的西装口袋，转身时撞翻了侍者的托盘。

- **1932.01** `vienna-adolescence.workshop-apprentice/apprenticeship-rejected` tags: adolescence, school, work, family, social
  “太小，手也薄。”Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说完，把Katharina Lechner推过的木板翻过来，指着上面一道啃痕。Alois Lechner把布包往肩上提了提，说打扰了。Katharina Lechner在门口多站了一会儿，直到里面的刨子声把话头盖过去，才跟着Alois Lechner下楼。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; reputation -0.05000000000000002; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

  _effects: state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.pressure 42 -> 48; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.loveOfIt 73 -> 67; state_parameter lc17-child-prodigy.promise 54 -> 52; mood -0.03999999999999998_

  Katharina Lechner只把Friedrich Winter的名字写在一张草稿纸背面，第二天那张纸就在教室后排传了一圈。有人把名字念出来，有人问Katharina Lechner是不是要请人喝咖啡；Katharina Lechner说那是表姐的名字，声音大得连自己都吓了一跳。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.talk 30 -> 42; state_parameter yf18-first-awakening.nerve 20 -> 14; reputation -0.02999999999999997; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1932.01** `p9-childhood-youth-aspiration.prepare-exam/oil-ran-out-slept-in-exam` tags: adolescence, education, aspiration, school, books, club, exam, task
  煤油烧到一半时灯芯冒起黑烟，Katharina Lechner只好借面包房后窗透出的光背公式。后半夜他靠在墙上睡着了，醒来已经七点。考场上脑子发沉，算术卷子只答了一半，Luise Moser收卷时敲了敲桌面。
  _effects: health -0.06000000000000005 (熬夜且睡眠不足); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +failed_entrance_exam; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1932.10** `yf18-politics.latin-pamphlet::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/burn-it-in-the-courtyard` tags: politics, school, pamphlet, censorship, awakening, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Lechner在学校的煤炉边把油印纸烧了，灰烬和煤渣混在一起。Luise Kral第二天问第三十四页读得怎么样，Katharina Lechner说译文看不出来；Luise Kral点点头，把课本收回去，从此没有再借给Katharina Lechner任何书。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-luise-kral; flag +yf18_burned_pamphlet; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-38_

- **1932.12** `yf18-performance.choir-solo::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/laugh-and-bow` tags: performance, choir, humiliation, school, voice, reform, medical, schooling
  Katharina Lechner听见自己的声音劈开，索性停下来，对着教堂的穹顶笑了一声。Luise Moser在风琴后面瞪眼，Katharina Lechner却把那句拉丁文降了八度重新唱了一遍，声音厚得像另一个人；台下有人先笑，随后跟着鼓掌，Elisabeth Lechner在最后一排把手套举起来挥了挥。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; flag +yf18_laughed_at_solo; mood 0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Schriftsetzerlehrling @ org-winter-print; cash 6 (apprentice advance)_

  Katharina Lechner已经走到检票口，听见身后有人喊自己的小名。回头看见Alois Lechner提着一只保温壶，站在卖报纸的摊子旁边。Katharina Lechner把票递过去又收回来，最后陪她坐在长椅上喝完那壶汤；火车开走时，两个人都没有看站台。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.resolve 38 -> 18; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 30 -> 40; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `bc18-ghost.death-certificate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/correct-it-twice` tags: bureaucracy, legal, death, identity, mistake, paperwork, absurd, vienna
  Katharina Lechner在窗口要求更正，Anna Bauer把表格翻了三遍，最后开出一张新的出生证明，名字却少了一个字母。于是城里同时住着一个死人、一个活人，和一个只在档案里出生的陌生人。Katharina Lechner拿着两张纸站在区公所门口，忽然不知道该用哪一张去领下个月的煤票。
  _effects: cash -1 (更正材料的手续费和车钱); state_add bc18-bureaucratic-ghost state:world-vienna-1912:347; relation rival with person-anna-bauer; condition; flag +two_living_files_one_death; mood -0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-44; flag +bureaucratic_ghost; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `state_reaction:yf18-runaway-plan/a-family-question` tags: yf18-runaway-plan, escape, youth, plan, home, travel, risk, state
  饭桌上又为房租吵起来。Katharina Lechner把面包掰成很小的块，听见父亲说“这个家养不起闲人”；Katharina Lechner没有回嘴，只在心里把格拉茨的地址又背了一遍。

  Katharina Lechner把包袱重新塞回床底，又在上面压了一双冬鞋。母亲进来换床单时，Katharina Lechner站在门口，假装在看墙上的圣像；床单抖开的声音像一列很慢的火车，Katharina Lechner数着它经过的每一节车厢。
  _effects: state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.preparation 37 -> 41; state_parameter yf18-runaway-plan.suspicion 48 -> 54; mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1935.01** `state_tick:ld18-public-desire/the-story-changes-hands/strangers-know-best` tags: ld18-public-desire, desire, public, scandal, reputation, body, rumor, notoriety
  一个陌生女人在电车上叫住Katharina Lechner，说自己认识她的“那位”，还劝她趁年轻找个正经人家。Katharina Lechner没有回答，提前一站下了车；走在回家的路上，她发现自己的步子越来越快，像在躲一个并不存在的人。
  _effects: state_parameter ld18-public-desire.rumorHeat 45 -> 53; state_parameter ld18-public-desire.shame 45 -> 49; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.020000000000000018_

  她被拒绝入境，理由是“没有固定住所”。她在边境站给Rosa打电话，电话转到邻居家；对方说“先回来再说”，她买了一张反向车票。她在边境站的长椅上坐到天亮，才买下反向车票。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -5 (反向车票和电话费); flag +border_refused; relation acquaintance with person-friedrich-hofer; condition; health -0.020000000000000018 (在边境站过夜); knowledge “没有固定住址会被拒绝入境，亲戚的电话不能作为住址证明”_

- **1935.08** `r2-law.legal-aid::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/law-student-takes-case` tags: law, legal_aid, poverty, court, bureaucracy, vienna, urban, crisis
  Helene Moser是法律援助站的值班学生，还没毕业，案卷抱在怀里像一摞砖。Katharina Lechner问能不能赢，Helene Moser说先别想赢，先想清楚欠条上有没有利息。两个人趴在桌上算了一下午，Josef Winter送来一壶凉茶，说这案子有希望。
  _effects: flag +represented_by_law_student; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49; knowledge “the loan note does not mention interest and may be unenforce”_

  Katharina Lechner把房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱在纸上算了一遍，推到Hermann Stein 2面前。Hermann Stein 2看完没有点头，只把纸折起来收进袖口，说再想想。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1936.01** `bc18-ghost.double-tax::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/pay-both` tags: bureaucracy, tax, identity, paperwork, legal, absurd, money, vienna
  税吏先来收活人的税，三天后，另一个窗口又来收死人的遗产税。Katharina Lechner把两张税单并排放在桌上，发现名字、门牌和年份都分毫不差，只有税率不同。为了避免再被请去问话，Katharina Lechner把两份都交了；月底，家里的煤只够烧到复活节。
  _effects: cash -4 (活人和死人两份税单); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 15 -> 20; mood -0.030000000000000027; condition; flag +paid_tax_twice_

- **1936.01** `state_tick:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/paper-trail-year/another-window-opens` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  一个税务员把Katharina Lechner的死亡记录和一张新房租收据订在一起，寄到了另一个区。三个星期后，Katharina Lechner收到一封要求“已故纳税人”补缴暖气税的信；去问时，窗口说档案正在路上，让Katharina Lechner先等通知。通知没有来，房租却涨了。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 40 -> 47; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 32 -> 42; cash -1 (跑窗口的车钱); flag +heating_tax_for_the_dead_

- **1936.02** `oj18-zoo.night::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/broken-tooth` tags: zoo, animals, night, keeper, work, odd_job, suspense, vienna
  夜里十一点，母狮不肯碰桶里的马肉。Katharina Lechner举着煤油灯绕到兽栏后面，看见它右槽牙断了一截，牙龈肿得发亮。Katharina Lechner把肉切碎，又去敲Luise Weiss 3的门；第二天兽医来了，Ferdinand Huber在晨会上第一次记住Katharina Lechner的名字。
  _effects: organization_found 普拉特动物园夜班队 (other); position 夜班饲养员 @zoo:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000520:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:006561; state_add oj18-odd-apprenticeship state:world-vienna-1912:382; skill; reputation 0.030000000000000027; flag +zoo_night_shift; knowledge “母狮的右槽牙断了，牙龈肿得发亮，夜里不肯吃马肉。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-86; role; flag +odd_job_apprentice_

- **1936.02** `state_reaction:bc18-bureaucratic-ghost/another-file-opened` tags: bc18-bureaucratic-ghost, bureaucracy, identity, paperwork, death, mistake, legal, absurd
  Katharina Lechner刚在一个窗口证明自己活着，另一个窗口又从抽屉里抽出一张死亡通知。两个办事员隔着走廊互相看了一眼，谁也没有先开口；Katharina Lechner站在中间，忽然明白自己不是一个人，而是一份正在被转抄的卷宗。
  _effects: state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 20 -> 32; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.duplicates 2 -> 3; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.sightings 2 -> 3; flag +another_file_opened; mood -0.009999999999999787_

  _effects: skill; flag +strike_committee_member; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1937.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Hermann Stein 2在几天后找到Katharina Lechner，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; household; role; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006559_

- **1937.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Katharina Lechner在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Katharina Lechner的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

- **1937.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Katharina Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Emil 2。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Hermann Stein 2把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Katharina Lechner躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.07999999999999996 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:417_

- **1937.01** `state_reaction:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/the-thing-in-the-basement` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  夜班结束前，Katharina Lechner听见地下室传来拖拽声。下去时，门开着，地上只有一道湿拖把的痕迹；第二天，所有人都说那间屋子锁了十年。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 20 -> 28; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 30 -> 27; knowledge “地下室那扇门后面有东西被搬动过，但巡查簿上没有记录。”_

  普查员坚持要看到活人，Katharina Lechner在门口站到半夜。最后是隔壁的狗先叫了起来，Ferdinand Weiss披着外套出来作证，说这个人每晚都回来喂猫。普查员在表格上写：“一名在世者，由狗确认。”Ferdinand Weiss签了字，Katharina Lechner请他喝了一杯热酒，两个人都觉得这事不该再提。
  _effects: cash -1 (请邻居喝的一杯热酒); state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.risk 42 -> 37; state_parameter bc18-bureaucratic-ghost.fileDepth 47 -> 51; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; condition; flag +verified_by_a_dog; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  一年过去，Katharina Lechner已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Katharina Lechner也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。

  Katharina Lechner把钥匙推回去，说要去听课。Alois Lechner当场停掉每月二十克朗的零用钱，母亲没敢再劝。他第二周就找了两家补习学生，每周四晚给人补拉丁文。
  _effects: cash -15 (失去零用后自付开销); relation kin with person-alois-lechner; flag +self_supporting_student; skill_

  夜里有人从铁丝网下递进来一块面包，是Luise Weiss 2的妹妹。Ferdinand Huber的手电扫过来时，Katharina Lechner把面包塞进机器底座，自己蹲下去系鞋带。手电过去后，面包上全是机油；Luise Weiss 2还是掰了一半给Katharina Lechner。
  _effects: food; flag +smuggled_bread_into_labor_factory; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-35; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-78_

  Katharina Lechner的定亲对象家托人送来一只小木盒，里面是定亲时交换的银勺和一张退婚的字条，理由写着“门风”。Katharina Lechner把银勺擦亮，放回盒里，没有去敲门。那晚院子里的水管滴了一整夜。退婚的人家后来搬去了另一条街；Katharina Lechner再经过那扇门，只看见新刷的绿漆。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; reputation -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +engagement_broken_by_rumor_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:005870_

- **1939.01** `state_reaction:public-scandal/gossip` tags: public-scandal, scandal, reputation, social, state, state_reaction, public_humiliation, rumor
  Katharina Lechner走进咖啡馆时，邻桌的声音低了一拍。没有人当面说什么，但每个人都知道该说什么。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007921_

- **1939.01** `vienna-life-anschluss-1938/complies` tags: politics, persecution, 1938
  Friedrich Hofer在街口设了登记桌，要求住户交出姓名、职业和宗教。Katharina Lechner排队填了表，回家把门牌擦干净。楼里有人连夜搬走，也有人站在阳台上挥手。
  _effects: role; cash -3 (new permits and stamps); condition_

- **1939.01** `ds17-blackmail.debt::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-factory/the-first-installment` tags: love, scandal, blackmail, crime, money, secret_intimacy, kept_secret, vienna
  Hermann Stein 2在咖啡馆后门等Katharina Lechner，把一只没有封口的信封推过来：里面是旅馆登记页的抄本和一张写着数目的纸。Katharina Lechner数了三遍钱，还是差一点；Elisabeth Lechner说可以卖掉自己的胸针，Katharina Lechner没有答应，只把针线盒最底下的钥匙换了个地方。
  _effects: cash -6 (the first blackmail payment); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; state_transition public-scandal -> ds17-kept-secret; flag +paid_first_installment; flag +kept_secret_active_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:oj18-odd-apprenticeship/strange-year/trusted-with-the-keys` tags: oj18-odd-apprenticeship, work, odd_job, apprenticeship, night, trust, secrecy, state
  一年过去，Katharina Lechner已经能闭着眼说出哪块地板会响、哪盏煤气灯该在几点关。师傅把备用钥匙交下来，没有解释为什么第三把钥匙磨得最亮；Katharina Lechner也没有问，只在巡查簿最后一页画了一个小小的记号。
  _effects: state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.trust 37 -> 42; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.strangeKnowledge 38 -> 43; state_parameter oj18-odd-apprenticeship.nightsWorked 24 -> 36; cash 1 (night-shift wages); skill_

  Emil Weiss 2说军需订单压着，星期天全车间都来。Katharina Lechner在机器前站了十二个钟头，午饭是配给汤和一块掺了锯末的面包。收工时，Emil Weiss 2多发了一张代价券，说下个月凭券买煤。
  _effects: cash 6 (黑板上的空格); food; health -0.06000000000000005 (黑板上的空格); condition; flag +work13_worked_wartime_sunday_

- **1939.02** `df18-luck.borrowed-at-the-card-table::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/the-favor` tags: luck, debt, bargain, gambling, obligation, night, supernatural, vienna
  牌桌上输掉最后一张钞票后，Emil Weiss 2把一叠钱推到Katharina Lechner面前，说不用写借条，只要Katharina Lechner答应“以后有一天帮一个忙”。Katharina Lechner数了数，正好二十克朗。Emil Weiss 2没有说是什么忙，只把烟按灭在桌角，说时候到了会来找Katharina Lechner。
  _effects: cash 20 (牌桌上借来的二十克朗); state_add df18-borrowed-luck state:world-vienna-1912:494; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; flag +df18_took_card_money; mood 0.020000000000000018; flag +df18_luck_borrowed; knowledge “我得到了一点不该白拿的好运，代价还没有说清。”_

- **1939.08** `r15-work.factory-hiring::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-printshop/hired-on-probation` tags: work, factory, employment, industrial, wage, vienna, urban, crisis
  工厂只肯先签六周。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说干得好再续，干不好随时走。Katharina Lechner点头，把这句话记在心里，也记住了门口那块写着“本厂不负责任何工伤”的牌子。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; employment factory worker @ org-winter-print; state_add factory-employment state:world-vienna-1912:495_

- **1940.01** `major-family.proposal-refused/proposal-refused.wait-for-the-brother` tags: family, courtship, refusal, major, lifecycle
  Otto Kral把话说得很慢：母亲还在，弟弟的学徒期还有三年。Katharina Lechner点头，把准备好的结婚证词折起来放回口袋，那是借教堂的旧纸写的。两人在电车站分开，约好明年复活节再谈。Otto Kral上车前替母亲拢了拢披肩，没有回头看。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; flag +engagement_postponed; cash -1 (tram fare to the meeting); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

  _effects: interaction_

  夜班前，Katharina Lechner听见飞轮里有细碎的响声，叫Hermann Stein 2停了机器。拆开护罩，里面卡着一截断螺栓；Hermann Stein 2没有说话，第二天让机修工装了一道新护板，又在工头会上提了Katharina Lechner的名字。

  洗礼定在星期日下午，Katharina Lechner抱着孩子站在教堂后排，听见神父念出教父的名字。Katharina Lechner忽然想起另一张没有递交的登记纸，手指在襁褓边缘收紧；孩子哭了，Katharina Lechner跟着低头，假装是在哄。

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/stillbirth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, injury
  孩子生下来没有哭声。接生的人把布盖上去，Alois Winter在楼梯间坐了很久；Katharina Lechner在床上躺了三天，家里没有办葬礼，只去市政办事处登记，付了六克朗。登记表上母亲一栏写着Katharina Lechner的名字；此后邻居提起Katharina Lechner，只说“那个没了孩子的母亲”，柜子里还留着没拆的襁褓。
  _effects: health -0.12 (stillbirth); cash -6 (midwife and burial); role_

  Katharina Lechner把Grete Kraus的名字写在“联络员”后面，月薪一克朗，职责是每周来两次、把外面的消息带进来。Gustav Berg说这个岗位没有编制，Katharina Lechner说先按临时用工算；第一个月，Grete Kraus带来的消息比文件还多。
  _effects: position 联络员 @zoo:person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000520:child-evt:world-vienna-1912:006561; relation colleague with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000290:child; flag +appointed_ally; knowledge “联络员不在正式编制里，工资走临时用工。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Katharina Lechner胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018_

  _effects: cash -3 (先付一张床位的月租); residence; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  Katharina Lechner在Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的账房里向Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne开口预支十二克朗，因为家里的煤和面包钱同时到期。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne从抽屉里数出钞票，让会计在发薪日扣回，并记下“下不为例”。
  _effects: cash 12 (预支工资，先拿到现金); reputation 0.050000000000000044_

- **1947.12** `ml18-investment.transylvanian-mine::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sell-the-shares-to-a-baron` tags: investment, mining, fraud, money, risk, business, vienna, urban
  矿山还没出金，Katharina Lechner就把股份转给了一个想给女儿做嫁妆的男爵，赚了二十五克朗。半年后，假金矿的消息见报，男爵在俱乐部里拦住Katharina Lechner，问知不知道岩芯的事。Katharina Lechner说不知道，手却一直放在酒杯后面。
  _effects: cash 25 (profit from selling the mining shares early); state_add ml18-sudden-fortune state:world-vienna-1912:784; reputation -0.020000000000000018; flag +sold_mine_shares_early; knowledge “男爵买下股份时，矿山还没有出过一克金。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +sudden_fortune; reputation 0.020000000000000018; mood 0.030000000000000027_

- **1947.12** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Helene Berg 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Katharina Lechner把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Helene Berg 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-128_

  Katharina Lechner在保险公司写字间的小桌上抄完一页德文书信，又算出利息表上最后一行，速记只漏了两个词。Helene Stein把卷子翻过来看背面，说手是稳的。月底他领到七十克朗，扣掉十五克朗保证金，签字画押，坐进靠窗那把椅子，桌上有一块吸墨纸和一支借来的笔。

  _effects: reputation 0.050000000000000044; knowledge “市政厅旁的会议上，官员让记录员把三条请愿要求记进了正式记录”_

  Katharina Lechner傍晚从面粉厂下工回来，发现挂在腰带上的房门钥匙不见了，在楼梯间来回找了四趟。Rosa Weiss在二楼拐角的煤堆旁捡到那把铜钥匙，钥匙上缠着红线，一眼就认出来。Rosa Weiss敲开Katharina Lechner的门，把钥匙拍在桌上，说自己眼神好使。

  洗礼定在星期日下午，Katharina Lechner抱着孩子站在教堂后排，听见神父念出教父的名字。Katharina Lechner忽然想起另一张没有递交的登记纸，手指在襁褓边缘收紧；孩子哭了，Katharina Lechner跟着低头，假装是在哄。

  Mitzi Kraus把账本塞进钢琴后面的夹层，说等风声过去再拿出来。Berta Kraus第二次来时带了一个记录员和一张搜查令；钢琴被挪开时，琴键响了一串不成调的音。Katharina Lechner看见黑皮本翻开的那一页上，已经用红笔圈出了三笔利息。

- **1953.01** `vienna-women-housework.piecework-bundle/piecework.hide-defects` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  篮底那两件领子卷得很紧，Katharina Lechner用围裙角把篮沿擦了擦。Josef Bauer翻了翻面上几件，拿尺子比了比，没再往下。Franz Moser在走廊里问交了多少，Katharina Lechner含混说了句够买面包。
  _effects: cash 0.7 (交活所得，含未返工的两件); reputation -0.020000000000000018_

- **1953.01** `state_tick:ds17-kept-secret/one-more-year/confession-at-the-wrong-table` tags: ds17-kept-secret, love, secret, shame, family, risk, kept_secret, body

  Katharina Lechner和Katharina Weiss在阁楼里支起一块旧门板当桌子，每周三晚上互相考问。Katharina Weiss把容易错的公式写在烟盒背面，Katharina Lechner负责讲解历史背景。考试结果出来，两个人都过了，Katharina Weiss把烟盒送给Katharina Lechner作纪念，说以后别再弄丢。烟盒被Katharina Lechner放在书架上，一直没拆。

- **1954.11** `statistical_infection`
  Katharina Lechner在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1954.12** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.quit` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Katharina Lechner说互助会先退了，这笔钱要留给煤和面包。Franz Winter 2没争辩，在蓝皮本上把名字划了一道，问清楚是不是全家都退，然后把划掉的那行折进本子夹缝。她说以后想再进，要重新排三个月。门关上，Katharina Lechner听见铅笔在楼梯上轻轻敲了两下栏杆。
  _effects: cash 0.4 (省下本周会费留作煤钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1954.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Katharina Lechner的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。
